By combining controlled geometric interventions, verifiable physical references, and multidimensional evaluation, GeoFlip assesses progress from visual realism toward physical consistency conditioned on geometry.

</details>


### [70] [Any Forcing: Any-Step Generality and One-Step Stability for Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42031)
*Jiahua Xu,Yijie Fang,Nannan Wang,Xunhao Lin,Li Cao,Lei Hao,Mingrui Zhu,Fei Gao,Xinbo Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Any Forcing 统一框架，实现任意步数灵活采样与单步稳定生成的自回归视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频生成受限于固定采样步数，无法灵活权衡质量与效率；单步生成视觉质量下降且在长序列上误差累积，导致内容漂移与生成崩溃。

Method: 1) 统一对抗判别器，同时进行真实分布对齐、步间一致性对齐与首帧质量增强，兼顾灵活采样与单步质量；2) 免训练的自适应长期记忆选择机制，基于全局语义相关性与局部价值贡献筛选历史状态，并重映射其时间位置以保持记忆缓存内的时间关系。

Result: 实现高质量且稳定的单步自回归视频生成，在单张 H100 GPU 上短视频与长视频生成分别达到 20.9 FPS 与 24.7 FPS。

Conclusion: Any Forcing 通过对抗对齐与自适应记忆选择，统一了任意步数通用性与单步稳定性，兼顾生成质量与推理效率。

Abstract: Autoregressive video generation enables efficient inference through streaming prediction. However, existing methods are typically restricted to fixed sampling steps, limiting the flexibility to balance generation quality and efficiency, while one-step generation suffers from degraded visual quality and error accumulation over long sequences, leading to content drift and generation collapse. To address these issues, we propose Any Forcing, a unified framework for achieving Any-Step Generality and One-Step Stability. Specifically, we introduce a unified adversarial discriminator that jointly performs real-distribution alignment, inter-step consistency alignment, and first-frame quality enhancement, enabling flexible sampling while preserving one-step generation quality. Furthermore, we propose a training-free adaptive long-term memory selection mechanism that selects historical states based on global semantic relevance and local value contribution, while remapping their temporal positions to preserve temporal relationships within the memory cache, thereby enabling stable one-step long-video generation. Experiments demonstrate that Any Forcing achieves high-quality and stable one-step autoregressive video generation, achieving 20.9 FPS and 24.7 FPS for short and long video generation on a single H100 GPU, respectively.

</details>


### [71] [Learning Clean Generative Processes from Noisy Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42036)
*Fabian Kruse,Lorenzo Baldassari,Ivan Dokmanić*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 仅用独立含噪观测学习干净图像的生成模型：Noise2Noise回归只能学到不低于训练噪声水平的后验预测，作者施加"一致性"约束（预测应等于继续采样后的期望），推导其唯一确定向干净分布的延拓条件，并在Poisson、二项、Gamma、负二项测量上验证；CIFAR-10、光子计数显微与SAR实验表明一致性关系可修复数据噪声水平以下的延拓。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型及其非高斯噪声推广需要在每个噪声水平上获得后验预测，但实际只能获取含噪图像、缺少干净图像监督。现有Noise2Noise式方法在训练数据噪声水平以下无法给出可靠预测，而生成干净图像恰恰需要该区间的预测，因此需要一种无干净数据的机制来补全低噪声区的后验预测。

Method: 1) 用Noise2Noise回归从独立含噪视图学习后验预测，并证明其仅在高出训练噪声水平时可辨识；2) 引入一致性约束：某噪声水平上的预测应等于沿采样过程继续推进后所得预测的期望，从而把高噪声区已学得的预测向下延拓；3) 推导一致性唯一确定延拓至干净图像分布的条件，并对Poisson、二项、Gamma、负二项测量噪声逐一验证这些条件成立。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、光子计数显微成像与合成孔径雷达数据上，所需后验预测可完全由含噪视图学到；一致性关系能有效修复训练噪声水平以下的延拓，而去掉一致性时该区间性能严重退化。

Conclusion: 一致性约束为无干净数据条件下的非高斯噪声生成建模提供了理论与可行方案：高噪声区靠Noise2Noise、低噪声区靠一致性延拓，二者结合即可逼近干净图像分布，且对多种常见测量噪声具有保证。

Abstract: We study how to learn a generative model of clean images using only noisy observations. Diffusion processes and their generalizations to non-Gaussian noise require posterior predictions at every noise level. We first show that it is possible to learn these predictions from independent noisy views using Noise2Noise regression, but only at or above the noise level of the training data. To learn the predictions below that level, needed to generate clean images, we impose consistency: a prediction should equal its expectation after continuing the sampling process. We derive conditions under which consistency uniquely determines the continuation to the clean image distribution and verify them for Poisson, binomial, Gamma, and negative-binomial measurements. Experiments on CIFAR-10, photon-counting microscopy, and synthetic aperture radar show that the required posterior predictions can be learned from noisy views alone and that the consistency relation repairs the continuation beyond the noise level of the data, which severely deteriorates without consistency.

</details>


### [72] [Think in 360°: Benchmarking and Bootstrapping Panoramic Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42082)
*Lidong Lu,Lidong Zou,Xinyi Mao,Jiaxin Lv,Zhenquan Li,Liangrui Dong,Tong Lu,Guo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个全人工标注的全景视频理解基准 PanoVideoBench（1000题，5类15型），并构建基于真实全景视频的推理数据集 PanoVideoCoT（13465条QA-CoT），通过全景CoT监督微调与证据特权在线策略自蒸馏训练 PanoVideoReasoner，在基准上达55.7%，较骨干提升12.5分、超越此前SOTA 2.6分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全景视频涵盖相机四周所有方向，相关证据散布于不同方向与时刻，模型需跨视球与时间关联观测；但现有基准与训练资源不足以诊断和强化这类跨视角、球-时证据整合能力。

Method: 1) 构建 PanoVideoBench：1000道人工标注题，覆盖5大类15种题型，含目标搜索与全景能力的诊断性标注，评测11个闭源/开源/全景专用模型；2) 以球-时接地合成（sphere-time-grounded synthesis）将已验证的全景观测与成功的智能体探索转化为13465条QA-CoT对，构建 PanoVideoCoT；3) 以通用视频MLLM为骨干，经全景CoT监督微调 + 证据特权在线策略自蒸馏（Evidence-Privileged OPSD，教师用已验证透视视图引导学生自生成轨迹）训练 PanoVideoReasoner。

Result: 评测显示跨视角跟踪与球-时证据整合为11个模型最弱能力；PanoVideoReasoner 在 PanoVideoBench 上取得55.7%，较骨干提升12.5点，较此前SOTA提升2.6点，增益集中于空间、跨视角与时间类能力。

Conclusion: 接地的球-时监督可赋予通用视频MLLM全景推理能力，使能力提升与基准所暴露的薄弱维度精准对应。

Abstract: A panoramic video captures all directions around the camera, but relevant evidence can appear in different directions and at different times. Understanding it requires models to connect observations across the viewing sphere and time. We introduce PanoVideoBench, the first manually annotated benchmark for comprehensive panoramic video understanding. Its 1,000 questions span five categories and 15 question types, with diagnostic annotations for target search and panoramic capabilities. Evaluation of 11 proprietary, open-source, and panoramic-specialized models identifies cross-view tracking and sphere-time evidence integration as their weakest capabilities. To strengthen these capabilities, we construct PanoVideoCoT, the first reasoning dataset grounded in real panoramic video, through sphere-time-grounded synthesis that converts verified panoramic observations and successful agentic explorations into 13,465 QA-CoT pairs. Starting from a general video MLLM, PanoVideoReasoner learns from this corpus through Panoramic CoT SFT and Evidence-Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation, a panoramic instantiation of Vision-OPD's OPSD in which the teacher uses verified perspective views to guide student-generated trajectories. It reaches 55.7% on PanoVideoBench, improving the backbone by 12.5 points and the previous SOTA by 2.6. The gains concentrate on the same spatial, cross-view, and temporal capabilities exposed by the benchmark, showing that grounded sphere-time supervision can equip a general video MLLM with panoramic reasoning.

</details>


### [73] [Latent Reasoning in Continuous Space for Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42149)
*Byungwoo Jeon,Yoonwoo Jeong,Hyunseok Lee,Minsu Cho,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出在连续隐空间中进行推理的 LARC 框架，通过课程式 SFT 与信息增益 RL 两阶段训练，将 token 级推理迁移为隐状态推理，显著提升统一多模态模型的图像生成质量与测试时扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理增强型统一多模态模型（UMM）难以像大语言模型那样通过链式思维推理获得测试时扩展与细粒度可控性。作者将其归因于模态不匹配：推理发生在离散 token 空间，而图像生成发生在连续空间，token 级瓶颈限制了推理信息的表达与利用。

Method: 提出隐空间推理框架 LARC，直接在连续隐状态上推理以绕开 token 级瓶颈。训练分两阶段：(i) 课程式监督微调（curriculum SFT），逐步将 token 级推理转化为隐式推理；(ii) 信息增益强化学习（information-gain RL），在无真值监督下使隐式推理自我进化。

Result: 在多个文本到图像生成基准上，LARC 稳定提升生成质量、提示词跟随能力与组合保真度；在 GenEval 上达到 SOTA，较 BAGEL 提升约 7 个百分点；并展现出明显的测试时扩展行为，在并行与串行两类测试时扩展策略下均优于基于 token 的生成方式。

Conclusion: 在连续隐空间中进行推理可有效对齐推理与图像生成所处的空间，缓解 token 级瓶颈，使统一多模态模型获得可扩展、可控的推理增强生成能力，为推理驱动的图像生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) achieve remarkable success on diverse multimodal understanding and generation tasks, yet they still struggle to effectively leverage language-based chain-of-thought reasoning for image generation. As a result, existing reasoning-augmented UMMs often fail to inherit the test-time scaling and fine-grained controllability observed in large language models. We attribute this failure to a fundamental modality mismatch: reasoning is performed in a discrete token space, whereas image generation operates in a continuous space. To bridge this gap, we introduce a Latent Reasoning framework in Continuous space (LARC), which alleviates the token-level bottleneck by reasoning over continuous hidden states. Specifically, the training framework consists of two stages: (i) curriculum supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and (ii) information-gain reinforcement learning (RL). First, the curriculum SFT stage gradually converts token-level reasoning into latent reasoning. Subsequently, the information-gain RL stage self-evolves latent reasoning without ground truth supervision. Experiments across diverse text-to-image generation benchmarks show that LARC consistently improves generation quality, prompt following, and compositional fidelity. Notably, LARC achieves state-of-the-art performance on GenEval with a 7%p gain over BAGEL and demonstrates clear test-time scaling behavior, outperforming token-based generation under both parallel and sequential test-time scaling strategies.

</details>


### [74] [Rethinking Asymmetry in Self-Supervised 3D Geometry Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42092)
*Gyeongjin Kang,Freeman Cheng,Botao Ye,Weijie Lyu,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Eunbyung Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作以几何精度而非渲染质量为目标，将自监督的不对称性从输入空间迁移到3D表征空间：共享骨干利用全部视角建立对应，仅在重建场景中排除留出视角；并利用视角密度对齐稀疏/稠密上下文特征、并与冻结视觉基础模型特征对齐，作为无需几何标注的额外训练信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从无位姿图像学习3D几何的方法依赖“观测—解释”间的不对称性，但以往通过分离分支或掩码注意力在输入空间施加该约束，会限制视角间信息交互，目标也偏向渲染质量。作者认为当留出视角位于上下文视角之间时，上下文已足以约束其几何，因此应以几何精度为主要目标重新设计不对称性的施加位置。

Method: 1) 在3D表征空间施加不对称性：共享骨干网络使用所有视角（含留出视角）进行特征匹配与对应学习，仅将留出视角从重建场景中排除；2) 视角密度对齐：以稀疏上下文与稠密上下文描述同一场景，将稀疏上下文特征对齐到稠密上下文特征，作为渲染之外的几何训练信号；3) 特征对齐冻结的视觉基础模型，进一步获取额外增益；4) 所有信号均不需几何标注。

Result: 相比输入空间的不对称设计，所提方法在深度与位姿估计上均有提升；视角密度对齐与基础模型特征对齐带来额外增益；最终模型在几何估计上优于现有自监督方法；受控实验分别隔离并验证了各组件的贡献；自监督预训练还能改善后续的监督训练。

Conclusion: 将自监督不对称性施加于3D表征空间、并结合视角密度对齐与基础模型特征对齐，是一种无需几何标注、以几何精度为导向的有效自监督3D学习范式，且可作为有益预训练。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning relies on asymmetry between what a model observes and what it must explain. Recent methods learn 3D geometry from unposed images by predicting cameras and scene structure, then rendering held-out views. To avoid trivial solutions, they impose this asymmetry in the input space, restricting how views interact through separate branches or masked attention. We revisit this design with geometric accuracy, rather than rendering quality, as the main objective. When a held-out view lies between context views, the context already constrains the geometry it observes. We therefore impose the asymmetry in the 3D representation space, excluding held-out views only from the reconstructed scene while a shared backbone uses all views for correspondence. This improves depth and pose estimation over input-space designs. We further exploit view density: sparse and dense observations depict the same scene but provide different amounts of correspondence. Aligning sparse-context features with dense-context features supplies a geometric training signal alongside rendering, without a pretrained geometric teacher. We additionally align features with a frozen vision foundation model, which brings further gains. None of these signals requires geometric annotation, and the resulting model improves geometry estimation over prior self-supervised methods. Controlled experiments isolate the contribution of each component, and self-supervised pretraining also improves subsequent supervised training.

</details>


### [75] [A Better Critic Matters for Distribution Matching Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42135)
*Zongze Wu,Xianbang Wang,Zhengyang Geng,Mingdeng Cao,Zhoutong Zhang,Richard Zhang,Eli Shechtman,Ruiming Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究DMD蒸馏中critic所需的容量与信息：提出critic与teacher联合缩放（joint scaling）及步感知critic（STAC），二者结合在图像、文生图、文生视频任务上稳定提升少步蒸馏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMD依赖teacher score与在线critic对student分布score估计之差来训练少步student，但critic需要多大容量、需要何种信息才能提供可靠训练信号尚不明确；实践中增大teacher而固定小critic会导致质量下降甚至训练崩溃。

Method: 容量侧：在ImageNet上对七种SiT模型规模做受控实验，比较固定小critic与critic随teacher同步缩放（joint scaling）。信息侧：不再让critic建模与步数无关的student输出混合分布，而是提出STAC，以student的生成步为条件估计步特定score。

Result: 固定小critic时增大teacher会降低student质量并引发训练崩溃；joint scaling可避免崩溃并使学生受益于更大teacher。STAC带来稳定的额外增益。二者在类条件ImageNet生成、SDXL与FLUX.2文生图、Wan2.1文生视频上均取得一致性提升。

Conclusion: critic的容量需与teacher协同扩展，且应具备步感知信息，才能为DMD少步蒸馏提供可靠信号；joint scaling + STAC为可移植的通用改进。

Abstract: Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD) trains a few-step student using the difference between a teacher's score and an online critic's estimate of the student distribution's score. We investigate what the critic needs, in capacity and in information, to provide a reliable training signal. On the capacity side, controlled experiments across seven SiT model sizes on ImageNet show that increasing teacher size while keeping a small critic fixed can degrade student quality and cause training collapse. Scaling the critic alongside the teacher, a strategy we call joint scaling, prevents collapse in our experiments and allows the student to benefit from larger teachers. On the information side, rather than forcing the critic to model a step-agnostic mixture of student outputs, we introduce a step-aware critic (STAC) that conditions on the student's generation step to estimate step-specific scores. Together, joint scaling and STAC consistently improve few-step distillation across class-conditional ImageNet generation, text-to-image generation with SDXL and FLUX.2, and text-to-video generation with Wan2.1.

</details>


### [76] [ReLift: Dense 3D Tracking as Lifted Trajectory Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42136)
*Assaf Singer,Maroun Khriesh,Noam Rotstein,Yoni Kasten,Or Litany*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ReLift，将稠密3D跟踪重新表述为几何轨迹修复与补全，用单目2D跟踪器+3D几何模型"提升"得到轨迹，再由轨迹校正器修复误差与遮挡缺口，在WorldTrack上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目视频稠密3D跟踪需同时建立时序点对应、估计深度并推断遮挡期间的轨迹，联合学习目标在4D监督稀缺下过于苛刻。作者发现强预训练2D跟踪器与3D几何模型已能提供良好基础：把轨迹提升并仅对遮挡段插值，其性能已可媲美使用相同几何的专用3D跟踪器。但提升轨迹仍继承对应与深度误差，且遮挡时深度来自遮挡物而非被跟踪点。

Method: ReLift为高效前馈框架，核心是轨迹校正器：沿轨迹方向与跨轨迹方向交替施加注意力，以保留可靠观测、修复被污染的位置、补全缺失运动。训练仅使用几何输入，在带模拟提升误差的3D轨迹上直接监督，从而与图像、特定跟踪器和几何模型解耦，把4D监督集中到轨迹精化上。

Result: 在WorldTrack上取得最高平均精度：全点APD较最强基线提升2.1，动态点提升3.0；更快的配置与领先3D跟踪器性能相当，且速度快于4RC。该模型可跨不同跟踪器与几何提供者复用，无需重训练即可一致改善提升轨迹。

Conclusion: 将稠密3D跟踪解耦为"2D跟踪+几何提升+轨迹修复"是可扩展的有效范式，集中于轨迹级的4D监督可获得可复用模型，并可直接受益于未来2D跟踪器与几何模型的进步。

Abstract: Dense 3D tracking from monocular video requires establishing point correspondences over time, estimating their depth, and inferring their trajectories during occlusion. Existing methods typically learn these tasks jointly, a demanding objective given the scarcity of 4D supervision. We show that strong pretrained 2D trackers and 3D geometry models provide an effective foundation: lifted trajectories, with occluded segments simply interpolated, already rival dedicated 3D trackers using the same geometry. However, lifted trajectories inherit correspondence and depth errors, and under occlusion, their depth comes from the occluder rather than the tracked point. We introduce ReLift, an efficient feed-forward framework that recasts dense 3D tracking as geometric trajectory repair and completion. At its core, a trajectory corrector alternates attention along and across trajectories to preserve reliable observations, repair corrupted positions, and complete missing motion. Using only geometric inputs allows us to train directly on 3D trajectories with simulated lifting errors, decoupling training from images and from any specific tracker or geometry model. Concentrating 4D supervision on trajectory refinement yields a reusable model that consistently improves lifted trajectories across trackers and geometry providers without retraining, so it benefits directly from future advances in either component. On WorldTrack, ReLift achieves state-of-the-art mean accuracy, improving on the strongest baselines by 2.1 APD on all points and 3.0 on dynamic points, with faster configurations remaining competitive with leading 3D trackers while running faster than 4RC. Visit our supplementry page for more examples and code.

</details>

### [77] [Li$^{2}$SAO: Lifting Light Singl-Rollout Asynchronous RL Optimization via Group-Relative Actor-Critic Bootstrapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40726)
*Zichen Bai,Yanli Wang,Yang Liu,Junhao Shi,Xueqin Liu,Jinguo Sun,Muye Li,Shaoqiang Zheng,Xuefei Diao,Zhuwei Zeng,Nan Duan,Peng Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对轻量级单轨迹异步优化LiSAO中价值模型训练不足导致早期落后的问题，提出Li²SAO：critic未收敛时用组相对优势训练actor、组中心回报训练critic，之后再切换为单轨迹GAE，从而在早期追平/超越GRPO并抬高后期性能上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步RL已成为LLM后训练主流范式，但GRPO的组采样与异步训练不匹配（rollout长度长尾分布），SAO据此提出单轨迹异步优化，却依赖闭源、经价值预训练扩展得到的大价值模型。作者复现出无预训练价值模型的轻量版LiSAO，发现其在推理任务上不如GRPO、在智能体任务上更优，且因价值模型训练不足在早期训练显著落后，需弥补这一冷启动缺陷。

Method: 在单轮推理与多轮智能体任务上对比分析GRPO与LiSAO；提出Li²SAO，在critic冷启动阶段采用组相对优势训练actor、以组中心回报作为critic目标，待critic具备一定质量后切换为单轨迹GAE更新。

Result: GRPO在推理任务上优于LiSAO，LiSAO在智能体任务上反超；Li²SAO在训练早期即追平或超过GRPO，并进一步提升后期性能上限。

Conclusion: 异步单轨迹优化不必依赖昂贵的预训练价值模型，通过分阶段的actor-critic自举（组相对优势+组中心回报预热，再转单轨迹GAE）即可兼顾早期稳定性与最终性能上限。

Abstract: Asynchronous reinforcement learning (RL) is becoming a mainstream paradigm for post-training large language models (LLMs), as it improves training efficiency by updating the model as rollouts arrive. Recent work argues that group-wise sampling in GRPO does not naturally fit asynchronous RL training because rollout lengths follow a long-tailed distribution, and proposes Single-rollout Asynchronous Optimization (SAO) to improve stability and off-policy behavior, especially on agentic tasks. However, SAO relies on a closed-source value model obtained from scaling value pretraining. In this paper, we analyze GRPO and LiSAO, our lightweight reproduction of SAO without the pretrained value model, on single-turn reasoning and multi-turn agentic tasks. We find that GRPO outperforms LiSAO on reasoning tasks, while the reverse holds on agentic tasks. Since the value model of LiSAO is not yet well trained , LiSAO lags behind GRPO substantially in early training steps. To address this issue, we propose Li$^{2}$SAO, which Lifts Light Single-rollout Asynchronous Optimization with group-relative actor-critic bootstrapping: while the critic is cold, a group-relative advantage trains the actor and a group-centered return trains the critic, after which training switches to single-rollout GAE. Experiments show that Li$^{2}$SAO catches up with or surpasses GRPO in early training and further raises the later performance ceiling. Code is available at https://github.com/jd-cc-coder/Li2SAO.

</details>


### [78] [Mage-VL-Cowork: A Full Training Recipe to Learn Hybrid Computer Use From Evolved Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40747)
*Xiaoyi Zhang,Haoqing Wang,Xinjie Zhang,Xiang Ming,Xing Wang,Senqiao Yang,Kaichen Zhang,Zhaoyang Jia,Andong Tan,Xinjiang Wang,Jinghao Guo,Yifei Shen,Xiao Li,Peng Zhang,Xun Guo,Jiahao Li,Shicheng Zheng,Jinglu Wang,Zongyu Guo,Wenxuan Xie,Bin Li,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出4B混合式计算机使用智能体Mage-VL-Cowork，通过五阶段渐进式课程（midtraining/SFT/RL）统一视觉GUI交互与代码/终端执行，配套可复用技能条件化的数据合成流水线Mage-CUA-Forge，在OSWorld-Verified上达到66.0%，为同参数量级SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CUA需在单一策略中无缝融合视觉GUI操作与代码、终端执行，而传统逐任务构建智能体环境成本高、复用性差；同时直接蒸馏默认教师模型难以获得高质量轨迹与分阶段监督信号。

Method: 从通用多模态对齐checkpoint出发，构建端到端渐进式五阶段课程（跨midtraining、SFT、RL）；数据侧以Mage-CUA-Forge用技能条件化的可复用生成工作流与应用专用preparer替代逐任务agent构建，覆盖6,915项技能、合成293,806个桌面与Web任务；训练侧不直接蒸馏默认教师，而是迭代演化教师harness并实例化专用CUA harness以产出高质量轨迹及阶段特定监督。

Result: 在各训练阶段逐步提升，最终Mage-VL-Cowork-4B在OSWorld-Verified上达66.0%，推理成本更高效，在同参数规模下取得SOTA，并刷新端侧智能前沿。

Conclusion: 可复用的技能条件化数据合成显著降低逐任务开销（82.6%），配合教师harness演化与渐进式课程，可在4B小模型上实现GUI+代码混合的高性能端侧CUA。

Abstract: Effective computer-use agents (CUAs) should seamlessly combine visual GUI interaction with code and terminal execution within a single policy. We present Mage-VL-Cowork, a 4B hybrid CUA trained from a general multimodally aligned checkpoint through an end-to-end, progressive five-stage curriculum across midtraining, SFT, and RL. To power this curriculum at scale, Mage-CUA-Forge replaces per-task agent construction with reusable, skill-conditioned generation workflows and application-specific preparers, synthesizing 293,806 desktop and web tasks across 6,915 skills while reducing recurring per-task expenditure by 82.6%. Rather than directly distilling a default teacher, we evolve the teacher harness and instantiate specialized CUA harnesses to yield high-quality trajectories alongside stage-specific supervision. Mage-VL-Cowork-4B finally climbs to 66.0% on OSWorld-Verified through different training stages with more efficient reasoning cost, achieving state-of-the-art performance at its parameter size and pushing forward the frontier of on-device intelligence.

</details>


### [79] [The Generative Gap Between Speculative Drafter and Continuous Diffusion Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40847)
*Yujian Chen,Hao Jiang,Guang Cheng,Weijian Luo,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在无验证、从头训练与统一预算设定下比较LangFlow式CDLM与DSpark式草稿模型架构的独立生成能力，长序列(128/1024)上LangFlow在GenPPL与MAUVE上均优于DSpark两种变体，短序列(16)上DSpark-RNN领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CDLM（迭代精炼式独立生成）与推测解码草稿模型（提出文本供目标模型验证纠正）通常在不同评价体系下被评估，二者架构优劣缺乏可比证据；作者希望在剥离验证机制与目标模型特征后，公平比较两类架构的独立生成能力。

Method: 采用LangFlow作为CDLM代表，并将DSpark的Markov头部与recurrent-head架构改造为独立生成；全部模型从头训练，在OpenWebText上做无条件、单块生成，长度16/128/1024，统一预处理、骨干维度与训练更新预算，不使用目标模型特征与验证；用外部语言模型计算GenPPL，用MAUVE度量与参考文本的分布相似度；并做条件KL分析以刻画Markov头仅依赖前一token所排除的依赖关系。

Result: 默认去噪步数等于序列长度时，LangFlow在长度128与1024上两项指标均优于两种DSpark变体，长度16上DSpark-RNN领先；循环记忆（recurrent memory）解除前一token条件限制并提升两项指标，但长序列差距依然存在；教师概率匹配可降低Markov变体的GenPPL，但未见MAUVE提升；固定权重下增加LangFlow去噪步数可在长度16、128上同时改善两项指标，并改变长度128处相对DSpark-RNN的排序。

Conclusion: 在所评估的预算下，LangFlow具有长序列生成优势；全序列迭代精炼是超越循环头记忆所带来的增益、进一步改进独立生成的有效路径。

Abstract: Continuous diffusion language models (CDLMs) and speculative drafters offer efficient language generation but are typically evaluated differently: CDLMs generate independently through iterative refinement, whereas drafters propose text for target-model verification and correction. We compare their architectures without verification, using LangFlow for CDLMs and adapting DSpark's Markov and recurrent-head architectures for standalone generation. All models are trained from scratch for unconditional, single-block OpenWebText generation at lengths 16, 128, and 1024, without target features or verification, using common preprocessing, backbone dimensions, and training-update budgets. We evaluate generation perplexity (GenPPL) with an external language model and distributional similarity to reference text with MAUVE. With default denoising steps equal to sequence length, LangFlow outperforms both DSpark variants on both metrics at lengths 128 and 1024, while DSpark-RNN leads at 16. A conditional KL analysis characterizes dependencies excluded by the Markov head's previous-token conditioning. Recurrent memory removes this restriction and improves both metrics, but the longer-sequence gap persists. Teacher probability matching further lowers Markov GenPPL without observed MAUVE gains. Increasing LangFlow's denoising steps at fixed weights improves both metrics at lengths 16 and 128, changing the length-128 ordering relative to DSpark-RNN. These results establish LangFlow's longer-sequence advantage under the evaluated budgets and highlight iterative full-sequence refinement as a route to improving standalone generation beyond gains from recurrent head memory.

</details>


### [80] [Qistas: A Pre-Registered, Portable Protocol for Fairness Evaluation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40893)
*Nawar S. Alseelawi,Mustafa S. Aljumaily*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Qistas 协议：面向特定社会构成（以伊拉克为首个实例）的 LLM 公平性评测框架，强调预注册、配对设计与功效分析，并以 58 条种子题库的语义审查作为首次实地检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 公平性基准几乎都是英语生成、以美国社会类别为设定，翻译版本把这些不适用类别带入其他国家；且多数基准在运行模型前未固定假设，缺乏预注册与可检验的最小效应声明。

Method: 构建可跨国移植的评测协议，含：精简的 3×5 分类法（4 个格子按设计关闭）、分类型检查清单、累积裁定日志、结构审计与人工复核手册。测量四个轴：匹配反事实对上的程序对称性、差异性拒答、歧义下的刻板印象依赖、已记录事件的术语框架。全部测量按构造配对，采用配对统计（符号翻转置换检验、精确 McNemar 检验、模板聚类 bootstrap 区间、Holm 校正族），并做含模板聚类设计效应的先验功效分析。

Result: 58 条种子题库的语义审查显示，25 个模板组中有 24 组至少未通过一项检查；暴露的缺陷模式包括评判标准泄漏进提示、破坏阿拉伯语一致性标记的 token 级替换、认知上不平衡的反事实对、单一主题的拒答探测、未书写的方言对照被计为覆盖。

Conclusion: 本文为协议论文而非结果论文，未评测任何模型；作者认为上述缺陷是反事实公平性基准的普遍风险，随附预注册固定了假设、指标、排除规则与分析计划。

Abstract: Fairness benchmarks for large language models (LLMs) are overwhelmingly English-born and American in their social categories; translated versions carry those categories into societies where they do not apply, and almost none fix their hypotheses before the models are run. We present Qistas (Arabic qistas, "the just balance"), a protocol for evaluating how LLMs treat the social, confessional, and regional constituencies of a society, portable across countries and instantiated first for Iraq. In its pilot form it measures only what needs no verdict on contested truth: procedural symmetry across matched counterfactual pairs, differential refusal, stereotype reliance under ambiguity, and terminological framing of documented events. Every measurement is paired by construction and analysed with paired machinery (sign-flip permutation tests, exact McNemar tests, template-clustered bootstrap intervals, Holm-corrected families), with a power analysis that states, before any data exist, the smallest effect each axis can detect after the design effect of template clustering. We release the authoring apparatus (a pruned 3x5 taxonomy with four cells closed by design, per-type checklists, a cumulative rulings log, a structural audit, and a human review notebook) and report its first field test: a semantic review of a 58-item seed bank in which 24 of 25 template groups failed at least one check. The defect patterns it exposed (evaluation criteria leaking into prompts, token-level swaps that break Arabic agreement, epistemically unbalanced pairs, single-theme refusal probes, unwritten dialect twins counted as coverage) are, we argue, general hazards of counterfactual fairness benchmarks. This is a protocol paper, not a results paper: no model has been evaluated, and the accompanying pre-registration commits the hypotheses, metrics, exclusion rules, and analysis plan that the pilot will follow.

</details>


### [81] [CoFiBreak: A Coarse-to-Fine Diversity Reward for Effective Multi-Turn Jailbreak](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40926)
*Jialin Song,Xiaodong Liu,Weiwei Yang,Mingqian Feng,Chengquan Guo,Qiyao Ma,Carolyn Buractaon,Anson Ho,Wuyang Chen,Christopher White*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoFiBreak 提出粗到细多样性奖励的 GRPO 训练方法，在保持高攻击成功率与低推理成本的同时显著提升自动红队攻击策略多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动红队以攻击成功率为唯一奖励时，强化学习易使攻击者收敛到少量提示模式（攻击多样性崩塌），导致安全评测反复测试近似重复的攻击而偏离真实有害意图。现有方法通常在攻击成功率或推理成本与多样性之间做取舍。

Method: 为 GRPO 设计粗到细的多样性奖励：粗粒度奖励鼓励攻击者使用组内欠代表性的策略，拓宽策略覆盖；细粒度奖励在移除提示嵌入中的有害意图信息后，惩罚重复使用相同人格、框架与输出格式，拓宽策略实现方式。多样性奖励仅施加于已超过有效性阈值的攻击，且两项奖励仅在训练阶段计算，从而保持推理效率。

Result: 以 Llama3.1-8B-Instruct 为受害者模型训练，在 HarmBench 上于多个前沿受害者模型上发现 1.6–2.0 倍于最强基线的细粒度成功策略；在 AdvBench 上达到 98.8% ASR@10，且多样性得分为最强攻击基线的 2.1 倍；攻击迁移至未见的前沿模型 Claude-Opus-4.6 时达 71.0% ASR@10。

Conclusion: 粗到细多样性奖励可有效缓解 GRPO 训练中的攻击多样性崩塌，在保持攻击有效性与推理效率的前提下提升细粒度策略多样性，并具备良好的跨模型迁移性。

Abstract: Automated red teaming trains an attacker large language model (LLM) to elicit harmful responses from a victim LLM, but reinforcement learning can collapse attackers onto a narrow set of prompts when attack success is the only reward. Such a collapse of attack diversity can bias safety evaluation toward repeatedly testing near-duplicate attack patterns rather than the underlying harmful intents. Existing methods often trade off attack success or inference cost for greater content or strategy diversity. We propose CoFiBreak, a coarse-to-fine diversity reward for Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) that improves attack diversity while preserving strong attack effectiveness and low inference cost. The coarse reward broadens which strategies the attacker uses by encouraging strategies underrepresented within each GRPO group, while the fine reward broadens how those strategies are realized by discouraging reuse of the same personas, framings, and output formats after harmful-intent information is removed from prompt embeddings. Diversity rewards apply only to attacks that already exceed an effectiveness threshold, so diversity cannot be gained through ineffective attacks. Since both rewards are computed only during training, CoFiBreak also retains inference efficiency. Trained with Llama3.1-8B-Instruct as the victim, CoFiBreak discovers 1.6-2.0x as many distinct successful fine-grained strategies as the strongest baseline across frontier victims on HarmBench. It reaches 98.8% ASR@10 (attack success rate with 10 generations per intent) on AdvBench with the highest diversity score among all compared methods, which is 2.1x that of the strongest-attacking baseline. The resulting attacks transfer to unseen frontier LLMs, reaching 71.0% ASR@10 on AdvBench with the unseen victim Claude-Opus-4.6. The code will be released.

</details>


### [82] [Adaptive Personalization Under Preference Epistemic Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40930)
*Priyanka Dey,Rajarshi Das,Vikas Bahirwani,Qi Zeng,Julia Kruk,Qi Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"认知不确定性下的自适应个性化"任务，构建多语言基准（250 personas、5 语言、25K 轮对话），分离策略选择与响应生成；发现八款模型存在"识别—生成差距"，系统性地偏向"先利用"既有偏好；通过监督内部策略表征的训练可缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类偏好随时间演化：可能保持有效、因语境变化而变得不确定、或被明确修订。现有对话式 AI 依赖被动的记忆检索，无法随偏好不确定性调整响应策略，因此需要形式化并评测模型的"自适应个性化"能力。

Method: 1) 形式化"认知不确定性下的自适应个性化"问题；2) 开展人类偏好研究，验证人们偏好随不确定性变化的状态依赖（state-contingent）策略；3) 构建多语言基准：250 个 persona、5 种语言、25K 对话轮，将策略选择与响应执行解耦；4) 评测 8 个闭源/开源模型；5) 对比提示干预与以人类状态条件偏好为监督目标、针对模型内部策略表征的训练方法。

Result: 人类研究支持状态依赖策略偏好；八个模型均出现"识别—生成差距"：在候选项中挑选策略时表现可靠，但开放式生成时策略校准度下降 35–46%；该失败具系统性，模型默认"先利用"，无视偏好认知不确定性过度依赖既有偏好；提示干预无效，而监督内部策略表征的训练在分布外 persona 与会话风格上将用户可接受性泛化差距缩小约三分之一。

Conclusion: 偏好不确定性下的策略自适应是当前语言模型的系统性短板，且源于策略表征而非表层提示；直接监督内部策略表征、对齐人类状态条件偏好是可行且可泛化的改进路径。

Abstract: Human preferences change over time: an established preference may remain valid over time, become uncertain as context shifts, or be explicitly revised. Effective personalization requires conversational AI to adapt its response strategy to evolving preferences rather than relying on passive memory retrieval. We formalize this capability as adaptive personalization under epistemic uncertainty. A human preference study further confirms that people favor state-contingent response strategies as preference uncertainty changes. To evaluate whether language models mirror this adaptation, we create a multilingual benchmark spanning 250 personas, 5 languages, and 25K conversational turns that decouples strategy selection from response execution. Across eight closed- and open-weight models, we uncover a recognition-generation gap: models reliably identify appropriate strategies when selecting among candidate options, but collapse during open-ended generation, suffering a 35-46% drop in strategy calibration. Crucially, this failure is systematic: models default to exploit-first behavior, over-relying on existing preferences regardless of preference epistemic uncertainty. We show this failure is addressable: while prompting interventions fail, targeted training that supervises models' internal strategy representations toward state-conditional human preferences narrows the user acceptability generalization gap by a third on out-of-distribution personas and conversational styles.

</details>


### [83] [Better Retrievers, Worse Labels: How Incomplete Relevance Judgments Distort Abstention Evaluation in Conversational RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40966)
*Ivan Kochergin,Vasily Konovalov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出对话式RAG基准中用“是否存在金标文档”来判定上下文可回答性是错误捷径，并量化其危害：检索器越强，该捷径精度越低（.68→.10），建议改用基于内容标注的拒答评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MTRAG、RETECO等端到端基准要求系统基于自身检索结果作答，拒答（abstention）需按“检索到的上下文是否足以回答”评分。但存在一个便利捷径：直接用相关性判断（relevance judgments）中是否有金标文档来推断充分性，将无金标文档的上下文视为不可回答。作者要检验该捷径是否成立。

Method: 复现官方基线，并在RECOR（RETECO track 2）上检验该捷径：使用四种检索器（nDCG@10 从 .18 到 .59）与三个Qwen3阅读器。用两个开放LLM评判器加四个小型开放模型组成的评审团，按内容对每个上下文打标签；另由一个不同模型家族的模型对250个上下文做盲审并仲裁。MTRAG上（人工标注判断）亦做对照。

Result: 检索器越强，含答案却无金标文档的上下文占比越高（28%→72%），捷径精度越低（.68→.10；盲审下 .70→.21）。Qwen3-4B对这类“假漏判”上下文正确作答62–67%，而对被判不足的无金标上下文仅23–30%、对无文档上下文仅1%。假漏判多源于同一问题的难负例、其他轮次的金标或金标页面的相邻块。MTRAG上精度从 .72 降至 .50。该捷径使可回答性AUROC最多低估 .19，而最佳充分性检测器竟是一个从不读取文档的检索分数。

Conclusion: “无金标即不可回答”是系统性错误的捷径，且随检索质量提升而恶化。应基于内容标注而非金标存在性来评估拒答，并已公开代码、运行记录与标注。

Abstract: Conversational RAG benchmarks such as MTRAG and RETECO evaluate retrieval and generation end to end: each system answers from its own retrieval, so abstention must be scored against whether each retrieved context is sufficient. A convenient shortcut reads sufficiency off the relevance judgments and treats a context without gold documents as unanswerable. We re-run the official baselines exactly and test this shortcut on RECOR (RETECO track~2) with four retrievers (nDCG@10 from .18 to .59) and three Qwen3 readers. Two open LLM judges and a panel of four small open models label each context by content, and a model of another family audits 250 contexts blind, with adjudication. The stronger the retriever, the larger the share of no-gold contexts that still contain the answer (28\% to 72\%) and the lower the shortcut's precision (.68 to .10; .70 to .21 under the audit). A reader agrees: Qwen3-4B answers 62--67\% of these false misses correctly, against 23--30\% of no-gold contexts judged insufficient and 1\% with no documents. In most false misses, the answer comes from a hard negative mined for the same question, another turn's gold or a neighbouring chunk of the gold page. On MTRAG, whose judgments come from annotators, precision drops from .72 to .50. Under the shortcut, answerability AUROC is up to .19 too low, and the best sufficiency detector appears to be a retrieval score that never reads the documents. We release code, runs and labels, and recommend scoring refusals against content labels, not the presence of gold.

</details>


### [84] [Fewer Heads, Fewer Memories: Pruning Redundant Attention Heads Suppresses Verbatim Memorisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40974)
*Aryan Chowdhury,Riza Batista-Navarro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在推理前将大量任务冗余注意力头置零，可显著抑制GPT-2-XL的逐字记忆（记忆计数下降91.5%），同时不损害甚至改善语言建模困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM会逐字复现训练数据，带来隐私泄露、知识产权暴露与泛化边界等问题；理论上注意力头可充当联想记忆机制且记忆容量随头数增长，而实证发现许多注意力头冗余、可剪枝。两条线索并存，但"剪除冗余头对记忆复现的影响"尚属空白。

Method: 以GPT-2-XL(1.5B)为对象，先在用于识别冗余头的基准上确定任务冗余注意力头，然后在推理前将这些头权重置零（剪枝729个头），并在已知被记忆的序列上评估记忆计数、在WikiText-103上评估困惑度，无需重训练或改动训练数据。

Result: 剪除729个任务冗余注意力头后，记忆计数下降91.5%，同时WikiText-103困惑度改善0.92。

Conclusion: 注意力头冗余可被利用来在推理阶段缓解记忆复现，且无需重训练或修改训练数据，为隐私与IP风险提供了低成本干预手段。

Abstract: Large language models have been shown to memorise and reproduce verbatim sequences from their training data, raising concerns about privacy leakage, intellectual property exposure, and the limits of generalisation. Recent theoretical work suggests that attention heads may contribute to this behaviour by functioning as associative memory mechanisms, with memorisation capacity scaling with the number of heads. At the same time, empirical work has shown that many attention heads are redundant and can be pruned with limited impact on downstream performance. Despite these parallel findings, the effect of pruning redundant heads on memorised-sequence reproduction remains unexplored. In this work, we demonstrate that zeroing out a substantial fraction of attention heads prior to inference can significantly suppress verbatim memorisation while preserving, or even improving, language modelling performance on the benchmark used to identify redundant heads. Evaluating GPT-2-XL(1.5B) on known memorised sequences, we find that pruning 729 task-redundant heads reduces the memorisation count by 91.5%, while improving WikiText-103 perplexity by 0.92. These findings suggest that attention-head redundancy can be exploited to mitigate memorisation at inference time, without retraining or modifying the training data.

</details>


### [85] [Do Open Language Models Invent Scripture? BILAL-BENCH: Benchmarking Islamic-Source Fabrication in Bengali](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40996)
*Sabah Nushra,Md Kishor Morol*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出BILAL-BENCH：一个240题、孟加拉语、经专家验证的开放式伊斯兰教来源伪造基准，覆盖古兰经归属、圣训、教法、当代议题及对抗性虚假前提提示，采用区分"来源错误"与"断言强度"的双轴评分。评测7个1B–120B开源模型共1680条双标注回答，发现规模并不能单调改善表现，圣训溯源是最弱环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型编造古兰经经文或圣训会赋予文本虚假的宗教权威，读者可能将其视为神圣内容，危害显著。现有伊斯兰幻觉基准多为阿拉伯语且以封闭式问答为主；孟加拉语资源则主要研究通用领域幻觉或事实知识，缺少针对开放式宗教来源伪造的评测工具。

Method: 构建BILAL-BENCH：240题、专家验证的孟加拉语开放式基准，覆盖古兰经归属、圣训、教法（fiqh）、当代议题与虚假前提对抗提示；采用双轴评分标准，将来源错误与断言强度（自信程度）分离；对7个1B–120B参数规模的开源权重模型进行评测，共1680条双标注回答。

Result: 7个模型中有6个在79.3%–95.3%的来源生产类问题上自信地编造内容，且规模增大无单调改善。圣训溯源为各模型最弱类别：315条回答中仅2条正确且来源正确。两个8B BanglaLLaMA变体自信伪造率相近但错误模式不同。模型间差异更大体现在"对无法支撑的主张是否保留"（适当弃答或纠正虚假前提的回答数从6到46条不等），而非来源准确性，且与参数量无关。

Conclusion: 孟加拉语开源模型在开放式伊斯兰来源生成上普遍存在高比例的自信伪造，扩展参数规模并不能解决该问题；模型可靠性更多取决于是否具备恰当的弃答与虚假前提识别能力。因此需要针对弃答行为和来源溯源（尤其圣训）进行专门优化，并将"断言强度"与"来源准确性"分开评估。

Abstract: When a language model invents a Qur'anic verse or hadith, it gives false authority to text that readers may treat as sacred. Existing Islamic hallucination benchmarks are largely Arabic and often closed-ended, while Bengali resources mainly study general-domain hallucination or factual knowledge. We introduce BILAL-BENCH, a 240-question expert-verified benchmark for open-ended Islamic-source fabrication in Bengali. It covers Qur'anic attribution, hadith, fiqh, contemporary issues, and adversarial false-premise prompts, and uses a two-axis rubric that separates source errors from assertiveness. We evaluate seven open-weight models from 1B to 120B on 1,680 double-annotated responses. Six of the seven models confidently fabricate on 79.3-95.3% of source-production questions, with no monotonic improvement from scale. Hadith provenance is the weakest category across models: only two of 315 responses are correct and correctly sourced. The two 8B BanglaLLaMA variants have similar confident-fabrication rates but different error profiles. Models vary far more in whether they withhold a claim they cannot support than in source accuracy: appropriate abstention or false-premise correction ranges from 6 to 46 responses across the benchmark, and does not track parameter count.

</details>


### [86] [Language Models Show Conformity Bias in RAG: When the Majority Endorses a Poisoned Claim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41012)
*Mehrdad Ghassabi,Audrina Ebrahimi,Zahra Zojaji,Amin Milani Fard*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在 SciFact 的 200 道题上，Gemma-3-12b 对毒化证据的检出率从"少数毒化"条件下的 25% 降至"多数毒化"条件下的 10%，呈现出类似从众（conformity）的偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 会把检索文档中的误导性证据引入模型上下文，但已有工作较少考察"多份相互一致的毒化文档"这一数量/一致性因素是否会影响模型对错误信息的识别能力。

Method: 从 SciFact 中以随机种子 38 抽取 200 道问题，构造两种上下文对比：A 为 1 份毒化 + 6 份健康文档（少数条件），B 为 6 份相互一致的毒化文档 + 1 份健康文档（多数条件）；在 Gemma-3-12b 上测量 poison-detection rate，并用精确 McNemar 检验比较配对结果。

Result: 检出率由少数条件的 25%（50/200）下降到多数条件的 10%（20/200）。在结果发生变化的 38 道题中，34 道由"检出"变为"未检出"，仅 4 道反向变化；精确 McNemar 检验 p = 6.04e-7，差异显著。

Conclusion: 结果与"对相互一致的毒化多数证据产生类从众响应"一致，提供了 RAG 错误信息检测中存在从众偏置的初步证据：当毒化主张在检索证据中占主导时，Gemma-3-12b 显著更难检出。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) can expose language models to misleading evidence in retrieved documents. We investigate whether increasing the number of mutually agreeing poisoned documents affects a model's ability to detect misinformation. We evaluate 200 randomly selected questions from SciFact (random seed 38), comparing contexts with one poisoned and six healthy documents against contexts with six mutually agreeing poisoned documents and one healthy document. For Gemma-3-12b, poison-detection rates decrease from 25% (50/200) in the minority condition to 10% (20/200) in the majority condition. Among the 38 questions with different outcomes across conditions, 34 change from detected to undetected, while only four change in the opposite direction (exact McNemar test, p = 6.04e-7). This pattern is consistent with a conformity-like response to a majority of mutually agreeing poisoned documents. Our results provide initial evidence of conformity bias in RAG-based misinformation detection: Gemma-3-12b is substantially less likely to detect poisoned claims when they dominate the retrieved evidence.

</details>


### [87] [EDU-Jev Training System-One Decision Models for Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41079)
*Jiazheng Li,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EDU-Jev：0.8B/2B/4B单步决策模型，直接输出选项概率分布而不生成文本，在教育基准上显著超越商用API与开源4B模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育场景中大量自动化决策本质是从固定选项集合中选择（如打分、判定是否满足评分标准、定位首个错误步骤）。用生成式语言模型做此类决策存在三方面问题：输出需解析、长输出抬高推理成本、文字表达的置信度未必反映正确概率。

Method: 构建EDU-Jev系列（0.8B、2B、4B）：输入评估上下文与带预定义选项的问题，模型不经文本生成，直接返回选项上的概率分布；采用指针头（pointer head）为每个选项打一个marker分数，将预测约束在允许选项内；训练为单阶段监督学习，以软交叉熵对齐目标标签分布，校准性通过实证评估。

Result: 在12个教育基准上，EDU-Jev 4B平均准确率67.6%，比五个商用决策API中最强者高9.4个百分点，比最佳开源4B决策模型高18.7个百分点；在4个跨域教育集上与最强API持平，但在通用决策与推理任务上仍落后；2B模型在单张H100上约150次决策/秒。

Conclusion: 面向教育固定选项决策，"单步、无生成、指针约束"的专用小模型在准确率与吞吐效率上均优于通用生成式方案与商用API，但通用决策与推理能力仍不及大型API。

Abstract: Many automated decisions in education involve choosing from a fixed set of options: assigning a rubric score, checking whether an answer meets a marking criterion, or identifying the first wrong step in a solution. Generative language models are increasingly used to make these decisions, but their responses require parsing, longer outputs increase inference costs, and confidence expressed in words does not necessarily reflect the probability of being correct. We present EDU-Jev, a family of single-pass ("System One") decision models at 0.8B, 2B and 4B parameters. Given an assessment context and a question with predefined answer options, the model returns a probability distribution over those options without generating text. A pointer head scores a marker for each option, ensuring the predictions are restricted to the allowed choices. Training uses a single supervised stage with soft cross-entropy against the available target label distributions; calibration is assessed empirically. Across twelve education benchmarks, EDU-Jev 4B achieves an average accuracy of 67.6%, exceeding the strongest of five commercial decision APIs by 9.4 percentage points and the best open 4B decision model by 18.7 points. On four cross-domain education sets, it matches the strongest API, which remains ahead on general decision-making and reasoning tasks. The 2B model processes about 150 decisions per second on a single H100 GPU.

</details>


### [88] [Language Models Show a "Women-Are-Wonderful" Effect in Blame Attribution That is Not Just Leniency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41116)
*Anika Sharma,Snehalkumar 'Neil' S. Gaikwad,Snigdha Chaturvedi,Shashank Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在语言模型的“归责”任务中发现女性即是美好（WaW）效应：性别改变只影响少数判定，但变动方向一致偏向女性；且效应高度依赖角色——被评判者为女性时归责几率下降9–52%，而受影响方为女性时归责几率上升2.7–9.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明语言模型存在对女性的正面评价偏向（WaW效应），但既往工作多聚焦于对“被评判者”的单一视角。本文旨在检验归责场景下WaW是否同样存在，并区分“被评判者性别”与“受影响方性别”两种角色，考察其交互与情境依赖性。

Method: 从r/AmItheAsshole采集11,270条人际冲突样本，构建匹配的反事实对照，分别操纵被评判者（原帖作者）与受影响方（冲突对方）的性别；在四个语言模型上比较判定结果，并按冲突类型分层分析，另以探索性探针考察同情、合理性、伤害与保护等中间判断。

Result: 性别仅改变少数判定（verdict），但变化方向一致偏向女性。角色效应显著：原帖作者为女性使归责几率降低9–52%；受影响方为女性则使归责几率扩大为2.7–9.2倍。受影响方WaW随冲突类型变化，在沟通类与关系类冲突中增强，在家庭冲突中减弱。探针显示受影响方性别可改变模型对同情、合理性、伤害与保护的判断。

Conclusion: WaW并非单纯“对被评判女性的宽容”，而是一种角色敏感的归责偏差：模型在行为波及女性时评判更严厉。该结果提示归责类应用中的性别偏差需按角色与情境分别审计，而非以单一“女性受益/受损”结论概括。

Abstract: This paper investigates the Women-Are-Wonderful (WaW) effect in language-model blame attribution: a favorable bias in which women receive more positive evaluations than men. Using 11,270 interpersonal conflicts from r/AmItheAsshole, we construct matched counterfactuals that vary the gender of the person being judged (the original poster) and the person affected (the counter-party). Across four language models, gender changes a minority of verdicts, but those changes consistently favor women. The effect is strongly role-dependent. Making the original poster a woman reduces the odds of blame by 9--52%, while making the counter-party a woman multiplies them by 2.7--9.2X. Counter-party WaW also varies across conflict types, strengthening in communication and relationship conflicts and weakening in family conflicts. Exploratory probes suggest that counter-party gender can shift judgments of sympathy, reasonableness, harm and protection. WaW is therefore not just leniency toward women being judged: models also judge conduct more harshly when it affects a woman.

</details>


### [89] [HyperRepair: Recovering Forgotten General Capabilities of Fine-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41147)
*Junrong Tang,Jianghui Wang,Hao Kong,Yang Lin,Yibo Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HyperRepair：一种仅需推理成本的框架，用超网络从源模型的修复经验中学习，为共享预训练主干的新模型生成即插即用的修复侧网（RSN），在保持领域专精的同时恢复通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调提升目标领域性能但会削弱模型的通用能力，现有方法在"通用能力"与"领域专精"之间存在权衡，难以兼得。

Method: HyperRepair 以"仅推理成本"实现通用能力恢复：先在源模型上训练修复器，再从这些修复中学习一个超网络，使其可泛化到共享同一预训练主干的其他模型。超网络利用"激活指纹"（概括模型相对预训练主干的变化）生成定制的修复侧网（RSN），RSN 通过提示条件门控（prompt-conditioned gate）有选择性地施加修正。由此，对来自未见适配领域的模型无需目标域样本、也无需额外优化即可完成恢复。

Result: 在两个模型家族、12 个 LoRA 适配数据集和 15 个通用基准上评估。默认划分下，HyperRepair 达到基模型平均通用分数的 93.4%，同时保留微调模型平均领域分数的 99.5%。

Conclusion: HyperRepair 在无需目标域数据与额外训练的前提下，有效缓解微调的通用能力退化问题，兼顾通用性与领域专精，具备跨模型、跨适配领域的泛化能力，且仅增加推理开销。

Abstract: Fine-tuning improves a language model's performance in a target domain but can weaken its general capabilities. Existing methods often face a trade-off between general capabilities and domain specialization, making it difficult to preserve both. We introduce HyperRepair, a framework for recovering general capabilities while retaining domain specialization at only inference cost. To make recovery generalizable across models sharing a pretrained backbone, HyperRepair learns a hypernetwork from repairs trained on source models. The hypernetwork uses activation fingerprints that summarize changes from the pretrained backbone to generate a tailored Repairing Side Net (RSN), whose prompt-conditioned gate applies corrections selectively. This enables recovery on models from unseen adaptation domains without target-domain examples or additional optimization. We evaluate HyperRpair across two model families, 12 LoRA adaptation datasets, and 15 general benchmarks. On the default split, HyperRepair achieves 93.4% of the base models' average general score while retaining 99.5% of the fine-tuned models' average domain score.

</details>


### [90] [S2TDR: Structured Multimodal Reconstruction for Sticker-Aware Dialogue Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41176)
*Yuanchen Shi,Fang Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Sticker-to-Text Dialogue Reconstruction (S2TDR)框架，将对话中的表情贴图轮次重构为贴合上下文的文本话语，并通过情感分析、意图识别与对话摘要任务验证其优于直接视觉融合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表情贴图在社交媒体对话中承载非字面、依赖语境的情感、立场与意图表达，通用视觉融合或单独图像描述难以准确处理其语义，导致现有多模态对话模型理解不足。

Method: S2TDR是一种结构化多模态重构框架，将贴图解读分解为三步：视觉线索提取、情感—意图推断、上下文感知对话重构；OCR文本仅作为辅助线索（可用时）。重构后的对话为纯文本，可直接输入现有文本对话模型。

Result: 在情感分析、意图识别和对话摘要实验中，S2TDR在多个下游模型上总体优于直接贴图集成方法；组件消融、无OCR贴图及重构质量分析进一步表明方法有效。

Conclusion: 结构化重构是将贴图语义融入对话理解的一种可解释且有效的途径，可兼容既有文本对话模型，并为非字面多模态语义建模提供新思路。

Abstract: Stickers are widely used in social media dialogues to express affect, stance, and intent beyond literal text. Their meanings are often non-literal and context-dependent, making them difficult to handle with generic visual fusion or standalone captioning. We propose Sticker-to-Text Dialogue Reconstruction (S2TDR), a structured multimodal reconstruction framework that converts sticker turns into contextually appropriate textual utterances. S2TDR decomposes sticker interpretation into visual cue extraction, affective-intent inference, and context-aware dialogue reconstruction, using OCR text only as an auxiliary cue when available. The reconstructed dialogue can then be consumed by existing text-based dialogue models. Experiments on sentiment analysis, intent recognition, and dialogue summarization show that S2TDR generally improves multiple downstream models over direct sticker integration methods. Further analyses on component ablations, OCR-empty stickers, and reconstruction quality indicate that structured reconstruction provides an interpretable and effective way to integrate sticker semantics into dialogue understanding.

</details>


### [91] [TraceSpec: Understanding and Improving Speculative Decoding for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41177)
*Jihao Zhao,Shuaishuai Zu,Pengfei Li,Biao Qin,Guanghuan Fang,Ben Feng,Baochuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TraceSpec将LLM agent轨迹评估与自适应草稿训练结合：构建TraceSpec-Traj数据集（1万训练态、2500测试态，覆盖浏览与软件工程），揭示聚合投机解码指标掩盖了上下文位置维度的接受率下降，并据此按状态条件分配预测预算训练草稿模型，回放吞吐较DSpark基线提升12.5%–22.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent通过多轮模型调用在演化的推理-动作-工具反馈历史中执行任务，而现有聚合式投机解码指标把这些性质不同的决策混为一谈，无法指出何处需要草稿监督，指导性不足。

Method: 提出TraceSpec框架，结合动态执行与固定前缀重放；构建TraceSpec-Traj语料，含10,000训练状态与2,500测试状态，覆盖网页浏览与软件工程两类负载。分析五个投机解码基线，按长度匹配比较晚期浏览动作的接受率，并细分工作负载与执行阶段。训练上使用中间assistant轮次，并基于状态条件的、前缀感知的接受差距估计，在固定预测预算下进行自适应分配。

Result: 聚合接受率可随上下文长度上升，但在长度匹配的晚期浏览动作内部反而下降；工作负载与阶段进一步区分这些趋势。在三种目标模型规模和两种温度下，回放吞吐较其DSpark基座提升12.5%–22.6%；Qwen3-4B消融显示自适应分配相较固定整轮监督使接受长度提升3.3%–10.0%，进一步对比验证了状态条件与连续token一致性的作用。

Conclusion: TraceSpec把agent执行轨迹分析与草稿模型改进打通：所提供的评估资源、轨迹发现与训练策略共同说明，轨迹条件驱动的自适应草稿监督是提升agent场景投机解码效率的有效路径。

Abstract: LLM agents execute tasks through repeated model calls over evolving histories of reasoning, actions, and tool feedback. Aggregate speculative-decoding metrics merge these distinct decisions, providing limited guidance on where draft supervision is needed. We present TraceSpec, a framework connecting trajectory evaluation with adaptive draft training. It combines dynamic execution with fixed-prefix replay and introduces TraceSpec-Traj, a corpus of 10,000 training states and 2,500 test states spanning browsing and software engineering. Analysis of five speculative baselines reveals that aggregate acceptance can rise with context length while declining within matched late browsing actions. Workload and phase further distinguish these trends, motivating supervision guided by trajectory conditions. TraceSpec trains on intermediate assistant turns and allocates a fixed prediction budget using state-conditioned estimates of prefix-aware acceptance gaps. Across three target sizes and two temperatures, it improves replay throughput by 12.5% to 22.6% over its DSpark base. Qwen3-4B ablations show that adaptive allocation increases accepted length by 3.3% to 10.0% over fixed full-turn supervision, with additional comparisons substantiating the roles of state conditioning and consecutive-token agreement. The resulting evaluation resources, trajectory findings, and training strategy connect the analysis of agent execution to practical draft improvement.

</details>


### [92] [Beyond Response Scoring: An AI-aware Cognitive Supervision Framework for Automated Mathematical Short Answer Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41215)
*Shengran Guo,Min Chi,Feifei Qiao,Varad Patwardhan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AICG双分支多任务框架，将Q矩阵引导的技能掌握/学习增益认知监督与Gen-AI检测加权结合用于自动短答案评分，在49,402条数据上将MAE降低50.3%、QWK由0.798升至0.927。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASAG方法难以理解与推理含符号和公式的复杂数学答案；仅输出分数的方法无法区分分数相同但技能状态不同的作答，泛化性差；同时忽略了生成式AI使用对学生表现评估可能带来的向上偏差。

Method: 两分支多任务框架：第一分支基于RoBERTa并行回归、序数回归与分类头做单题评分；第二分支利用专家设计的Q矩阵将作答关联到测评技能，联合预测G-DINA导出的技能掌握度与学习增益，并通过共享编码器为评分提供认知监督。另训练Gen-AI检测器（监督对比预训练+线性分类）估计辅助概率并转化为可靠性权重，削弱不可靠认知证据。

Result: 在覆盖19个学期的49,402条教师评分作答上，相对RoBERTa回归，MAE降低50.3%，QWK从0.798提升至0.927；消融显示Q矩阵引导的认知监督是主要增益来源，AI感知加权在无加权认知模型基础上再降MAE 4.2%，并在最近四个后Gen-AI学期中持续改善MAE与QWK。

Conclusion: 将认知诊断（Q矩阵、G-DINA技能掌握）作为评分任务的辅助监督，并对Gen-AI使用进行检测加权，可显著提升复杂数学短答案评分的准确性与一致性，并缓解AI辅助带来的评估偏差。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models have achieved remarkable success in automated short answer grading (ASAG). However, current methods in ASAG struggle to comprehend, reason about and evaluate complex mathematical answers involving symbols and formulas. These score-only methods fail to generalize across diverse responses and skill states associated with identical scores. Moreover, they ignore the potential upward bias introduced by generative Artificial Intelligence (AI) usage in assessing students' performance and knowledge mastery. To address these limitations, we propose AICG (AI-aware Cognitive-supervised Grading), a two-branch multi-task framework. The first branch combines RoBERTa-based regression, ordinal, and classification heads for individual-answer grading. The second branch uses an expert-designed Q-matrix to associate responses with assessed skills and jointly predicts G-DINA-derived skill mastery and learning gain. Through the shared encoder, these skill-level targets provide cognitive supervision for answer grading beyond score labels. AICG further uses a Gen-AI detector, trained through supervised contrastive pretraining followed by linear classification, to estimate assistance probabilities and convert them into reliability weights, attenuating unreliable cognitive evidence during learning assessment. We conduct experiments on 49,402 instructor-graded responses spanning 19 academic semesters. Relative to RoBERTa regression, AICG reduces mean absolute error (MAE) by 50.3% and improves quadratic weighted kappa (QWK) from 0.798 to 0.927. Ablations identify Q-matrix-guided cognitive supervision as the primary source of improvement, while AI-aware weighting further reduces overall MAE by 4.2% over the unweighted cognitive model and consistently improves MAE and QWK across the four most recent post-Gen-AI semesters.

</details>


### [93] [PsyInterviewee: Informational Invariance and Relational Adaptivity in Mental Health Interview Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41289)
*Jiawei Zhang,Huachuan Qiu,Ruocheng Guo,Zijun Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PsyInterviewee 将“受保护的事实通道”与“动态交互通道”分离，以非对称写权限与事实核验同时保证临床信息不变性与关系适应性，在自动化评测中优于四个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的模拟受访者研究主要依靠更丰富的患者画像与行为建模来提升“拟人度”，但拟人度本身不等于可靠性。可靠的模拟需同时满足两点：保持画像支撑的临床事实（informational invariance），并让信息披露与互动行为随访谈动态调整（relational adaptivity）。

Method: 提出 PsyInterviewee 框架：将受保护的事实通道与动态交互通道解耦；采用非对称写权限——对话上下文只能更新“披露状态”和“行为状态”，无权写入新的临床事实；这些状态再决定披露哪些画像支持的信息以及如何表达；此外对生成的临床陈述与画像进行一致性核验。

Result: 在 LLM 访谈者与受访者的自动化交互评测中（由两个 LLM 评委打分），PsyInterviewee 在两条轴线的多数指标上均高于四个代表性基线；消融实验表明两个通道分别贡献临床事实锚定与回答调制。

Conclusion: 模拟受访者的可靠性不能仅由拟人度刻画，而需显式约束事实不变性并保留交互适应性；通过通道分离与非对称写权限可在不牺牲临床一致性的前提下实现行为层面的灵活适配。

Abstract: LLM-based simulated interviewees support interviewer training and the evaluation of mental health interviewing systems. Existing studies have primarily focused on improving human-likeness through richer patient profiles and behavioral modeling. However, human-likeness alone does not establish their reliability. Reliable simulation requires preserving profile-grounded clinical facts (informational invariance) while adapting disclosure and interaction behavior to the interview (relational adaptivity). To this end, we introduce PsyInterviewee, which separates a protected truth channel from a dynamic interaction channel and verifies generated clinical claims against the profile. Specifically, asymmetric write permissions allow dialogue context to update disclosure and behavioral states without authorizing new clinical facts; these states guide what profile-supported information is revealed and how it is expressed. In an automated evaluation of interactions between LLM-based interviewers and interviewees, PsyInterviewee achieves higher scores than four representative baselines on most metrics across both axes, as assessed by two LLM judges. Ablations suggest that the two channels contribute to clinical grounding and response modulation, respectively.

</details>


### [94] [In-Model Context Compression with Agentic Soft Tokens for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41291)
*Xuning He,Kai Fan,Runyu Zhang,Binghuan Wang,Yuxiang Zhang,Xue Jiang,Yihong Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 Agentic Soft-Token Compression (ASTC)，利用模型原生循环动态将已吸收的上下文以软 token 形式前传，与硬 token 精确值并存，实现长程 LLM agent 的压缩与计算节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程 LLM agent 的交互上下文不断累积，带来显著计算开销并淹没关键决策证据。现有方法依赖训练外部压缩器或上下文管理 agent 将上下文重写为更短的硬 token 序列，但外部选择可能与 LLM 演化中的表示不对齐，且必然有损。

Method: ASTC 为免训练方法：(1) 借助常用的混合 Gated Delta Network (GDN)-attention 架构，利用模型原生循环动态判断已被吸收的信息；(2) 将这些信息作为 agentic soft token 前传，同时保留精确值作为 hard token；(3) 形成紧凑序列，减少后续层计算并产生可复用 KV cache，使节省沿 agent 轨迹延伸。

Result: 在长程任务上，ASTC 在端到端 Attention Pairs 最多减少 50.68% 的情况下匹配或超过未压缩 agent 的准确率；而经训练的压缩器每个成功任务所需的 Attention Pairs 是 ASTC 的 15.4 至 103.7 倍。

Conclusion: Agentic soft token 是一种模型原生的方式，可在任务表示演化过程中将已吸收的上下文前传，兼顾效率与准确性，优于外部训练的压缩方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents play an important role in solving complex and sustained tasks, with ever-growing interaction contexts. However, these accumulated contexts will raise substantial computational cost and obscure key decision-relevant evidence. Existing methods typically train external compressors or context-management agents to rewrite context as shorter hard-token sequences, but such external selection may not align with the LLM's evolving representation and is necessarily lossy. In this paper, we introduce Agentic Soft-Token Compression (ASTC), a training-free compression method using soft tokens within LLM agent for long-horizon tasks. Specifically, ASTC uses the model's native recurrent dynamics to determine the already absorbed information, leveraging the commonly-used hybrid Gated Delta Network (GDN)-attention. It then carries this information forward as agentic soft tokens while retaining exact values as hard tokens. The resulting compact sequence reduces later-layer computation and yields reusable KV caches, extending the savings across the agent's trajectory. Extensive experiments on long-horizon tasks show that ASTC matches or exceeds the uncompressed agent's accuracy with up to 50.68% fewer end-to-end Attention Pairs, while trained compressors require 15.4 to 103.7 times more Attention Pairs per successful task. These findings highlight agentic soft tokens as a model-native way to carry absorbed context forward as the task representation evolves.

</details>


### [95] [From Transcription to Reformulation: Defining and Benchmarking Grounded Generative Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41292)
*Qing Wang,Qihan Hu,Hang Lv,Yan Yang,Boqing Liu,Xuechao Zhu,Zehan Li,Hongjie Chen,Jian Kang,Jie Li,Yongxiang Li,Lei Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"Grounded Generative Speech Recognition (GSR)"新范式，把语音识别重新定义为生成"语义有据"的文本而非唯一转写，并构建GSR-Bench基准与多维评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音语言模型已从纯转写扩展到通用语音到文本生成，但现有基准仍假设每条语音只有一个标准转写（single ground-truth transcript），无法容纳可读、格式化或结构化的输出。

Method: 定义"grounded reformulation space"刻画语音支持的所有合法文本实现；建立四级改写能力层级（字面转写、规范化改写、语义改写、语音有据的结构化）；据此构建GSR-Bench，覆盖精炼会议转写、跨方言翻译、上下文感知语音理解及静音/噪声鲁棒性场景；提出结合语音有据性评估与LLM语义判定的多维评估框架，并用人类偏好研究验证其可靠性。

Result: 在从传统ASR到端到端Speech-LLM的多种代表性架构上实验，揭示转写保真度与生成式改写能力之间的本质权衡；并识别出关键失效模式：声学幻觉、无支持生成、指令漂移。

Conclusion: GSR-Bench为超越精确转写匹配的生成式语音识别提供了首个综合评估基准，其分析为未来grounded生成式语音系统的设计与评估提供洞见。

Abstract: Speech language models have expanded automatic speech recognition (ASR) from transcription to general speech-to-text generation. Yet existing benchmarks still assume a single ground-truth transcript per utterance, an assumption that struggles when models produce readable, formatted, or structured outputs. In this work, we introduce Grounded Generative Speech Recognition (GSR), reformulating speech recognition as generating semantically grounded text instead of a single transcript. We define a grounded reformulation space that characterizes all valid textual realizations supported by the input speech, and establish a four-level hierarchy of reformulation capabilities, including literal transcription, canonical reformulation, semantic reformulation and speech-grounded structuring. Based on this formulation, we present GSR-Bench, the first comprehensive benchmark designed to evaluate generative speech recognition systems beyond exact transcript matching. GSR-Bench covers diverse scenarios including refined meeting transcription, cross-dialect translation, context-aware speech understanding, and robustness under silent or noisy acoustic conditions. To address the limitations of word-level evaluation metrics, we develop a multi-dimensional evaluation framework combining speech grounding assessment and LLM-based semantic judgment, whose reliability is validated through human preference studies. Extensive experiments across representative architectures, ranging from conventional ASR systems to end-to-end Speech-LLMs, reveal fundamental trade-offs between transcription fidelity and generative reformulation ability. Our analysis further identifies critical failure modes, including acoustic hallucination, unsupported generation, and instruction drift, providing insights into the design and evaluation of future grounded generative speech systems.

</details>


### [96] [Comparative Evaluation of LLM-Based Review Paradigms for Academic Paper Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41391)
*Fan Huang,Hongyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在ICLR 2025投稿上系统比较5种LLM评审范式（3种输入表示×3个模型），发现绝对评分经基率校准后优于对比排序（Elo），且API调用仅为后者24%；摘要输入优于全文摘要，模型选择与范式同样重要。人类分数对引用量的预测优于任何LLM范式，仅Group Rating在控制人类分数后仍保留引用相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投稿量激增使LLM辅助同行评审受到关注，但评审应如何结构化、模型影响有多大、LLM评分能否预测研究影响力三个问题尚无定论。

Method: 在232篇分层抽样的ICLR 2025投稿上，评测5种评审范式×3种输入表示×3个LLM；引入基率匹配的重校准对照以分离排序信息与分数定位效应；并在全部11,513篇已评分投稿上复现，与always-reject基线比较，并考察分数与引用量的关系。

Result: 直接绝对评分与对比排序在和人类分数的秩相关上相当，但绝对评分将多数预测压缩至录用阈值以上，几乎无法识别评审低于阈值的论文；重校准后Direct Rating一致性高于Elo，API调用仅为其24%。摘要输入优于全文摘要；模型与范式影响相当。全量复现证实上述模式，绝对一致性依赖评测集的分数分布，且所有范式在准确率上均未超过always-reject基线。人类分数对引用的追踪最好，唯Group Rating在控制人类分数后仍保有引用相关性，且是唯一能在录用池内排序的范式。

Conclusion: 仅凭排序质量无法评估评审质量；校准、阈值、模型选择与目标结果均需纳入考量。

Abstract: Growing submission volumes have renewed interest in using large language models (LLMs) to support peer review. It remains unclear how such evaluation should be structured, how much the model matters, and whether LLM scores predict research impact. We evaluate five paradigms across three input representations and three LLMs on 232 stratified ICLR 2025 submissions. Direct absolute rating matches comparative ranking on rank correlation with human scores, yet compresses most predictions above the accept threshold, flagging almost none of the papers reviewers scored below it. A base-rate-matched recalibration control locates the cause: comparative ranking supplies placement, not better ordering, and after recalibration Direct Rating reaches higher agreement than Elo while making 24% as many API calls. Abstract-only input beats full-paper summarization, and model choice matters as much as paradigm choice. Replicating on all 11,513 scored submissions reproduces these patterns, shows absolute agreement depends on the evaluated set's score spread, and finds none beating an always-reject baseline in accuracy. Human scores track citations better than any paradigm, with one exception: Group Rating retains its citation correlation after controlling for human scores, and alone ranks within the accepted pool. Ranking quality alone cannot assess review quality; calibration, thresholds, model, and target outcome all matter.

</details>


### [97] [BlindToChange: AI Agents Can Find Changes When Asked But Do They Look When Needed?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41435)
*Anirudh Phukan,Tanya Goyal,Danish Pruthi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出BlindToChange基准，衡量AI智能体能否主动发现并纳入用户请求中未明说、但会改变答案的近期真实事件；结果显示在事件未被显式提及时性能大幅下降（高达27.5分）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体可实时深度搜索，易被误认为能覆盖近期世界变化；但主流基准（如BrowseComp）直接说明要找什么，无法暴露"智能体未意识到需验证自身假设"的失败。真实用户请求往往只给出最终信息需求，需智能体自行发现相关变化并调整回答。

Method: 构建BlindToChange基准：请求为推荐类任务，其中显而易见的最佳候选因近期真实事件而不再适用；事件自动从Wikidata抽取，支持周期性更新；对比"事件未明说"与"直接询问该事件"两种设定下的系统表现，并分析搜索轨迹归因失败原因。

Result: 两种设定间存在显著差距，即便在高推理投入下也高达27.5个百分点；超过一半的失败源于智能体未直接搜索以核实相关事实是否已改变。

Conclusion: 提出并跟踪一个新的能力维度：系统需考虑用户请求中未显式提及的近期信息；BlindToChange可衡量并持续追踪该维度的进展。

Abstract: Given that AI agents can search the web broadly and deeply in real time, it is tempting to conclude that their outputs account for recent world events. We show that this conclusion does not hold in practice. While agents can search and easily find recent changes, the correctness of their responses depends on whether they search and what they search for. Popular agentic search benchmarks like BrowseComp cannot expose failures wherein agents do not recognize their own assumptions that should be verified, since these benchmarks explicitly state what needs to be found. In contrast, many user requests state only the final information need (e.g., an itinerary for a trip), leaving the agent to discover relevant changes (e.g., the temporary closure of a museum) and account for their consequences. To highlight such gaps, we introduce BlindToChange, a benchmark that measures whether agents proactively discover recent events that affect their responses. Our benchmark comprises requests for recommendations where an obvious candidate is no longer applicable due to a recent real-world event. These events are drawn automatically from Wikidata, allowing BlindToChange to be periodically refreshed. Strikingly, we find a big gap -- as high as 27.5 points, even at high reasoning effort -- in systems' performance, when the recent event is not explicitly mentioned compared to when we ask about it directly. Analyzing search trajectories, we learn that more than half of the failures can be attributed to the fact that agents do not directly search to verify whether a relevant fact has changed. Our benchmark highlights, and tracks, a new axis of progress, wherein systems account for recent information that is not explicitly mentioned in the user request.

</details>


### [98] [KV-Cache Patching: Tracing How and Where Fine-Tuned Behaviors Arise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41480)
*Jonas Loos,Oliver Eberle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过将LoRA微调模型与基座模型的KV cache互相patch，作者定位了微调行为的表征位置：部分行为可经由KV cache迁移，且效应集中于少数关键位置（系统提示与chat模板token）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调改变了模型行为，但这种行为究竟在何处被"实例化"尚不清楚：是仅在答案token生成时产生变化，还是通过对前序token的注意力处理改变而间接导致。

Method: 在LoRA微调模型与未修改基座模型之间互换/修补KV cache，观察行为是否随之迁移或消失；并分析patch比例与patch位置对效应的影响。

Result: 对德语作答、涌现性错位等部分行为，将微调模型的KV cache patch进基座模型可迁移该行为；反向patch可部分或完全消除微调行为。效应随被patch的KV cache比例增大而增强，并集中于少数关键位置，其中系统提示与chat模板token影响异常显著。

Conclusion: 微调行为并非只局限于答案token的生成阶段，而部分地编码于前序上下文的KV表征中；少数关键位置（尤其系统提示与模板token）对行为表达起主导作用。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a language model changes how it responds, but where is this fine-tuned behavior instantiated? To better understand this, we evaluate whether the change in model behavior arises only during the generation of the answer tokens where the change manifests, or whether altered processing of previous tokens causes it via the attention mechanism. For this, we patch the KV cache from a LoRA fine-tuned model into the unmodified base model, and vice versa. Our results show that for some behaviors, like responding in German or emergent misalignment, patching the KV cache can indeed transfer them from the fine-tuned to the base model. Correspondingly, patching the KV cache from base to fine-tune can partially or fully remove fine-tuned behavior. Furthermore, we find that the effect of patching grows with how much of the KV cache is patched and concentrates at a few pivotal positions, with system-prompt and chat-template tokens having disproportionate influence.

</details>


### [99] [KEEPER: Learning Control-Sufficient Textual Memory for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41482)
*Jinkyeong Choi,Chaebin Jeong,Junsung Jeon,Donghyeon Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"自一致性偏差"(self-consistency bias)概念，即递归文本记忆中既有错误随更新持续存在，并给出KEEPER框架比较四种辅助目标对智能体记忆可靠性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归文本记忆会把早期错误带入后续更新，但仅凭任务表现无法判断记忆是否可靠，因此需要区分"任务成绩"与"记忆正确性"，并考察监督目标如何影响错误的持续性。

Method: 在共享的递归接口下，沿"信息含量"与"更新敏感性"两个轴对四种辅助目标进行对比分析（KEEPER）；比较各条件在离线强化学习前后的任务表现，并对一条基线轨迹做claim（陈述）级别的错误持续性分析。

Result: 在评估的各条件中，与奖励相关的预测目标在离线RL前后均取得最高任务表现，优于联合监督；对某基线轨迹的claim级分析显示，即使出现纠正性观测，错误仍持续存在。

Conclusion: 任务表现不足以反映记忆可靠性，评估应同时纳入错误持续性与有效信息保留度，与任务结果并列考察。

Abstract: Recursive textual memory can carry earlier errors into later updates, yet task performance alone does not establish memory reliability. We define self-consistency bias as the persistence of incorrect claims already present in the incoming memory. We present KEEPER, a comparative analysis of four auxiliary objectives along information-content and update-sensitivity axes within a shared recursive interface, examining how supervision targets relate to agent performance. Among evaluated conditions, reward-related prediction achieve the highest task performance before and after offline reinforcement learning, outperforming joint supervision. A claim-level analysis of one baseline trajectory documents error persistence despite corrective observations. Together, these analyses motivate evaluating error persistence and valid-information retention alongside task outcomes.

</details>


### [100] [Sparse Text Diffusion under Dual Budgets: Context Connectivity and Expert Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41487)
*Alexandros Christoforos,Chadbourne Davis,Paul Raj*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文将长上下文扩散模型的两类重复开销形式化为“注意力图可用边数”与“每token激活专家数”两个预算，并据此统一分析局部/膨胀/全局注意力与条件专家计算；提出DualBudget稀疏专家配置，在科学摘要、多跳问答、对话、句对预测上验证，16K以内优于所列基线但随长度增加出现可测退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文扩散模型在token交互（注意力）与前馈/专家计算上重复付出代价，而"稀疏本身即可保留文档级信息"的假设缺乏依据；需要一种把两类成本显式分离、可量化分析的框架。

Method: 将成本表述为两个独立预算：注意力图可用边数与每token激活专家数；用该视角统一连接局部、膨胀、全局注意力与条件专家计算；形式化定义归一化掩码注意力，区分去噪深度与采样迭代次数，并推导图与计算上界；给出DualBudget稀疏专家配置，在四类任务上做长度研究与消融。

Result: 在科学摘要、多跳问答、对话、句对预测上保留全部表格测量；长度研究显示至16K长度相对所列基线有增益，同时随长度增长出现可测的性能退化；消融显示三项报告分数变化不一致（有升有降）；成本记录显示在未说明的计时约定下相对DiffuSeq降低20%。

Conclusion: 双预算视角可把结构稀疏与条件计算纳入同一成本框架，但实证收益有限且随长度衰减；训练速度、吸收态、全局连贯性与部署显存等结论未被测量，应与已有观察严格区分。

Abstract: Long-context diffusion repeatedly pays for both token interaction and feed-forward computation. We formulate these costs as two separate budgets: the edges available to an attention graph and the experts activated for each token. This view connects local, dilated, and global attention with conditional expert computation without assuming that sparsity alone preserves document-level information. We specify normalized masked attention, distinguish denoising depth from sampling iterations, and derive graph and compute bounds. A reported sparse-expert configuration, denoted DualBudget, is analyzed across scientific summarization, multi-hop question answering, dialogue, and sentence-pair prediction. All supplied table measurements are retained. The length study shows gains over the listed baselines through 16K, together with measurable degradation as length increases. Ablations reveal mixed changes across the three reported scores, while the cost record indicates a 20% reduction relative to DiffuSeq under an unspecified timing convention. The analysis separates these observations from unmeasured claims about training speed, absorbing states, global coherence, and deployment memory.

</details>


### [101] [Enhancing Knowledge Entity Relation Extraction in Abstracts and Full-text Content of Academic Articles Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41552)
*Yingyi Zhang,Chengzhi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估多种基于大语言模型（LLM）的知识实体关系抽取（KERE）策略，并引入两种新策略，在自建两个标注数据集上验证LoRA微调T5效果最佳（宏F1为0.7408与0.7013）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究仅考察单一类型的LLM策略，且主要依赖精确率、召回率等指标评估。本文希望从更广的评估角度出发，比较多种LLM与SLM协同策略，并探究其对小模型错误的缓解能力及模型优化方向。

Method: 设计并对比多类策略：闭源LLM的CoT提示、LoRA适配的开源LLM、LLM增强的小预训练模型（SLM）、SLM增强的闭源LLM；新增SLM增强的开源LLM、闭源LLM增强的开源LLM两种策略。人工构建NLP领域两个标注数据集（Abstract与Full-Text），从指标、SLM错误缓解能力、优化方向等多维度评估并开展错误分析。

Result: LoRA适配的T5在Abstract与Full-Text数据集上宏F1最高，分别为0.7408和0.7013；在LLM增强SLM策略下，LLM生成的实体描述可提升SLM性能；错误分析表明，不同LLM策略对SLM各类错误的缓解效果存在差异。

Conclusion: 各LLM策略具有不同局限与错误模式，据此提出若干优化方向；将错误模式互补的策略进行组合可进一步提升KERE性能。

Abstract: This study investigates the effectiveness of large pre-trained language models (LLMs) for knowledge entity relation extraction (KERE) from academic papers. Existing studies have examined individual types of LLM-based strategies and evaluated model performance using metrics such as precision and recall. To provide a broader evaluation, this study investigates multiple strategies, including closed-source LLM-based CoT prompting, LoRA-adapted open-source LLMs, LLM-enhanced small pre-trained language models (SLMs), and SLM-enhanced closed-source LLMs. In addition, this study introduces two strategies, namely SLM-enhanced open-source LLMs and closed-source LLM-enhanced open-source LLMs. These strategies are evaluated from multiple perspectives, including metrics, their ability to mitigate errors observed in SLMs, and potential directions for model optimization. This study manually constructs two annotated datasets in the NLP domain, covering Abstract and Full-Text datasets. Experimental results show that the LoRA-adapted T5 model achieves the highest macro F1 scores of 0.7408 and 0.7013 on the Abstract and Full-Text datasets, respectively. SLMs also benefit from LLM-generated entity descriptions under the LLM-enhanced SLM strategy. Error analysis further reveals that the effectiveness of LLM-based strategies in mitigating SLM errors varies across error types. Based on the identified limitations and error patterns of these strategies, several optimization directions are proposed. In addition, combining strategies with different error patterns further improves KERE performance.

</details>


### [102] [CEFR Level Assessment across Languages and Text Granularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41553)
*Eugene Matsumura,Yuki Arase*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出将focal loss与监督对比学习结合的统一训练目标，用于文本CEFR等级自动评估，在单/多语种、句/篇级设置下均超越强基线并刷新多项基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CEFR等级自动评估中，等级分布天然不均衡，且相邻等级间边界模糊，导致准确预测困难。

Method: 设计统一训练目标：focal loss通过强调欠代表与困难样本缓解类别不均衡；监督对比学习通过增强类内紧凑性与类间分离性学习更具判别力的表示。在句级与篇级、单语与多语设置下评估。

Result: 在所有实验设置下持续优于强基线，在多个CEFR评估基准上取得新的最优性能（SOTA）。

Conclusion: focal loss与监督对比学习的联合目标能有效应对类别不均衡与相邻等级边界模糊问题，是一种通用且有效的CEFR评估训练策略。

Abstract: Automatic assessment of textual CEFR levels is an important task in educational natural language processing. However, accurate CEFR level prediction remains challenging due to the naturally imbalanced distribution of CEFR levels and the ambiguous boundaries between adjacent levels. To address these challenges, we propose a unified training objective that combines focal loss with supervised contrastive learning. Focal loss mitigates class imbalance by emphasizing underrepresented and difficult examples, while supervised contrastive learning learns more discriminative representations by encouraging intra-class compactness and inter-class separation. We evaluate the proposed method on sentence- and document-level CEFR assessment in both monolingual and multilingual settings. Experimental results demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms strong baselines across all settings, establishing new state-of-the-art performance on multiple CEFR assessment benchmarks.

</details>


### [103] [Digital sovereignty in professional translation workflows: a case study of migrating from Windows to Linux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41561)
*Michael Farrell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过CAT工具与TMS生态筛查及一个译者工作站从Windows迁移至Linux的案例，探讨职业翻译中的数字主权，提出"控制—连续性—退出"三原则与三项实践要求，结论认为数字主权取决于工作流依赖的组织方式而非单个应用或操作系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 职业译者的翻译工作流高度依赖商业CAT工具、TMS及云服务，形成对供应商控制的基础设施依赖，威胁工作的自主性与可持续性；现有讨论多聚焦政策层面，缺乏对个体职业译者工作流层面数字主权的实证考察。

Method: 采用混合设计：一是对CAT工具与TMS进行生态筛查（基于文献证据及供应商有限回复），区分"Linux可用性"与"运行独立性"；二是开展单一案例研究，将译者工作站从Windows迁移至Linux，以本地可运行的Linux核心（LoopCAT、LibreOffice、本地部署的TranslateGemma，辅以OCR、通信、凭证管理与备份工具）重建完整职业工作流。分析框架为三原则（Control、Continuity、Exit）结合三项实践要求（原生Linux运行、不依赖强制性外部服务、可独立访问关键数据与资源）。

Result: 生态筛查显示：基于浏览器的平台虽可在Linux上访问，但仍依赖供应商控制的基础设施，即"可访问性"不等于"运行独立性"。迁移案例表明，以本地Linux核心重构单一职业翻译工作流是可行的，且在需要时仍可选择性保留部分云服务。

Conclusion: 数字主权取决于工作流依赖的组织方式，而非具体应用或操作系统；它不要求技术隔离，而要求对关键组件的实质控制、工作的连续性以及可行的退出选项。该框架针对本研究特定案例构建，推广至其他职业工作流时可能需要调整。

Abstract: This study examines digital sovereignty in professional translation through a screening of computer-assisted translation tools and translation management systems, and a case study of migrating a translator's workstation from Windows to Linux. The framework combines three principles - Control, Continuity and Exit - with three practical requirements: native Linux operation, independence from mandatory external services, and independent access to essential data and resources. Drawing on documentary evidence and the limited number of responses received from providers, the ecosystem screening distinguishes Linux accessibility from operational independence: browser-based platforms may remain dependent on provider-controlled infrastructure. The migration case demonstrates the feasibility of reconstructing one professional workflow around a locally operable Linux core, using LoopCAT, LibreOffice and locally run TranslateGemma, alongside tools for optical character recognition, communication, credential management and backup. Selected cloud services remained available where needed. The findings suggest that digital sovereignty depends on how workflow dependencies are organized, rather than on individual applications or operating systems. It does not require technological isolation, but meaningful control over essential components, continuity of work and practical exit options. The framework is tailored to the case examined here and may require adaptation to other professional workflows.

</details>


### [104] [BYOD: Build Your Own DLM - Efficient Autoregressive-to-Diffusion Language Model Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41574)
*Ruurd J. A. Kuiper,Robert A. Bagheri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出BYOD——一种仅用LoRA、免架构特调的AR-to-DLM转换配方，在Gemma 2 9B、Llama 3.1 8B、Qwen2.5 7B、Ministral 8B上成功诱导扩散生成，为Mistral系首个报道的转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型具备并行token更新、灵活生成与双向注意力优势，但现有系统依赖昂贵预训练或全参数微调，缺乏低成本、可推广的AR-to-DLM转换方案。

Method: 仅训练LoRA适配器（占最终参数4.1–5.8%），基座参数全部冻结；无需架构特调即可通用；每次转换25,000次迭代、最多102.4M tokens，单张Colab GPU上3.1–4.0小时完成。

Result: 四个模型均能从全掩码序列起生成、在受控注意力测试中利用双向上下文，并保留大量父模型知识；BYOD-Gemma在九任务评测中平均准确率50.6%，接近LLaDA 8B Instruct的51.9%。主要短板为低NFE预算下开放生成质量：生成128 token/64步时困惑度8.3–11.1，而LLaDA为4.1、AR父模型2.2–3.0。

Conclusion: BYOD为将扩散生成扩展到其他现有模型家族提供了实用路径，同时明确了高效转换下一步需弥合的差距（低NFE下的开放生成质量）；并开源独立训练评测实现与交互demo。

Abstract: Unlike autoregressive (AR) models, diffusion language models (DLMs) permit parallel token updates, flexible generation, and bidirectional attention, but leading systems rely on costly pretraining or full-model fine-tuning. We present Build Your Own DLM (BYOD), a generalizable LoRA-only recipe for parameter-efficient AR-to-DLM conversion. Without architecture-specific tuning, BYOD induces diffusion generation in Gemma 2 9B, Llama 3.1 8B, Qwen2.5 7B, and Ministral 8B; to our knowledge, this is the first reported AR-to-DLM conversion of a Mistral model.
