Conclusion: Re3Culture 是首个显式服务于文化价值对齐"目标定义"的人本锚定资源，表明目标定义需满足社群归属与内部覆盖、社群引导的具体情境、显式价值引出三项要求，从而为文化价值对齐提供可操作的数据规范。

Abstract: Cultural value alignment requires not only model optimization but also a well-specified target grounded in real-human evidence. Yet how such targets should be grounded in human data remains largely unexplored, raising three fundamental questions: (1) whose values should be represented? (2) where should cultural values be observed? and (3) what signals reveal underlying values? We investigate these questions through Re3Culture, a large-scale dataset linking (i) Representative communities: 700+ human participants across four cultures recruited through cultural-affiliation screening and demographic-aware sampling; (ii) Realistic scenario: culturally sensitive scenarios selected by community members, e.g., family and kinship; and (iii) Revealed value signals: 1,400+ value surveys, 20,000+ topic-grounded behavioral principles and explicit value priorities with responses for 6,000+ situated questions. Our analysis shows why these choices matter for defining the target, with three implications. First, estimating cultural values requires both community affiliation and broad internal coverage. Substituting other cultures or restricting subgroup coverage shifts value estimates. LLM simulations also disproportionately reflect particular subgroups. Second, values must be observed in concrete, community-guided situations. Abstract questionnaire profiles weakly recover situated priorities, and communities differ in which scenarios they identify as culturally value-revealing. Third, value priorities should be elicited explicitly, not learned from response preferences or bottom-line answers alone. Preferences conflate value agreement with other response-level qualities, and similar answers can be derived from different value priorities. By addressing these requirements, Re3Culture provides the first human-grounded resource that explicitly informs target definition for cultural value alignment.

</details>


### [206] [RECAST: REconstruction of Chain-of-Thought via Auditable Structured Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41813)
*Qi Wang,Kang Xu,Chengcheng Wan,Jiangtao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 RECAST：仅凭问题与闭源模型的最终答案，重建第一人称结构化推理链并清理成 SFT 数据，无需任何隐藏推理通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭源推理模型只暴露最终答案，中间推理被 API 界面、工具调用策略与安全过滤屏蔽。现有 CoT 抽取方法依赖侧信道（可见 reasoning 字段、工具调用携带的 scratchpad、签名/加密推理块）。作者复现三种代表性 SOTA 抽取方法，在标准 chat-completions 接口访问的 Claude-Sonnet-5.5 上均返回零 CoT token；失败是结构性的：无 reasoning 字段、命名工具选择被拒、自由文本 scratchpad 参数被过滤、无可重放的推理块。因此需另辟蹊径。

Method: RECAST 为基于重建的框架：以问题与目标模型最终答案为条件，驱动一个解码器重建第一人称结构化 rationale，随后进行清洗并构建可直接用于 SFT 的数据。系统开源，封装为 agent skill，单条 prompt 即可部署并跑通完整流水线。文中还给出威胁模型与受控的产物验证。

Result: 三种已有抽取方法在 Claude-Sonnet-5.5 上均获得零 CoT token；RECAST 在 Claude-Sonnet-5.5 上完成实例化，并在 GPT-6.1 与 Gemini 3 上以相同"仅答案"协议取得成功的定性重建。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，RECAST 是首个不借助隐藏推理通道、从 Claude-Sonnet-5.5、GPT-6.1、Gemini 3 等前沿闭源模型重建 CoT 的系统。本文为预印本，仅描述系统、威胁模型与受控产物验证，不声称大规模基准提升。

Abstract: Large closed-source reasoning models expose only their final answers. The intermediate reasoning that produces those answers is hidden behind API surfaces, tool-call policies, and safety filters. Existing chain-of-thought (CoT) extraction methods assume a side channel: a visible reasoning field, a scratchpad carried through tool calls, or a signed/encrypted reasoning block. We reproduce three representative state-of-the-art extraction methods and find that, on Claude-Sonnet-5.5 accessed through the standard chat-completions interface, all three return zero CoT tokens. The failure is structural rather than a bug: the reasoning field is absent, named tool selection is rejected, free-text scratchpad arguments are filtered, and no replayable reasoning block is exposed. We therefore take a different route. We present RECAST, a reconstruction-based framework that conditions a decoder on the question and the target model's final answer, reconstructs a first-person structured rationale, cleans it, and builds SFT-ready data. To the best of our knowledge, RECAST is the first system to reconstruct CoT from frontier closed models such as Claude-Sonnet-5.5, GPT-6.1, and Gemini 3 without using hidden reasoning channels. We instantiate RECAST on Claude-Sonnet-5.5 and additionally obtain successful qualitative reconstructions on GPT-6.1 and Gemini 3 with the same answer-only protocol. The system is open-source and packaged as an agent skill: one prompt deploys it and runs the complete pipeline to produce SFT-ready CoT data. This preprint describes the system, its threat model, and a controlled artifact validation; it does not claim large-scale benchmark improvements.

</details>


### [207] [ReuScope: Auditing Data Reuse and Evaluation Sensitivity in Capsule Vision 2024](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41835)
*Eunseob Choi,Kyeonghun Kim,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ReuScope 审计协议，以 Capsule Vision 2024 为案例，分离"数据复用观测"与"性能对数据来源的敏感性"，避免将验证集分数下降误读为泄漏导致的虚高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像挑战赛数据常跨分区复用，但现有做法缺少区分"数据关系度量"与"评估敏感性"的规范流程，易把源移除引起的性能下降直接解释为泄漏虚高。

Method: 协议结合三部分：(1) 数据关系测量（字节级同一文件、共享源录像、缺失患者标识）；(2) 固定预测、改变评估总体的对比；(3) 固定评估、改变训练数据的对比。另设匹配对照（样本量、类别数）控制混淆，并做移除近邻训练行 vs 等量远距离训练行的探索性双重差分实验（10 个配对种子）。

Result: 字节完全相同文件越过训练-验证边界，41 段 Kvasir-Capsule 录像的帧同时出现在两分区；移除相关训练行后公开验证平衡准确率由 82.5% 降至 61.2%；同一模型在其他来源验证行上则反而提升 1.38 个百分点。匹配对照排除样本量与类别数解释。探索性 DiD 估计为 -0.355 个百分点（真实类概率），准确率的配对 t 区间跨零。

Conclusion: ReuScope 区分"已观测到的数据复用"与"条件性性能敏感性"；公开验证集下降只度量对来源移除的敏感性，而非泄漏导致的虚高量，并为每条结论明确规定总体、类别支撑与不确定性单位。

Abstract: We develop ReuScope, a protocol for auditing data reuse and evaluation sensitivity, using Capsule Vision 2024 as a case study. It combines measurements of data relationships with two comparisons: changing the evaluation population while holding predictions fixed, and changing training data while holding evaluation fixed. Byte-identical files cross the public training-validation boundary, and all 41 Kvasir-Capsule recordings that contribute frames occur in both partitions. Shared source recordings establish cross-split overlap; missing cross-recording patient identifiers prevent counting unique shared patients. Across ten paired seeds, removing all training rows attributed to Kvasir-Capsule, plus five rows from other sources flagged as near-matches to it, lowers public-validation balanced accuracy from 82.5% to 61.2%. Evaluating the same fitted models on the validation rows of the other sources reverses the comparison: removal improves balanced accuracy by 1.38 percentage points. The public-validation drop measures sensitivity to source removal, not an amount of leakage-induced inflation. Matched controls show that sample size and class counts alone do not explain the observed score differences. An exploratory experiment removes the training neighbors of validation frames that have close training matches, or equally many distant training rows, and yields a difference-in-differences estimate, relative to matched unflagged frames, of -0.355 percentage points in true-class probability; the paired-t interval for accuracy spans zero. The resulting procedure distinguishes observed reuse from conditional performance sensitivity and specifies the population, class support and uncertainty unit for each conclusion.

</details>


### [208] [Dynamical Memory Transformer: Selective Recurrent Memories with Constant Cost Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41850)
*Yannis Bendi-Ouis,Xavier Hinaut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Dynamical Memory Transformer（DMT），一种将序列编码与信息读取解耦的混合架构：编码端用多个由复对角循环矩阵动力系统实现、带输入依赖泄漏率的记忆单元，解码端用注意力读取其输出；支持O(T)总工作量、O(log T)并行深度的结合扫描，推理时状态尺寸固定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer显式保留序列历史、可直接检索，但KV-Cache规模与新token计算成本随上下文长度增长；状态空间模型将历史压缩为固定大小的循环状态，却损失了可寻址性。作者希望在保留固定内存与并行训练优势的同时提升信息的可读取与利用能力。

Method: DMT为混合架构，分离序列编码与信息读取。编码器由若干记忆单元组成，每个单元是带复对角循环矩阵的动力系统，其更新由输入依赖的泄漏率控制；解码器通过注意力直接读取并组合各单元输出，不经过token历史。该递推形式允许结合扫描（associative scan），在固定维度下实现O(T)总工作量与O(log T)并行深度；自回归推理时循环状态与卷积缓存保持固定大小。

Result: 在认知任务与语言建模任务上与Mamba、Transformer对比：DMT在多项认知任务上取得最佳结果，语言建模性能接近Transformer，但落后于Mamba；在selective copy任务上，DMT的长度外推能力强于Mamba与Transformer。

Conclusion: 结果表明并行动力记忆单元是一种在序列上保持并利用信息的有前景机制，在固定内存、可并行训练与可寻址读取之间取得了折中；其语言建模能力仍是相对短板，需进一步改进。

Abstract: Transformers retain an explicit history of the sequence, allowing attention to directly retrieve past representations. However, the size of the KV-Cache and the cost of a new token increase with the context length. State Space Models compress the past into a fixed-size recurrent state, at the cost of reduced addressability. We present the Dynamical Memory Transformer (DMT), a hybrid architecture that separates sequence encoding from information reading. The encoder uses several memory units, each implemented by a dynamical system with a complex diagonal recurrent matrix. An input-dependent leak rate controls their updates. The decoder then uses attention to read and combine their outputs, without going through the token history. The formulation of the recurrence allows an associative scan with O(T ) total work and O(log T ) parallel depth, with fixed dimensions. During autoregressive inference, the recurrent states and the convolution cache remain fixed in size. We compare DMT with Mamba and Transformer on cognitive and language modeling tasks, and show its strengths and limitations: DMT achieves the best results on several cognitive tasks and language modeling performance close to that of Transformer, but remains behind Mamba in language modeling. On selective copy, DMT also shows stronger length extrapolation than Mamba and Transformer. These results demonstrate that parallel dynamical memory units are a promising mechanism for retaining and using information over sequences.

</details>


### [209] [Learning to Interact and Direct: Co-Evolving Video Director Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41865)
*Yingzhao Jian,Zhongan Wang,Yuxuan Hou,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LIT框架，将视频导演智能体、模拟用户与元工程师组成协同进化闭环，共同提升导演与交互能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频导演智能体的自进化研究侧重脚本规划，却忽视了"导演执行"与"用户交互"之间的相互强化关系：交互改善能澄清用户意图并优化工作流，执行可靠则使协作从问题排查转向创意决策。

Method: LIT由三部分组成：导演智能体、模拟用户、元工程师。模拟用户分配创意任务以探测导演弱点；元工程师利用任务轨迹修订导演的交互与制作策略。针对元智能体被动归因的局限，引入协同进化范式——元智能体主动学习外部知识以提升问题发现与解决方案生成能力，三者构成进化闭环。

Result: 在Test与OOD Test划分上，LIT相较初始流水线分别提升制作分数19.1%、交互分数32.0%；受控消融显示制作与交互提升存在相关性，协同进化机制进一步促进了导演技能与交互模式的探索。

Conclusion: 导演与交互的协同进化可形成自增强闭环，成为提升视频导演智能体综合能力的有效路径。

Abstract: Video director agents orchestrate multistage production to realize users' creative visions. Emerging research on their self-evolution advances screenplay planning effectively. However, the mutual reinforcement between directing and user interaction remains largely overlooked. Improved interaction clarifies user intent and informs workflow refinement, while more reliable execution shifts collaboration from troubleshooting to creative decisions. To this end, we introduce LIT (Learning to Interact and DirecT), a framework comprises a director agent, a simulated user, and a meta engineer. Simulated user assigns creative tasks to probe the director's weaknesses, while the meta engineer uses the task traces to revise the director's interaction and production strategy. Although this approach can address discovered interaction and production issues, it still relies on passive attribution by the meta agent. This limits its ability to adapt to broader user interaction patterns and production techniques. To solve this problem, we equip LIT with a co-evolution paradigm. The meta agent proactively learns external knowledge, thereby improving its ability to discover problems and propose solutions. The meta agent, simulated user, and director together form an evolutionary closed loop, collaboratively enhances the system's directing and interaction capabilities. Across the Test and OOD Test splits, LIT improves production and Interaction scores by 19.1% and 32.0%, respectively, relative to the initial pipeline. Controlled ablations reveal the correlation between improvements in production and interaction, where the co-evolution mechanism further encourage directing skill and interaction mode exploration.

</details>


### [210] [Fuzzy selective state model for forecasting the dynamics of consumer preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41879)
*P. A. Golovinski,A. A. Drobyshev,A. O. Shatalova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将顾客偏好建模为三角模糊数，用Mamba型选择性状态空间模型在Matveev双分量模糊数代数中演化，证明状态保持为三角数且为历史观测的凸组合，模糊度不随时间累积，并在合成数据上验证预测精度与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售连锁顾客偏好具有模糊性，需要既能预测偏好动态、又能给出可语言解释的不确定性估计的模型。现有方法难以同时保证模糊数运算的代数合法性与模糊度不随时间膨胀，也缺乏对顾客永久流失的检测手段。

Method: 以三角模糊数描述顾客偏好，构建Mamba类选择性状态空间模型，隐状态取三角数，运算在Matveev双分量模糊数代数中进行；递推参数由清晰特征计算，模糊部分保持非负系数线性。

Result: 证明状态始终为合规三角数且为历史观测的凸组合，模糊度不随时间累积，代数计算与Zadeh扩展原理一致；含竞品促销与顾客转换的合成实验中，107参数轻量模型预测中心MAE为0.154（常量惯性算子0.163、朴素预测0.179），与同架构清晰模型持平，并额外给出可语言解读的模糊度估计。

Conclusion: 该框架在保证模糊代数严谨性的同时实现轻量高精度偏好预测，并能利用预测的语言化读出识别顾客向竞争对手的永久性转移。

Abstract: We consider forecasting the dynamics of consumer preferences of retail chain customers. A customer's preference is described by a triangular fuzzy number. The evolution of the preference over time is governed by a Mamba-type selective state space model whose hidden state is a triangular number, with computations carried out in M.G.~Matveev's two-component algebra of fuzzy numbers. The recurrence parameters are computed from crisp features, while the fuzzy part of the model remains linear with non-negative coefficients. We show that the state then always remains a proper triangular number and is a convex combination of past observations, so that the fuzziness of the forecast does not accumulate over time and the algebraic computations coincide with Zadeh's extension principle. On a synthetic example with competitor promotions and customer switching, a lightweight 107-parameter model reaches a mean absolute error of the forecast centre of 0.154 versus 0.163 for the constant-inertia evolution operator and 0.179 for the naive forecast, matches a crisp model of the same architecture and additionally provides a linguistically interpretable estimate of forecast vagueness. We also show how the linguistic readout of the forecast can be used to detect a permanent switch of a customer to a competitor.

</details>


### [211] [Automated Keyword Generation Using LLMs in a High-Volume Data Virtualization Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41893)
*Saiful Khan,Yiwen Xing,Joyraj Chakraborty,Afrah Farea,Philip Beaucamp,Samuel Jackson,Min Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出用LLM为数据虚拟化服务中的虚拟数据集自动生成关键词的5种文本选取策略，并在5个案例、3种LLM与2种嵌入技术下评测，结果表明策略效果依赖文本描述质量，据此实现多策略关键词生成服务(KGS)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DVS中用户会基于源数据集创建大量虚拟数据集，需频繁检索发现已有数据集以避免重复劳动；而有效搜索引擎依赖关键词，人工标注成本高，故需自动生成关键词。

Method: 基于LLM生成关键词：设计5种为LLM选取不同文本数据集合的策略；在5个不同源数据集的应用案例所产生的虚拟数据集上应用这5种策略；使用3种LLM生成器与2种嵌入技术进行实验对比（含基于嵌入的相似度评估）。

Result: 5种策略的性能取决于文本描述的质量；实验揭示了不同策略在不同案例和生成器/嵌入组合下的表现差异，从而支持采用多策略组合方案。

Conclusion: 采用多策略实现的关键词生成服务(KGS)集成于扩展的DVS中；自动化关键词生成对数据丰富、高频访问的大规模DVS不可或缺。

Abstract: In modern data infrastructure, a data virtualization service (DVS) allows users to create many versions of source datasets for different processing tasks. For example, a multivariate or multi-modality dataset can be used for different machine learning tasks (e.g., classification, detection, segmentation, forecasting, and so on). Data selection, participation, integration, and preprocessing in different tasks result in many virtual datasets [Khan et al., SSC 2026]. Meanwhile, different users need to discover existing virtual datasets frequently and easily to avoid duplicate efforts. To enable an effective search engine in a DVS, it is desirable to automatically generate keywords for each virtual dataset. In this work, we present a solution for generating keywords for virtual datasets using large language models (LLMs). We propose five strategies for selecting different collections of text data for processing by the LLMs. We applied the five strategies to virtual datasets created in five application case studies with different source datasets. We conducted experiments with three LLM generators and two embedding techniques. The experiments show that the performances of the five strategies depend on the quality of the text descriptions. The results help us adopt a multi-strategy implementation of a new keyword generation service (KGS) as part of an extended DVS. The work demonstrates that the capability of automated keyword generation is indispensable for a high-volume DVS that is dataset-rich and frequently accessed.

</details>


### [212] [Using Machine Learning to Measure Self-Efficacy Belief Through Learners' Interaction Traces in Technology-Enhanced Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41900)
*Maeva Somny,Linda Chouati,Marie Lefevre*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于数据挖掘与机器学习的方法，从学习者在TEL系统中解题时的交互痕迹检测低自我效能感，并通过跨两所高校的实验验证分类模型的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我效能感影响学习绩效、学业选择与未来职业发展；TEL环境兼具支持绩效与动机的潜力，若能在学习过程中尽早识别低自我效能感学习者，便可及时实施补救干预。然而，基于TEL使用数据检测自我效能感的研究仍十分匮乏，故本研究具有探索性质。

Method: 开展跨两所大学的实验，同步采集学习者的交互痕迹与自我效能感自评量表数据；构建包含多个分类器的机器学习流水线，进行基于数据挖掘的特征工程与筛选，以识别指示低自我效能感的关键特征。

Result: 初步结果表明可基于交互痕迹实现对低自我效能感的检测；同时发现自我效能感在学习过程中具有高度动态性，对其进行近距离、连续监测有助于提升预测性能。

Conclusion: 研究证实了利用TEL交互痕迹检测低自我效能感的可行性，强调应持续监测自我效能感的动态变化以改进预测，并据此为后续补救性教学实践与系统完善提供方向。

Abstract: Self-efficacy beliefs play a critical role in learners' performance, academic choices, and, more broadly, in their future career trajectories. As Technology-Enhanced Learning (TEL) environments have demonstrated strong potential for supporting both performance and motivation, investigating self-efficacy within such environments is of particular interest, especially for implementing remedial practices as early as possible to support learners' self-efficacy beliefs. Our research aims to develop a system capable of detecting low self-efficacy based on learners' interaction traces while they solve exercises in a TEL system. In this paper, we present initial results and future perspectives on a detection approach based on data mining and machine learning techniques to identify features indicative of low self-efficacy beliefs. Because the detection of learners' self-efficacy beliefs through their use of TEL systems remains largely unexplored, this work is exploratory in nature. To this end, we conducted an experiment across two universities to collect interaction traces and self-reported measures of self-efficacy. We then developed a machine learning pipeline comprising multiple classification models, enabling us to highlight the importance of closely monitoring of learners' self-efficacy beliefs for improved prediction performance, given their highly dynamic nature during learning.

</details>


### [213] [ReX-GG: a LLM Ensemble Pipeline for Relation-extraction and Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41901)
*Giacomo Magnifico,Eduard Barbu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种可定制的轻量级多LLM协同集成工作流，借助JSON结构化输出与匿名同行评审式排序抑制幻觉，并在英文科普文本因果关系抽取任务上实现平均感知准确率0.778。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM集成框架多依赖多步骤流程与额外的答案排序模块，偏向token/span分析而非结构化输出，导致架构笨重且存在多环节失效风险；而更轻量的方案又易受幻觉传播影响、缺乏输出控制，因此需要兼顾轻量与可控的集成方案。

Method: 构建可定制的轻量级多LLM协同集成工作流：以JSON结构化输出约束各模型生成，采用匿名同行评审式排序进行答案选择，从而缓解幻觉输出与单模型失效点；在英文科普文章的关系抽取任务上验证，覆盖四类本体论支撑的关系类型（强因果、弱因果、对比、组合），并引入语义节点规范化，最终输出交互式颜色编码HTML因果图；通过匿名用户研究进行评估。

Result: 匿名用户研究显示，相对于人工标注金标准，系统平均感知准确率达到0.778；模块化架构支持API式与本地部署两种LLM配置。

Conclusion: 该轻量级、模块化的协同集成框架可在无需重型排序模块的情况下有效抑制幻觉并提升输出可控性，具备灵活部署能力，且以开放许可发布以促进可复现性与协作研究。

Abstract: Current LLM ensemble frameworks focus on multi-step setups with additional modules for answer ranking, often opting for token and span analysis rather than structured outputs, leading to heavyweight architectures with potential fail states along the pipeline. Faster, lighter solutions are more vulnerable to hallucination propagation and can lack output control in more complex pipelines. This paper proposes a customisable, lightweight ensemble workflow of coordinated Large Language Models that leverages JSON-structured outputs and anonymous peer-review ranking to mitigate hallucinatory outputs and single-model failure points. The pipeline is demonstrated on a relation extraction task applied to popular science articles in English, targeting four ontologically-grounded relation types (strong causation, weak causation, contrastive, and compositional), with semantic node canonicalisation and interactive, colour-coded HTML causal graphs as the final output. Performance is evaluated through an anonymous user study, achieving an average perceived accuracy of 0.778 against a human-annotated gold standard. The modular architecture supports flexible deployment across both API-based and in-house LLM setups, and the full framework is released under an open license to foster reproducibility and collaborative research.

</details>


### [214] [Structure-Aware Skill Retrieval and Arbitration for Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41909)
*Anran Liu,Benzhao Hou,Zongsheng Cao,Daichao Wang,Lang Chen,Shitong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出以原型（archetype）为中心、具备检索增强的技能智能体 ArchOR，在 OptSkills 基础上加入 CARL 与 S³R 两个检索模块，在相同主干与技能库下将五个基准的 Micro-Avg. Pass@1 提升至 65.00%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体将自然语言优化任务转为求解器可校验的答案，但在叙述性改写下鲁棒性差，且可复用经验多以实例级别存储，泛化与迁移能力受限。

Method: 在 OptSkills 上扩展：(1) Centroid-Aware Representation Learning (CARL)，通过低秩原型残差精炼缓存技能表示的排序；(2) Signature-Guided Retrieval (S³R)，为技能附加开放维度结构签名，以加权 Jaccard 重叠优先筛选候选，并将语义重排限制在 top-K 集合内；(3) 保基线仲裁规则，决定结构检索路径何时替换原选择器。

Result: 在与 OptSkills 相同的 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 主干及公开技能库下，五个基准上 Micro-Avg. Pass@1 达 65.00%，较 OptSkills 提升 1.64 pp（Micro-Avg.）与 2.68 pp（Macro-Avg.）。

Conclusion: 以原型/结构签名为中心的技能组织与检索机制可有效缓解叙述改写下的脆弱性并提升技能复用效率，且在不更换主干模型的前提下取得稳定增益。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can turn natural-language optimization tasks into solver-checked answers, but current agents remain brittle under narrative rewrites and often store reusable experience at the instance level. We introduce \textsc{ArchOR}, an archetype-centric skill agent that builds on \textsc{OptSkills} by adding two retrieval-focused modules. \emph{Centroid-Aware Representation Learning} (CARL) refines the ordering of cached skill representations through a low-rank prototype residual. \emph{Signature-Guided Retrieval} (S\textsuperscript{3}R) attaches open-dimensional structural signatures to skills, prioritizes candidates by weighted Jaccard overlap, and bounds semantic re-ranking to a top-$K$ set. A baseline-preserving arbitration rule determines when the structural route should replace the original selector. With the same DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash backbone and released skill library, \textsc{ArchOR} reaches 65.00\% Micro-Avg.\ Pass@1 across five benchmarks, improving over \textsc{OptSkills} by 1.64~pp Micro-Avg.\ and 2.68~pp Macro-Avg.

</details>


### [215] [Sensorium: An Open-Source Multimodal Data Collection and Curation Library for Embodied Surgical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41946)
*Lorenzo Mazza,Ariel Rodriguez,Stefanie Speidel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Sensorium——一个基于 ROS/ROS2 的开源 Python 库，用于从可配置的机器人及传感器数据流中采集、处理、标注与整理多模态手术数据，并在 1516 次离体/活体及体模实验中验证其可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身手术 AI 依赖可靠的多模态数据，但手术数据采集成本高、难以复现，且高度依赖特定硬件配置，缺乏统一的数据记录、处理、标注与整理工具链。

Method: 开发开源 Python 库 Sensorium，基于 ROS 与 ROS2 中间件，集成面向操作者的数据采集、可配置处理流程、语义标注和回合制（episodic）数据集生成四大模块，支持可配置的机器人与传感器数据流接入。

Result: 在涵盖多种手术任务与传感配置的 1516 次体模、离体及活体猪实验中评估了 Sensorium，并报告了初步的可用性反馈。

Conclusion: Sensorium 提供了端到端的多模态手术数据管护框架，可降低数据采集与复用的门槛，支撑具身手术 AI 的数据基础设施需求；后续需进一步完善定量可用性评估。

Abstract: Embodied surgical AI requires reliable multimodal data, yet surgical data collection remains costly, difficult to repeat, and strongly dependent on the hardware setup. We present Sensorium, an open-source Python library for recording, processing, annotating, and curating multimodal surgical data from configurable robot and sensor streams using the Robot Operating System (ROS) and ROS2 middleware. The library combines operator-facing data acquisition, configurable processing, semantic annotation, and episodic dataset generation. We evaluate Sensorium across 1516 recordings of phantom, ex vivo, and in vivo porcine experiments involving multiple surgical tasks and sensing configurations, and report preliminary usability feedback.

</details>


### [216] [Optimizing Stochastic Policy Geometry for Few-Step Flow Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41949)
*Zhuguanyu Wu,Congjia Chen,Ruihao Gong,Yang Yong,Shiqiao Gu,Yi Xu,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Geo-FSRL 提出一种几何最优的选择准则，用于少步流模型 GRPO 中随机去噪转移策略的选取，在保持流系数不变的同族高斯策略中最小化条件 KL，并配合梯度尺度匹配归一化权重，在 4 步图像/视频生成中优于 Flow-GRPO 与 Flow-CPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步流模型依赖极少步的大幅去噪转移，对其施加 GRPO 需在转移中引入随机性；当单步跨越较宽噪声区间时，随机策略的选择成为优化关键。已有方法仅约束策略保持目标流系数，但该约束对应一族可行策略，缺乏在其中择优的判据。

Method: 提出选择原则：在给定条件状态与时间步对下，选取使任意非零速度输出更新所诱导的条件 KL 散度最小的可行策略。在系数保持的各向同性高斯转移族中，该准则约化为最小化单一标量几何因子（同时支配速度空间 Fisher 信息与条件转移对数似然比方差）；推导该族内唯一闭式最优解，且与更新幅度、方向无关，转移系数由源与目标噪声水平共同决定。鉴于最小化后的几何因子仍随步变化，进一步采用梯度尺度匹配，推导闭式策略梯度权重与 KL 权重以归一化显式 score 尺度与参考 KL 曲率。训练使用原始少步调度，推理保留确定性求解器。

Result: 在 4 步设定下，Geo-FSRL 在图像生成与视频生成模型上均取得高于 Flow-GRPO 与 Flow-CPS 的最终奖励得分。

Conclusion: 以条件 KL 最小化为准则可唯一确定少步流 RL 的随机转移几何，配合梯度尺度匹配实现稳定优化；方法不改变少步调度与确定性推理，具备即插即用性，并可推广至图像与视频生成。

Abstract: Few-step flow models generate samples through a handful of large denoising transitions. Applying GRPO to such models requires introducing stochasticity into the denoising transitions, and when a single transition spans a wide range of noise levels, the choice of the resulting stochastic policy becomes a major factor in optimization. Recent constructions constrain the policy to preserve the target flow coefficients, but this constraint admits a family of policies and offers no criterion for choosing among them. We introduce Geo-FSRL (Geometry-Optimized Few-Step Reinforcement Learning), built on a selection principle: at a fixed conditioning state and timestep pair, choose the admissible policy that minimizes the conditional KL divergence induced by any given nonzero velocity-output update. Within a coefficient-preserving family of isotropic Gaussian transitions, this criterion reduces to minimizing a single scalar geometry factor that determines the conditional KL for every velocity-output update. The same factor governs velocity-space Fisher information and conditional transition log-likelihood-ratio variance. We derive the unique closed-form optimizer within this family, shared across all nonzero update magnitudes and directions, with transition coefficients determined by both the source and target noise levels. Because the minimized geometry factor still varies across timesteps, we further adopt gradient-scale matching from prior work and derive closed-form policy-gradient and KL weights that normalize explicit score scales and reference-KL curvature. Geo-FSRL trains on the original few-step schedule and retains the deterministic solver at inference. Our method achieves higher final reward scores than Flow-GRPO and Flow-CPS on both image generation and video generation models under the 4-step setting. Code is available at https://github.com/ModelTC/LightX2V/tree/main/lightx2v_rl

</details>


### [217] [Prune Gradually, Adapt Sparsely: Efficient Pruning of Large Language Models at Extreme Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41968)
*Jean Lucien Randrianantenaina,Hung Nguyen,Nasib Ullah,Jinbin Zhang,Nilesh Gupta,Inderjit S. Dhillon,Rohit Babbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ETAPS：渐进收紧全局稀疏约束并用固定小预算的稀疏增量训练，在无需模型级优化器状态下实现端到端掩码优化，在50%–90%稀疏度上优于ELSA且成本大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型权重剪枝可降低存储与推理开销，但高稀疏度下保持质量困难。现有方法存在质量—成本权衡：一次性剪枝成本低但高稀疏度下质量急剧下降，即使配合剪枝后恢复；直接以端到端目标优化掩码的方法（如SOTA的Lee et al., 2026 / ELSA）质量高但需模型规模的优化器状态与辅助变量，成本高昂。

Method: ETAPS采用渐进式剪枝与稀疏自适应：分阶段逐步收紧全局稀疏约束，同时以固定的小预算训练稀疏增量（sparse delta）。核心观察是渐进移除权重使单步剪枝幅度足够小，可由稀疏自适应补偿，从而使自适应后的模型能提供端到端梯度来指导下一步剪枝决策，而不需要模型规模的优化器状态。

Result: 在125M至13B共六个模型上，ETAPS在50%–90%所有评估稀疏度下困惑度均低于ELSA，最高降低42%。成本方面，90%稀疏度下在单张H200上剪枝LLaMA2-7B（ELSA需四张），持久状态总量小5.0倍，GPU小时数减少49%。

Conclusion: 渐进剪枝结合稀疏自适应可在极低成本的条件下实现高质量极端稀疏剪枝，为严苛稀疏场景提供了一条低成本高质量的新路径。

Abstract: Weight pruning can reduce the storage and inference cost of large language models (LLMs), but preserving quality at high sparsity levels remains challenging. Existing methods face a quality-cost trade-off in this regime. One-shot pruning is cheap but degrades sharply as sparsity increases, even when followed by post-pruning recovery. A growing line of work instead optimizes the mask directly from the dense model under the end-to-end objective; among these methods, [Lee et al.,2026], the recent state of the art at extreme sparsity, reaches high quality but at the cost of model-sized optimizer state and auxiliary variables. To address this gap, we propose ETAPS, which prunes gradually and adapts sparsely: it tightens a global sparsity constraint in progressive stages while training a sparse delta with a small, fixed budget. Our key observation is that gradual weight removal keeps each pruning step small enough for sparse adaptation to compensate, allowing the adapted model to provide end-to-end gradients that guide the next pruning decision without model-sized optimizer state. Across six models from 125M to 13B parameters, ETAPS achieves lower perplexity than ELSA at every evaluated sparsity from 50% to 90%, with perplexity reductions of up to 42%. ETAPS is also far cheaper: at 90% sparsity, it prunes LLaMA2-7B on a single H200, where ELSA uses four, with $5.0\times$ smaller total persistent state and 49% fewer GPU-hours. Together, our findings point to a promising direction for achieving high quality in demanding regimes such as extreme sparsity at substantially lower cost.

</details>


### [218] [When Does Test-Time Search Help World Action Models? An Empirical Audit of Candidate Selection and Simulator Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41970)
*Jun Liu,Dajie Sun,Zhenglun Kong,Zhenjiang Fan,Peiyan Dong,Fan Yang,Wenbin Zhangg,Geng Yuan,Xue Lin,Yanzhi Wang,Dong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文对世界-动作模型中的测试时缩放（test-time scaling）提出"前提链"审计框架，在 Cosmos Policy 与 RoboCasa 上检验决策信号、实际效率与评测完整性三大前提，发现选择信号近乎消失、剪枝加速远低于预期、自适应分配实验不具结论性，并定位并修复了一处静默渲染损坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型日益采用测试时缩放（候选选择、自适应算力分配）来提升控制性能，但这类收益只有在三个前提同时成立时才可信：候选间存在可利用的信号、名义算力节省能转化为真实加速、评测本身可靠。作者认为现有工作多以成功率单一指标汇报，缺乏对这些前提的独立审计，因此需要系统性地逐环验证。

Method: 采用"前提优先"（premise-first）与预注册（pre-registered）的方法论，在 Cosmos Policy 与 RoboCasa 上沿三条前提链开展审计：(1) 决策信号——对比学习价值选择器与随机选择，并构造后见之明（hindsight）oracle 上界，统计状态级候选结果一致性；(2) 实际效率——测量 token 剪枝带来的 FLOPs 削减与端到端实测加速比（含 decode-excluded 路径对照），并设置 1.5× 目标阈值；(3) 评测完整性——在算力密集的规划路径上排查静默渲染损坏，开发并发布检测器与修复方案；(4) 自适应分配实验以统计功效门控（power gate）判定是否具备结论性。

Result: 在干净数据上：学习价值选择器比随机选择差 1.66 pp；后见之明 oracle 仅提升 1.40 pp，且回合级置信区间跨越零；82% 的状态下候选结果完全相同，选择空间极小。token 剪枝使单次前向 FLOPs 最多下降 47%，但在部署路径上端到端实测加速至多 1.15×（排除 decode 的路径为 1.25×），未达到 1.5× 目标。自适应分配实验在功效门控下不具结论性。此外发现一处静默渲染损坏，其发生率随算力密集规划路径显著上升，但不影响官方 direct-policy 结果，并已发布检测器、修复补丁与脚本。

Conclusion: 测试时缩放应以"前提链"（决策信号、实际效率、评测完整性）逐环审计，而非仅报告成功率；任一环节失效都会使表面收益失真。本文结论严格限定于所测试的配置（Cosmos Policy、RoboCasa 及给定算力预算），不外推。

Abstract: World-action models increasingly apply test-time scaling, spending extra inference computation on candidate selection and adaptive allocation to improve control. Such gains are credible only when candidates carry exploitable signal, nominal compute savings become real speedup, and evaluation stays reliable. We audit all three on Cosmos Policy and RoboCasa with a premise-first, pre-registered methodology. On clean data, a learned value selector is 1.66 pp worse than random; a hindsight oracle gains only 1.40 pp, with an episode-level interval spanning zero; and 82% of states show identical candidate outcomes, leaving little selection headroom. Token pruning cuts per-forward FLOPs by up to 47% but yields at most a measured 1.15$\times$ end-to-end speedup on the deployed path (1.25$\times$ on a decode-excluded path), short of a 1.5$\times$ target, and our adaptive-allocation experiment is inconclusive under a power gate. We further identify and fix a silent rendering corruption whose rate rises sharply along compute-heavy planning paths while leaving the official direct-policy result unaffected, and release a detector, fix, and scripts. These findings show why test-time scaling should be audited as a chain of premises (decision signal, realized efficiency, evaluationintegrity) rather than by success rate alone. Conclusions are scoped to the tested configuration.

</details>


### [219] [ID2Pattern: Pretraining Generalizable Identity Behavior Patterns for Transaction Risk Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41988)
*Ze Zhao,Haoyu Jiang,Weiyu Han,Ruobing Zuo,Xiao Tan,Shuai Chen,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ID2Pattern预训练框架，将高基数身份字段（卡、设备、账户ID）的"身份行为模式"引入交易基础模型：先用LAID分词器以匿名方式刻画身份在序列中的出现与复现规律，再基于多尺度模式码本将LAID序列抽象为离散行为模式用于下游预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线支付系统中，卡、设备、账户等高基数身份字段蕴含丰富行为信号但利用不足。其词表持续增长且缺乏语义，导致基于词表与基于语义的编码方式均不适用；真正可泛化的信号不在具体ID取值，而在于其在交易序列中呈现的身份行为模式。

Method: 1）Local Anonymous ID（LAID）分词器：对身份字段编码，捕捉每个身份在序列内的出现方式与复现结构，不依赖具体取值，从而规避词表膨胀与语义缺失问题；2）多尺度模式码本：在无标签序列上预训练，将LAID序列抽象为离散行为模式，供下游预测使用。

Result: 在包含逾1亿条序列的工业数据集及六个公开数据集上，ID2Pattern均稳定优于强基线，工业场景平均提升4.13%，公开数据集平均提升8.83%；且在训练阶段所有身份取值均未见过时效果依然保持，显示出对新用户与新市场的泛化潜力。

Conclusion: 将高基数身份字段从"取值编码"转向"行为模式编码"是可行的预训练路径；LAID分词器与多尺度模式码本的组合在不依赖身份具体值的前提下实现可迁移表示，适用于冷启动与新市场拓展场景。

Abstract: Large-scale behavioral pretraining offers a promising route to generalizable representations in online payment systems. However, high-cardinality identity fields such as card, device, and account IDs, though rich in behavioral signals, remain underexploited. Their continually growing vocabularies and limited semantic meaning make existing vocabulary-based and semantics-based encodings ill-suited. Their generalizable signal lies not in specific values, but in the identity behavior patterns they exhibit across a transaction sequence. Building on this insight, we propose ID2Pattern, a pretraining framework that brings these patterns into transaction foundation models. ID2Pattern first encodes identity fields with a Local Anonymous ID (LAID) tokenizer, which captures how each identity appears and recurs within the sequence, independent of its specific value. It then pretrains a multi-scale pattern codebook on unlabeled sequences, which abstracts LAID sequences into discrete behavior patterns for downstream prediction. Experiments on an industrial dataset with over 100M sequences and six public datasets show that ID2Pattern consistently outperforms strong baselines, improving industrial by 4.13% and public datasets by 8.83% on average. Notably, ID2Pattern remains equally effective even when all identity values are unseen during training, highlighting its potential to generalize to new users and markets.

</details>


### [220] [TradeWar: A Benchmark for Execution-Grounded Trade Negotiation with Adaptive LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42004)
*Xiangyu Kong,Ruirui Yang,Xia Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TradeWar多国谈判仿真基准与TACTIC智能体，将谈判对话落地为关税/贸易份额政策并以经济健康指数EHI评估执行后收益，证明结算反馈驱动的跨回合适应优于单纯CoT与交互记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多轮谈判基准主要只评测"达成协议率"，忽视谈判承诺在执行后是否真正创造价值；LLM谈判智能体面临延迟执行反馈，仅靠自然语言对话能力不足以优化长期结果。

Method: 1) 构建TradeWar：多国仿真环境，把对话解析为具体关税与贸易份额政策，在结算阶段施加产能与贸易网络约束，并用经济健康指数（EHI）量化结果。2) 提出TACTIC智能体：基于贸易感知的自适应整合与心智理论，在不更新基础LLM权重的前提下，利用历史经验、对手行为与过往结算结果跨回合迭代优化谈判策略。3) 设置PromptLLM（零样本CoT）、MemoryLLM及去除跨回合更新的消融作为对照，并引入技术管制与能源危机冲击测试鲁棒性。

Result: PromptLLM达成率75%，但最终EHI仅57.1（×100）；TACTIC最终EHI达68.8。20个连续回合、3个随机种子下，TACTIC的EHI由58.2提升至73.2，而无跨回合更新的消融仅65.2。在技术管制与能源危机冲击下，TACTIC性能退化幅度小于PromptLLM与MemoryLLM。

Conclusion: CoT推理与交互记忆不足以应对延迟执行反馈；以结算结果为依据的自适应学习能显著改善TradeWar中的长程谈判表现，表明"执行落地评估+跨回合经验更新"是提升LLM谈判智能体的关键方向。

Abstract: Multi-turn negotiation remains a challenging problem for large language model (LLM) agents. While LLMs can conduct natural-language dialogue, existing benchmarks predominantly assess only agreement rates, overlooking whether negotiated commitments actually create value once executed. To address this gap, we introduce TradeWar, a multi-country simulation benchmark that translates negotiation dialogues into concrete tariff and trade-share policies, enforces capacity and network constraints during settlement, and evaluates outcomes via an Economic Health Index (EHI). To solve this execution-grounded task, we propose TACTIC (Trade-aware Adaptive Consolidation and Theory-of-mind for International Commerce), an agent that iteratively refines its negotiation strategy across episodes-without updating base LLM weights-by leveraging prior experience, opponent behavior, and past settlement outcomes. Despite using zero-shot chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting, PromptLLM attains a 75% agreement rate but only 57.1 final EHI (*100), whereas TACTIC reaches 68.8. Across 20 sequential episodes and three seeds, TACTIC's final EHI improves from 58.2 to 73.2, compared with 65.2 for an ablation without cross-episode updates. Under technology-controls and energy-crisis shocks, TACTIC also exhibits smaller performance degradation than PromptLLM and MemoryLLM. These results indicate that CoT deliberation and interaction memory alone do not fully address delayed execution feedback, whereas settlement-grounded adaptation improves long-horizon negotiation in TradeWar.

</details>


### [221] [Same Question, Different Answers: Auditing Persona-Dependent Variation in Six LLMs on Smoking-Cessation Advice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42039)
*Artin Yousefi,Aizierjiang Aiersilan,Lorien C Abroms,David A Broniatowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文审计了6个已部署LLM在戒烟建议上是否因用户画像不同而给出不同的事实覆盖，并测试了一种"事实底线"系统提示干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数亿人向LLM聊天机器人寻求健康建议，但"是否人人得到相同建议"这一问题缺乏实证；戒烟领域有明确临床指南且公共健康意义重大，适合作为审计场景。

Method: 对来自4家提供商的6个广泛部署的LLM，用4个戒烟问题×12个用户画像（在人生阶段、自述专业水平、吸烟史上不同），共获2,880条回复进行审计；随后评估一种可部署干预——用简短系统提示设定"事实底线"（每个用户应获得的核心医学事实集合），同时允许语气、词汇与框架自由适配。

Result: 模型输出依赖提问者身份：所有模型对青少年画像提及FDA批准的处方戒烟药物的比例比成人画像低32–67个百分点；6个模型中有5个对展现医疗专业知识的画像给出的事实少于无专业知识的画像。事实底线提示在所有模型中缩小了画像差异，但对总体事实覆盖率的影响在幅度和方向上因模型而异。

Conclusion: 提示在一个系统上验证后不能假定可迁移到另一系统。两点警示：平等提及率并非总是正确的临床目标（处方戒烟药物不推荐用于未成年人），可辩护的事实底线应随年龄调整而非统一；由于画像在多个属性上同时变化，报告的是场景级差异而非年龄或专业水平的孤立效应。作者公开全部2,880条回复与分析代码。

Abstract: Hundreds of millions of people now ask large language model (LLM) chatbots for health advice, which raises a question that is simple to pose and hard to answer: does everyone get the same advice? We audit six widely deployed LLMs from four providers on smoking cessation, a domain with codified clini- cal guidance and substantial public-health stakes, by putting the same four cessation questions to twelve user personas that differ in life stage, self-described expertise, and smoking history, for a total of 2,880 responses. What the models say depends on who appears to be asking. Every model named FDA-approved prescription cessation medications less often to teen personas than to adult personas, by 32 to 67 percentage points, and in five of six models a persona presenting health- care expertise received fewer facts than a persona presenting none. We then evaluate one deployable intervention: a short system prompt specifying a factual floor, the set of core med- ical facts every user should receive when they are relevant, while leaving tone, vocabulary, and framing free to adapt. The prompt narrows persona differences in every model, but its effect on overall fact coverage varies in both magnitude and direction across models, so a prompt validated on one system cannot be assumed to transfer to another. Two cautions frame these results. Equal mention rates are not always the correct clinical target, because prescription cessation pharmacother- apy is not recommended for minors, so a defensible factual floor must be age-aware rather than uniform. And because our personas vary on several attributes at once, we report scenario- level differences rather than isolated effects of age or expertise. We release all 2,880 responses and the analysis code.

</details>


### [222] [CausalWAM: Learning the Causes of Actions in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42096)
*Juntao Cheng,Jinzhou Tang,Fan Feng,Henrik I. Christensen,Yujia Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出CWAM（Causal World Action Model），通过稀疏路由约束使世界动作模型生成动作所用的隐变量仅依赖于动作相关隐变量，并证明该结构可识别至潜变量置换，在合成数据与真实机器人上验证理论与闭环效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）需对整幅场景做预测，但真正导致动作的只是场景中的一部分。仅靠示范数据拟合的WAM无法保证其生成动作时只使用"动作相关的潜变量"，而可能把动作相关潜变量与冗余（nuisance）变量纠缠在一起进行模仿。因此需要把"导致动作的因素"与"预测所依赖的因素"分离。

Method: 理论层面：在稀疏性约束下证明用于生成动作的潜变量是动作相关潜变量的可逆函数，且真实结构可识别至潜变量置换；该结论不要求充分的时间依赖性、不对未观测机制的演化作假设，即使动作相关潜变量与其余变量统计相关也成立。方法层面：提出CWAM，在预训练WAM的动作头上增加一条稀疏路由路径（sparse routing path）来注入该约束，世界建模目标保持不变。

Result: 合成数据实验验证了理论结论（可识别性），真实机器人实验表明闭环任务成功率/效率有所提升。

Conclusion: 通过在动作头引入稀疏路由实现因果解耦，可在不改动世界建模目标的前提下获得可识别的动作相关表示，从而提升闭环控制表现。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) learn latent variables from observations and use these to both predict the evolution of the scene and to generate actions. The prediction has to be of the whole scene, whereas only a portion of the scene causes actions. WAMs fitted to demonstrations do not, in general, guarantee that they will generate actions solely from the latent causes of the demonstrated actions (the action-relevant latent variables), as the model could just as well learn to imitate in this way whilst entangling the action-relevant latent variables with the nuisance variables. We therefore separate out what causes action from what prediction depends on. We prove that under a sparsity constraint the latent variables used to generate actions are an invertible function of the action-relevant latent variables alone and the true structure is identified up to a permutation of the latent variables. This requires neither sufficient temporal dependence nor any assumptions on how the unobserved regimes evolve and holds even when the action-relevant latent variables are statistically dependent on the rest. We therefore propose CWAM (Causal WAM), which incorporates the constraint into a pretrained WAM by adding a sparse routing path to its action head, with the world modeling objective remaining unchanged. Experiments on both synthetic data and real-world robots validate the theory as well as showcase the improved efficiency in closed-loop success.

</details>


### [223] [ACCT: Asymmetric Cross-LLM Context Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42116)
*Yuzhe Liu,Haolin Tian,Tonghan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ACCT，实现同族不同规模LLM间的KV cache稀疏转换与复用，在压缩75% KV时性能不降，长上下文下较目标模型prefill提速6.5–21.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构LLM部署中不同模型被动态调用，同一上下文被多个模型重复prefill，造成大量重复计算开销；需要把上下文计算与下游推理解耦并跨模型复用。

Method: ACCT面向同模型族不同规模模型间的KV cache迁移，将源模型KV转为目标模型可直接消费的稀疏KV状态。包含三个轻量组件：Cartographer为每个目标层与KV head识别与query相关的上下文token；Allocator在共享KV总预算下分配各层各head保留的token数；Compiler将选中的源KV转换为稀疏目标KV，使目标模型无需重处理原上下文。

Result: 在5个通用能力基准与6个强上下文依赖任务上评估：KV cache缩减75%时性能持平或超过目标模型原始推理；约19K token上下文下，KV转换相对目标模型上下文prefill加速6.5–21.2倍。

Conclusion: 验证了有限KV预算下跨模型迁移上下文计算的可行性，为异构部署中的上下文复用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: In real-world deployments, large language model (LLM) systems are increasingly evolving from single-model invocation toward heterogeneous deployments in which different models are dynamically invoked for inference. When the same context is reused across models, however, each newly selected target model typically has to process it again, repeatedly incurring substantial computational costs. Therefore, we propose ACCT (Asymmetric Cross-LLM Context Transfer), which targets KV cache transfer between models of different scales within the same model family. ACCT transforms the KV cache produced by a source model into sparse KV states that can be directly consumed by a target model, thereby enabling cross-model reuse of context computation and decoupling context computation from downstream inference. ACCT consists of three lightweight components: the Cartographer identifies query-relevant context tokens for each target layer and KV head; the Allocator determines how many context tokens each target layer and KV head retains under a shared total KV budget; and the Compiler transforms selected source KV into sparse target KV. The target model can therefore perform inference using the transferred KV without reprocessing the original context. We evaluate ACCT in two complementary settings comprising five general-capability benchmarks and six highly context-dependent tasks. Experiments show that ACCT matches or exceeds the target model's original inference performance even when reducing the KV cache size by 75%. At a context length of approximately 19K tokens, ACCT's KV conversion achieves a 6.5-21.2 times speedup over target context prefill. These results demonstrate the feasibility of transferring context computation across models under a limited KV budget.

</details>


### [224] [Latent Sparse Attention: Towards Subquadratic Ultra-Long Context Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42147)
*Ales Kubicek,Andrea Caciolai,Liangzu Peng,Sukrit Sumant,Benjamin Hoover,Fraser Greenlee,Ruairidh M. Battleday,Radim Urban,Torsten Hoefler,James C. R. Whittington*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LSA（Latent Sparse Attention）通过在学得的隐空间中对 key 层次聚类、让 query 对簇层次打分而非逐 key 打分，在超长上下文（>1M token）下兼顾精度与效率，并提供蒸馏版 D-LSA 与原生长训练版 N-LSA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic 工作流趋向长时程任务（如开放式科研），需超长上下文；但超过 1M token 时全注意力代价过高。现有稀疏注意力中的索引器要么在上下文规模上仍为二次复杂度，要么在超长尺度下精度下降。

Method: 将 key 在可学习的隐空间中层次聚类，query 只对簇层次结构打分（而非对每个 key 打分），从而降低索引开销并支持快速 prefill、decode 与训练。提出两种变体：D-LSA（通过蒸馏将预训练模型转为 LSA）与 N-LSA（仅用下一 token 预测损失原生训练）。

Result: 在 GLM-5.3-FLASH（QWEN-3.8-27B）上、10M token 时，D-LSA 使单层注意力相比原始稀疏注意力（全注意力）在 prefill 加速 22.7x（72.4x）、decode 加速 4.6x（42.5x），并在多类任务上保持相近精度。N-LSA 在 128k token 下精度与全注意力持平，训练每层加速 1.6x。

Conclusion: LSA 为模型利用超长上下文提供了实用且高效的注意力方案，在超长尺度同时取得显著加速与近似全注意力精度。

Abstract: Agentic workflows are increasingly shifting towards long-horizon tasks, such as open-ended scientific work, where models benefit from long context windows. However, at ultra-long context (above 1M tokens), full attention is prohibitively expensive. A promising approach is sparse attention, in which a dedicated indexer selects a subset of keys for the attention. Yet current methods of indexing either still have quadratic cost in context size or lose accuracy at ultra-long context scale. We address this with Latent Sparse Attention (LSA), an efficient attention method that maintains accuracy at ultra-long context. In LSA, keys are hierarchically clustered in a learned latent space, and queries score the hierarchy of clusters instead of scoring every key, making prefill, decode and training faster at long context. We propose two variants, Distilled-LSA (D-LSA), which can be applied to pre-trained models via distillation, and Native-LSA (N-LSA), which is trained natively on the next-token loss alone. On GLM-5.3-FLASH (QWEN-3.8-27B) at 10M tokens, D-LSA speeds up one attention layer by 22.7x (72.4x) in prefill and 4.6x (42.5x) in decode over its original sparse attention (full attention), while keeping similar accuracy across a variety of tasks. N-LSA matches the accuracy of full attention with a training speedup of 1.6x per layer at 128k tokens. LSA offers practical and efficient attention for models to benefit from ultra-long context.

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [225] [Joint Estimation and Network Configuration in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40826)
*Mohammadreza Barzegaran,Hamid Jafarkhani*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出采样“共识+新息”框架，在真实无线网络约束下推导最坏稳态估计误差的可计算上界（含图结构不一致、量化误差、传输时延与测量不确定性），并以该上界为网络设计目标，用半分布式拍卖算法联合优化通信图与链路速率分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际中通信网络、传输时延与有限速率通信会直接影响智能体间的不一致；而智能体达成一致并不保证估计准确，尤其当传感器测量不确定性不同时。现有工作未同时刻画“共识不一致”与“估计不准确”。

Method: 构建采样共识加新息（consensus-plus-innovations）框架；推导最坏稳态估计误差的可计算上界，涵盖通信图导致的不一致、量化误差、传输时延，以及相对真实状态的测量不确定性；将该上界作为网络设计目标，提出半分布式拍卖（auction）算法，联合修改通信拓扑与链路速率分配。

Result: 算法保持连通性，有限时间收敛到局部拍卖稳定（auction-stable）的网络配置，且每次被接受的变更都降低估计误差上界；在多个测试用例上验证，能在短时间内以更少链路和适中链路速率降低估计误差。

Conclusion: 将一致性误差与估计不准确统一纳入可计算误差上界，可实现兼顾链路数量、速率与精度的分布式感知网络设计。

Abstract: Distributed consensus-based sensing enables a multi-agent system to collaboratively estimate an unknown state by combining local measurements with information received from neighboring agents. In practice, however, the communication network, transmission delays, and finite-rate communication directly affect the disagreement among agents. On the other hand, agreement among agents does not ensure accuracy, particularly when sensors have different measurement uncertainties. We develop a sampled consensus-plus-innovations framework under realistic wireless network constraints that jointly accounts for inter-agent disagreement and estimation inaccuracy. We derive a computable upper bound on the worst-case steady-state estimation error that captures consensus disagreement due to the communication graph, quantization error, and transmission delay, together with estimation inaccuracy, which accounts for measurement uncertainty against the true state. We then use this bound as a network design objective and propose a semi-distributed auction-based algorithm that jointly modifies the communication graph and link-rate allocation. The proposed method preserves connectivity, converges in finite time to a locally auction-stable network configuration, and reduces the bound on the estimation error at every accepted change. The proposed method has been evaluated on various test cases, and the results show that it reduces the estimation error while maintaining fewer links and allocating moderate link rates in a short time.

</details>


### [226] [Maxwell-Consistent 3-D Magnetic Field Camera using Scalar-Potential-Based Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40856)
*Paul Jürß,Marija Boberg,Philip Suskin,Tobias Knopp,Fynn Foerger*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该文提出基于磁标量势及其球谐展开的磁场重建方法，用于86个三轴霍尔传感器组成的球形磁场相机；相比逐分量展开的基线，模型天然满足无源场麦克斯韦方程，重建精度更高、对噪声与传感器失效更鲁棒、非物理散度与旋度显著降低，且所需系数更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多种应用需要高空间与时间分辨率的精确三维磁场表征；此前的重建方法对每个矢量分量分别展开，无法保证重建场满足麦克斯韦方程（无散、无旋），可能引入非物理场结构并降低精度与鲁棒性。

Method: 在已有球形磁场相机（86个三轴霍尔传感器分布于球面）基础上，引入磁标量势并作球谐展开，由标量势梯度得到矢量磁场；与逐分量展开的基线方法对比，并以两个永磁体产生的模拟梯度磁场为测试场景，评估精度、抗噪与抗失效能力及散度/旋度误差。

Result: 所提方法重建精度更高；对传感器噪声和传感器失效更鲁棒；非物理散度与旋度大幅降低；在更少系数下即取得更优性能，并可支持更高阶多项式表示。

Conclusion: 基于标量势的球谐展开为球形磁场相机提供了物理一致、数据利用更高效的重建框架，在精度、鲁棒性与物理合理性上均优于逐分量基线，具备支持更高阶表征的潜力。

Abstract: Various applications require accurate 3-D characterization of magnetic fields with high spatial and temporal resolution. Building on our previously introduced spherical magnetic field camera employing 86 three-axis Hall sensors arranged on a spherical surface, we present a novel magnetic-field reconstruction approach based on the magnetic scalar potential and its spherical harmonic expansion. Unlike the previous reconstruction method that expands each vector component separately, our scalar-potential-based formulation is inherently consistent with Maxwell's equations for source-free fields by design, yielding curl-free and divergence-free field models. Using a simulated magnetic gradient field generated by two permanent magnets, we demonstrate that the proposed method achieves higher reconstruction accuracy, improved robustness to sensor noise and failure, and substantially reduced non-physical divergence and curl compared to the component-wise baseline. Furthermore, the scalar-potential approach attains superior performance with fewer coefficients, indicating a more efficient use of measurement data allowing higher-degree polynomial representations.

</details>


### [227] [Automated Optimization of Patient-Specific Electro-Anatomical Models Fitted to Cochlear Implant SCINSEV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41054)
*Minh Q. Vu,Erin L. Bratu,Jack H. Noble*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自动化优化流程，可在数分钟内完成患者特异性耳蜗电解剖模型（EAM）的拟合：基于术后CT构建四分区有限差分正向模型，用高斯过程贝叶斯优化拟合SCINSEV测量值，并通过三项降耗措施（粗网格+亚体素插值、互易性减半求解、雅可比可辨识性分析将四电阻率降至二）加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者特异性EAM对人工耳蜗编程中的听神经纤维仿真与神经健康评估至关重要，但将EAM拟合到刺激电流诱导的非刺激电极电压（SCINSEV）目前依赖专家手动调整或对预计算正向解的穷举搜索，均无法扩展到常规临床使用，存在显著计算瓶颈。

Method: 由术后CT导出四分区组织标签图（外淋巴、软组织、蜗轴、骨）构建有限差分正向模型，用高斯过程贝叶斯优化拟合实测SCINSEV。三项降耗：(1) 粗化网格并在亚体素触点位置做三线性注入与读取；(2) 仅求解一半刺激触点，利用互易性补全四分之三矩阵；(3) 基于雅可比的辨识性分析将四个电阻率降为两个。每项降耗分别以测量互易残差与拟合可重复性验证。

Result: 43只植入耳中，拟合中位耗时2.6分钟/耳。降分辨率目标下SCINSEV RMSE中位0.069 mV，优于同等评估预算下网格搜索的0.084 mV；与需中位9.1小时/耳的全分辨率穷举网格搜索相比，拟合误差统计上无显著差异，速度约快210倍。

Conclusion: 患者特异性耳蜗EAM拟合可在临床实用时间尺度上无需专家监督地完成，消除了患者特异性人工耳蜗编程与耳蜗仿真的一大计算障碍。

Abstract: Purpose: Patient-specific electro-anatomical models (EAMs) of the implanted cochlea are crucial for auditory nerve fiber simulation in cochlear implant (CI) programming and neural health estimation. Fitting an EAM to the stimulation current induced non-stimulating electrode voltage (SCINSEV) currently requires manual expert adjustment or exhaustive search over precomputed forward solutions, neither of which scales to routine clinical use. We present an automated optimization pipeline that fits patient specific EAMs in minutes. Methods: Four compartment tissue label maps (perilymph, soft tissue, modiolus, and bone) derived from postoperative CT were used to construct a finite difference forward model, fitted to the measured SCINSEV by Gaussian process Bayesian optimization. Three reductions cut fitting cost: a coarsened grid with trilinear injection and readout at sub-voxel contact positions; solving half the stimulating contacts and completing three quarters of the matrix by reciprocity; and a Jacobian-based identifiability analysis reducing four resistivities to two. Each reduction was validated separately against the measurement's reciprocity residual and the reproducibility of the fit. Results: Across 43 implanted ears, fitting took a median of 2.6 minutes per ear. Median SCINSEV RMSE on the reduced-resolution objective was 0.069 mV, against 0.084 mV for grid search at a matched evaluation budget. Against an exhaustive full-resolution grid search requiring a median of 9.1 hours per ear, the pipeline reached statistically indistinguishable fitting error roughly 210x faster. Conclusion: Patient-specific cochlear EAM fitting can be performed at clinically practical timescales without expert supervision, removing a major computational barrier to patient-specific cochlear implant programming and cochlear simulation.

</details>


### [228] [Prediction-Induced Sparsity for Tracked FMCW Radar: A Track-Conditioned Event-Gap FPGA Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41145)
*Huy Trinh*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出预测诱导稀疏性（PIS）跟踪模式架构，在事件形成与硬件调度之前利用跟踪先验，将预测拍频去旋转到新息中心坐标系并限定残差支撑，从而把跟踪不确定度转化为硬件容量收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跟踪式FMCW雷达本身已携带下一目标状态预测，但下游数字硬件通常仍按固定频谱搜索规模配置，未利用先验信息，造成资源与功耗浪费。

Method: 将预测拍频去旋转至以新息为中心的坐标系，并把跟踪器协方差传播为有界残差支撑K；以稀疏电平交叉事件包形式送入精确事件间隔谐振引擎，采用共享复数处理单元及K相关的状态/系数存储。

Result: 公开FMCW数据集上，因果先验条件化使事件记录平均减少33.1%，定点流在RTL中比特精确；Artix-7上将物理支撑从129降至23状态，LUT与BRAM分别降低65.0%和88.1%，匹配吞吐下VCCINT功耗实测降低19.9%。

Conclusion: 跟踪不确定度可转化为自适应雷达加速器的硬件容量约束，为事件驱动/自适应雷达硬件设计提供依据。

Abstract: Tracked FMCW radar already carries a prediction of the next target state, yet the downstream digital hardware is commonly provisioned for a fixed spectral search. We present prediction-induced sparsity (PIS), a tracked-mode architecture that uses the prior before event formation and hardware scheduling. The predicted beat is derotated to an innovation-centred coordinate, while tracker covariance is propagated to a bounded residual support K. Sparse level-crossing packets are then executed by an exact event-gap resonator engine with a shared complex processing element and K-dependent state/coefficient memory. On a public FMCW dataset, causal prior-only con- ditioning reduces event records by 33.1% on average, and the representative fixed-point stream is bit-exact in RTL. And on an Artix-7 implementation, reducing physical support from 129 to 23 states lowers LUT and BRAM use by 65.0% and 88.1%, respectively, and reduces measured VCCINT power by 19.9% at matched throughput. The results show how tracking uncertainty can become a hardware-capacity implication for future adaptive radar accelerators.

</details>


### [229] [Downlink MU-MIMO for LEO Direct-to-Device: Feasibility, Architecture, and Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41248)
*Bikshapathi Gouda,Neng Wang,Masoud Sajadieh,Meghna Agrawal*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 米级阵列单颗LEO卫星通过MU-MIMO预编码在同一时频资源上服务数百用户，空间自由度由口径波长数、轨道高度与最小仰角决定；MMSE预编码随SNR增长，几何计算预编码在3GPP NTN信道下几乎无损吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LEO直连设备（direct-to-device）商用系统吞吐受限：受限于卫星天线尺寸，且载荷仅以波束与频率复用区分用户。

Method: 提出单星米级阵列、基于多用户MIMO（MU-MIMO）预编码的载荷方案：由用户位置、卫星星历与姿态计算预编码；采用MMSE预编码并与MRT对比；用口径波长数、轨道高度、最小仰角推导空间自由度（DoF），DoF同时决定混合载荷的数字链路数；分析星地功能划分对星间链路/馈电链路负载与星上处理的权衡；几何预编码每无线帧（10 ms）更新，重度用户配对保持在数秒级；小区特定信号留于固定波束，用户数据跨所有小区联合预编码。

Result: MMSE预编码吞吐随SNR增长，而最大比发射（MRT）受干扰受限；在3GPP非地面信道模型、20 dB SNR下，基于几何的预编码相比完美信道知识吞吐损失小于0.5%；用户配对数秒级更新时吞吐损失低于1%；50×50阵列、600 km轨道、最小仰角60°时，单星服务410用户，平均6.1 bps/Hz，为四色频率复用固定波束吞吐的4倍以上。

Conclusion: 米级阵列LEO卫星经MU-MIMO预编码可显著提升同资源多用户复用与吞吐，几何信息驱动的预编码与低频更新的配对机制使方案在工程上可行，吞吐超过传统固定波束复用方案四倍以上。

Abstract: Direct-to-device connectivity from low Earth orbit (LEO) satellites is commercially available, but its throughput is limited by the satellite antenna size and by payloads that separate users only by beams and frequency reuse. This article shows that a single LEO satellite with a meter-scale array can serve hundreds of users on the same time-frequency resource through multi-user multiple-input multiple-output precoding. The number of users it can separate with a single stream each, i.e., its spatial degrees of freedom (DoF), follows from the aperture in wavelengths, the orbit altitude, and the minimum elevation angle. With minimum mean-square error (MMSE) precoding, the throughput of these users grows with the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), whereas maximum-ratio transmission becomes interference-limited. The precoder can be computed from the user positions and the satellite ephemeris and attitude, with a throughput loss of less than 0.5\% at 20~dB SNR compared with perfect channel knowledge under the 3GPP non-terrestrial channel models. The DoF also set the number of digital chains of a hybrid payload, and the functional split among satellites and ground trades inter-satellite-link and feeder-link load against on-board processing. As the satellite moves, the precoder can be updated from the known geometry every radio frame, i.e., 10~ms, while the computationally intensive user pairing can be kept for a few seconds with a throughput loss below 1\%. Finally, we keep the cell-specific signals on fixed beams while precoding the user data jointly across all cells of the satellite. For a $50\times50$ array at 600~km and a minimum elevation angle of $60^\circ$, one satellite serves 410 users at 6.1~bps/Hz on average, more than four times the throughput of fixed beams with four-color frequency reuse.

</details>


### [230] [Pinching Antennas for ISAC: Waveguide Architectures, Solutions, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41380)
*Qian Gao,Ruikang Zhong*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文综述了由“夹持天线系统(PASS)”赋能的通感一体化(ISAC)，按单波导、多波导、分段波导三类架构对比馈电结构、空间自由度、协议与上下行支持，并以分段波导SWAN案例验证联合段选择、天线定位与波束成形的强化学习方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISAC是6G核心设计原则，但固定天线几何结构限制了其性能；PASS通过介质波导引导电磁波并由位置自适应天线辐射，可提供空间重构能力，从而为通信链路与感知观测带来增益，因此需要系统性梳理PASS赋能的ISAC。

Method: 综述性研究：按单波导、多波导、分段波导三种架构进行对比分析（馈电结构、空间自由度、工作协议、上下行支持）；围绕物理一致性建模、联合空间-信号优化、动态信道获取、稳定实时控制四条技术路径组织解决方案；并开展SWAN案例研究，采用段滞回强化学习实现联合的段选择、天线定位与发射波束成形。

Result: 案例结果表明，波导损耗与逐段孔径增益之间存在耦合，形成了通信-感知性能的折中关系；强化学习框架能够在上述约束下联合优化段选择、天线位置与波束成形。

Conclusion: PASS为ISAC提供了新的空间重构维度，但需在物理一致建模、联合优化、信道获取与实时控制方面协同推进；未来研究方向包括联合收发操作、测量校准的数字孪生以及可信控制。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) is a core design principle for sixth-generation (6G) wireless systems, yet fixed antenna geometry limits its performance. Pinching antenna systems (PASS) guide electromagnetic waves along dielectric waveguides and radiate them through position-adaptive antennas, allowing communication links and sensing observations to benefit from spatial reconfiguration. This article reviews PASS-enabled ISAC through single-waveguide, multi-waveguide, and segmented-waveguide architectures, comparing their feed structures, spatial degrees of freedom, operating protocols, and uplink/downlink support. We organize solution pathways around physics-consistent modeling, joint spatial-signal optimization, dynamic channel acquisition, and stable real-time control. A segmented waveguide-enabled pinching-antenna array (SWAN) case study demonstrates joint segment selection, antenna po?sitioning, and transmit beamforming using segment-hysteresis reinforcement learning. The results illustrate how waveguide loss and per-segment aperture gain create communication-sensing tradeoffs. We conclude with research directions in joint transmit?receive operation, measurement-calibrated digital twins, and trustworthy control.

</details>


### [231] [Fluid Antenna Systems for ISAC Design: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41382)
*Yueling Zhao,Ping Yang,Hong Niu,Shuaixin Yang,Yue Xiao,Gang Wu,Merouane Debbah,Ertugrul Basar*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理流体天线系统（FAS）赋能通感一体化（ISAC）的研究进展，从理论性能极限到实际收发架构演进，并提出流体天线多模态调制（FA-MM）框架作为多维物理层设计案例，最后讨论预测性感知、自适应协调与空间状态安全管理等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ISAC受限于天线位置静态、离散所导致的几何刚性与功能冲突，FAS通过引入动态位置自由度（DoFs），将空间域从静态离散资源扩展为准连续、可重构的设计空间，为缓解上述矛盾提供有效手段，因此有必要对FAS-ISAC进行系统性综述。

Method: 采用综述性研究方法：沿"理论性能极限—实际收发机架构"的演进主线归纳FAS-ISAC研究；并以提出的流体天线多模态调制（FA-MM）框架作为多维物理层设计的代表性案例进行分析。

Result: 给出了FAS-ISAC的全面概览与设计演进脉络，并通过FA-MM框架展示了位置自由度与调制维度联合利用的可行物理层设计路径。

Conclusion: FAS的位置自由度可有效缓解ISAC的几何刚性与功能冲突；未来需重点关注预测性感知、自适应协调及安全的空间状态管理三大开放挑战。

Abstract: Fluid antenna systems (FAS) introduce dynamic positional degrees of freedom (DoFs), extending the spatial domain from a static and discrete resource to a quasi-continuous and reconfigurable design space. This spatial flexibility provides an effective means of alleviating the geometric rigidity and functional conflicts that constrain integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems. Against this backdrop, we provide a comprehensive overview of FAS-enabled ISAC, tracing the evolution from theoretical performance limits to practical transceiver architectures. To illustrate the surveyed design evolution, we further propose the fluid antenna multi-mode modulation (FA-MM) framework as a representative case study of multidimensional physical-layer design for FAS-ISAC. Finally, several open challenges are discussed, with emphasis on predictive sensing, adaptive coordination, and secure spatial-state management in future FAS-ISAC systems.

</details>


### [232] [PolarFlow: Spectral Geometric Flow Matching for Directed Graph Generation via Polar Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41471)
*Yanan Zhao,Feng Ji,Alba Carballo-Castro,Jingyang Dai,Antonio Ortega,Pascal Frossard,Wee Peng Tay*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: PolarFlow：基于极分解与截断谱坐标的连续几何流匹配框架，用于高效生成有向图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散扩散与流匹配方法以边级类别形式生成有向图，需对所有有序节点对做稠密预测并在生成中反复进行类别更新，计算开销大；且现有基准网络结构覆盖有限，难以全面评估。

Method: 提出基于极分解与截断谱坐标的连续几何流匹配。表示由四个耦合分量构成：编码正交因子的Stiefel框架与旋转角，以及刻画半正定因子的第二个Stiefel框架与非负特征值；在乘积空间的几何感知条件路径上学习联合速度场，重建连续邻接得分矩阵并离散化为二值有向图。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上，生成质量与竞争性离散方法相当，同时显著降低训练与采样时间；并引入Circuit与Twitter两个补充基准，覆盖工程功能网络与自然演化社交网络。

Conclusion: 以联合连续输运替代离散边级生成，在保留全局邻接结构的同时提升计算效率，为有向图生成提供了高效且可扩展的几何化建模范式。

Abstract: Directed graphs model asymmetric relationships in biology, transportation, social networks, and electronic design automation. Recent discrete diffusion and flow-matching methods generate directed graphs effectively, but their edge-wise categorical formulations require dense predictions over all ordered node pairs and repeated categorical updates during generation. This motivates a fundamental question: can comparable generative quality be achieved through a more computationally efficient formulation that better exploits the structure of directed adjacency matrices? Existing benchmarks also cover limited network structures, restricting comprehensive evaluation. We introduce PolarFlow, a continuous geometric flow-matching framework based on polar decomposition and truncated spectral coordinates. Its representation comprises four coupled components: a Stiefel frame and rotation angles encoding the orthogonal factor, and a second Stiefel frame and nonnegative eigenvalues characterizing the positive-semidefinite factor. PolarFlow learns a joint velocity field along geometry-aware conditional paths on this product space, reconstructs a continuous adjacency-score matrix, and discretizes it into a binary directed graph. By replacing discrete edge-wise generation with joint continuous transport, PolarFlow improves computational efficiency while retaining global adjacency structure. To broaden evaluation, we introduce complementary Circuit and Twitter benchmarks spanning engineered functional and naturally evolved social networks. Across synthetic and real-world datasets, PolarFlow achieves generative quality comparable to competitive discrete methods while substantially reducing training and sampling time.

</details>


### [233] [GNSS Receiver Operating Point Comparison Between Vector and Scalar Tracking Under High-Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41498)
*J. Fermín Llorente,Ernesto M. López,Javier A. Smidt,Pedro A. Roncagliolo*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 面向运载火箭用导航辅助GNSS接收机，提出一种基于C/N₀双层门控的量测噪声自适应机制，在低载噪比与高动态之间取得平衡，将跟踪失锁保持率提升一倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对20 g加速度阶跃，卡尔曼滤波载波跟踪环需采用宽带宽，但宽带宽导致低C/N₀下易受噪声驱动而失锁；高动态跟踪能力与噪声鲁棒性之间存在固有权衡。

Method: 以C/N₀为键构造Sigmoid包络门，并由环路带宽控制算法（LBCA）鉴相器输出统计量导出动态指示量进行调制，构成两层门控对量测噪声协方差R进行膨胀；噪声主导期膨胀R、降低卡尔曼增益，使滤波器依赖导航辅助预测滑行，检测到动态后一个历元内开启门控恢复高带宽整定。导航辅助被建模为随机控制输入，其不确定度由导航EKF协方差传播得到。

Result: 在20 g加速度阶跃仿真场景下，C/N₀=31 dB-Hz时，自适应矢量架构的失锁保持率为独立标量滤波器的两倍；接收机实现于开源MATLAB软件定义接收机MSGR。

Conclusion: 通过双层门控的量测噪声自适应，可在不牺牲动态响应速度的前提下显著提升低载噪比下的跟踪鲁棒性，验证了导航辅助与噪声自适应协同的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents a measurement noise adaptation mechanism for Kalman filter-based carrier tracking in a navigation-aided GNSS receiver designed for launch-vehicle applications. The tracking loop is tuned for 20\,g acceleration steps, which requires a wide bandwidth that makes the loop vulnerable to noise-driven pull-out at low carrier-to-noise density ratios. To resolve this tradeoff, the measurement noise covariance, $R$, is inflated via a two-layer gate: a sigmoid envelope keyed to the signal $C/N_0$, modulated by a dynamics indicator derived from the discriminator output statistics of the Loop Bandwidth Control Algorithm. During noise-dominated periods the gate inflates~$R$, reducing the Kalman gain so the filter coasts on the navigation-aided prediction; when dynamics are detected the gate opens within one epoch, restoring the high-bandwidth tuning. The navigation aiding is formulated as a stochastic control input whose uncertainty is propagated from the navigation EKF covariance. Simulation results on a 20\,g acceleration step scenario show that the adapted vector architecture doubles the lock-retention rate relative to the standalone scalar filter at $C/N_0 = 31$\,dB-Hz. The receiver is implemented in MSGR, an open-source MATLAB GNSS software-defined receiver.

</details>


### [234] [Group Physical-Layer Authentication and Confidential Beamforming in Non-Terrestrial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41579)
*Parsa Rajabi,Mohammad Reza Abedi,Paeiz Azmi,Nader Mokari*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该文研究高空平台站（HAPS）网络中群组物理层认证与保密波束赋形的联合设计，在认证、保密与单HAPS功率约束下最小化发射功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协调式HAPS网络需在共享功率约束下完成群组响应认证并保护隐私数据，现有工作缺乏对认证误差与保密波束赋形的联合刻画与优化。

Method: 在私有数据上叠加新鲜秘密标签；针对与当前标签无关的接收攻击，推导有限块长下虚警接受与合法拒绝的精确表达式，将认证误差目标转化为所需标签SINR；给出非零渐近标签能量地板，并分析残余频偏与峰值功率约束。合法接收机通过正交投影去除标签（损失一个时间维度）。对合谋窃听者，确定使AN在数据与AN功率同增时保持窃听SINR有界所需的空间覆盖条件。联合优化私有波束、标签波束与AN协方差，采用SDR、精确私有波束秩缩减与可行序列凸近似求解，并用修正对偶下界衡量最优性间隙。

Result: 标签长度由16增至64符号时，所需标签SINR降低7.16 dB；联合波束设计相比优化的固定标签方向基线平均发射功率降低27.4%。

Conclusion: 认证保证依赖于新鲜秘密标签，并排除对当前响应的中继转发。

Abstract: Coordinated high-altitude platform station (HAPS) networks need to authenticate group responses and protect private data under shared power constraints. This paper studies joint group physical-layer authentication (PLA) and confidential beamforming using a fresh secret tag superimposed on private data. For received attacks independent of the current tag, we derive exact finite-block expressions for false acceptance and legitimate rejection. These expressions translate authentication error targets into a required tag signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR). We establish a nonzero asymptotic tag-energy floor and analyze residual frequency offset and peak-power restrictions. The legitimate receiver removes the tag by orthogonal projection, which discards one temporal dimension. For colluding eavesdroppers, we identify the spatial coverage condition under which artificial noise (AN) keeps their SINR bounded as data and AN powers increase together. We jointly optimize the private beams, tag beam, and AN covariance to minimize transmit power under authentication, secrecy, and per-HAPS power constraints. The method combines semidefinite relaxation (SDR), exact private-beam rank reduction, and feasible successive convex approximation. A corrected dual lower bound measures the remaining optimality gap. Numerical results show that increasing the tag length from 16 to 64 symbols reduces the required tag SINR by 7.16 dB. Joint beam design reduces mean transmit power by 27.4% relative to an optimized fixed-tag-direction baseline. The authentication guarantee requires fresh secret tags and excludes relays of the current response.

</details>


### [235] [Learning Joint Synchronization, Channel Estimation, and Decoding: Comparison with a Finite-Blocklength Achievability Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41580)
*Bingcheng Chen,Giuseppe Durisi,Christian Häger*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文将TurboAE扩展到含未知时延与无记忆块衰落的SISO波形信道，采用无导频、模型引导的神经同步器实现联合同步、信道估计与译码，并与5G导频辅助基线及有限块长可达界比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短包通信中联合同步与译码的学习型方案已展现潜力，但其性能与信息论界的差距尚不明确，需在具备有限块长可达界参照的信道模型下进行量化评估。

Method: 在Dörner等(2023)TurboAE基础上扩展至含未知时延和无记忆块衰落的SISO波形信道；引入无导频、模型引导的神经同步器，联合完成同步、信道估计和译码；在块长64复符号、码率1 bit/信道使用下，与LDPC、极化码(SCL, list=8)等导频辅助5G基线对比BER/BLER，并对照导频辅助可达界。

Result: BER=10⁻³时，TurboAE较LDPC基线增益1.9 dB、较极化码SCL基线增益1.7 dB；BLER=10⁻³时优于LDPC基线但落后极化码基线约0.8 dB；所有方案距导频辅助可达界至少2.1 dB。

Conclusion: 无需导频的联合学习方案在短包BER上已超越5G基线，但BLER与有限块长界之间仍有显著差距，说明设计仍有较大改进空间。

Abstract: Learned schemes that jointly perform tasks such as synchronization and decoding have achieved promising results for short-packet communication. However, it remains unclear how close their performance is to information-theoretic bounds. To investigate this question, we extend the turbo autoencoder (TurboAE) proposed in Dörner et al. (2023) to a single-input single-output waveform channel with an unknown delay and memoryless block fading, for which an achievability bound from finite-blocklength information theory is available. Our extension incorporates a pilot-free, model-guided neural synchronizer and enables joint synchronization, channel estimation, and decoding. At a blocklength of $64$ complex symbols and a rate of one bit per channel use, the proposed TurboAE achieves a lower bit error rate (BER) than all evaluated pilot-assisted 5G baselines, with gains of $1.9$ dB at a BER of $10^{-3}$ over a baseline involving a low-density parity-check (LDPC) code and $1.7$ dB over a baseline involving a polar code and successive cancellation list decoding (list size $8$). In terms of block error rate (BLER), the TurboAE outperforms the LDPC-coded baseline, but lags behind the polar-coded baseline by about $0.8$ dB at a BLER of $10^{-3}$. Moreover, all evaluated schemes remain at least $2.1$ dB away from a pilot-assisted achievability bound, indicating substantial room for improved designs.

</details>


### [236] [Dynamic-Aware LC-DMA Beam Tracking for LEO Satellite Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41612)
*Mohamadreza Delbari,Mohammad Zarei,Denis Giniatoulline,Vahid Jamali*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出动态感知波束跟踪框架，显式建模LC-DMA的有限响应时间与LEO链路波束时变方向两种耦合动态，在预测跟踪时域内最大化累积接收SNR，仿真表明LC-DMA可支撑实际移动性场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LC-DMA是LEO卫星通信中高功率效率、低成本、可扩展的方案，但波束跟踪受两种耦合动态约束：用户与卫星移动导致的波束方向时变，以及LC移相器有限响应时间。因此需回答LC-DMA能否足够快地重构以支撑高移动性LEO链路。

Method: 在预测跟踪时域内构建最大化累积接收SNR的优化问题，约束包括LC单元物理动态与发射功率；采用交替优化框架求解该非凸问题，其中数字预编码用闭式MRT、接收端用MRC，相位偏移采用并行闭式动态感知更新。

Result: 仿真显示，当正确考虑LC-DMA时间响应时，其可支撑实际移动性场景；所提动态感知算法保持稳定SNR并优于传统设计，在高车载速度与低轨道方位角下优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: LC-DMA结合动态感知波束跟踪设计可满足高移动性LEO卫星链路需求，需显式纳入器件响应动态进行优化。

Abstract: Liquid crystal (LC)-based dynamic metasurface antennas (DMAs) offer a power-efficient, low-cost, and scalable solution for low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite communications where large apertures are needed to compensate for large path loss in the Earth-satellite link. However, beam tracking with LC-DMAs is governed by two coupled dynamics: the required time-varying beam direction caused by user and satellite mobility, and the finite response time of the LC phase shifters. This raises the question of whether LC-DMAs can reconfigure sufficiently fast to support high-mobility LEO satellite links. To address this problem, we develop a dynamic-aware beam-tracking framework that explicitly accounts for both dynamics. In particular, we formulate an optimization problem to maximize the cumulative received signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) over a predictive tracking horizon subject to the physical dynamics of the LC unit cells as well as transmit power constraints. To solve this non-convex problem efficiently, we develop an alternating optimization framework combining a closed-form maximum ratio transmission (MRT) digital precoder and maximum ratio combining (MRC) with a parallel, closed-form dynamic-aware phase-shift update. Simulation results show that when the time response of LC-DMAs is properly accounted for, it can support practical mobility scenarios. Moreover, the proposed dynamic-aware algorithm maintains stable SNR and outperforms conventional designs, particularly at high vehicle velocities and low orbital azimuth angles.

</details>


### [237] [Limitation of Bessel Beams in Near Field Communications: A Depth of Field Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41656)
*Aditya Jolly,Vitaly Petrov,Gábor Fodor,Emil Björnson*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文从景深视角解析证明有限孔径Bessel波束的无衍射传播范围存在上限，圆形与方形孔径下最大可用距离分别约为Fraunhofer距离的22%和19%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bessel波束因无衍射与自愈特性被视为辐射近场通信中聚焦波束的潜在补充，但已有工作表明有限孔径Bessel波束的实用范围仅占辐射近场区的一部分，其有限传播极限尚缺乏解析刻画。

Method: 针对有限圆形与方形孔径生成的Bessel波束，推导归一化阵列增益的解析表达式，并据此建立绝对与峰值归一化的3 dB景深判据；由判据导出临界锥角阈值，将Bessel波束配置空间划分为景深未定义、有限与无限三个区域。

Result: 在所提景深判据下，最大可用无衍射距离限于Fraunhofer距离的约22%（圆形孔径）与约19%（方形孔径）；数值结果验证了离散平面阵列下所得极限的准确性。

Conclusion: 研究从理论上确立了有限孔径Bessel波束的传播距离上限，并为未来近场通信中有限孔径Bessel波束设计提供了工程指导准则。

Abstract: Bessel beams have recently been explored as a candidate complement to focused beams in radiative near-field communications due to their non-diffracting and self-healing properties. However, recent work has shown that the practically useful range of finite-aperture Bessel beams can occupy only a fraction of the radiative near-field region, establishing the existence of a finite propagation limit. In this paper, we establish such a limitation analytically from a depth-of-field perspective for Bessel beams generated by finite circular and square apertures. To quantify the Bessel beam depth, we first derive analytical expressions for the normalized array gain and use them to establish absolute and peak-normalized $3$-dB depth-of-field criteria. These criteria reveal critical cone-angle thresholds that partition the Bessel-beam configuration space into (i) undefined, (ii) finite, and (iii) infinite depth-of-field regimes. Our analysis further reveals that the maximum usable non-diffracting range under the proposed depth-of-field criterion is limited to $\approx22\%$ and $\approx19\%$ of the Fraunhofer distance for circular and square apertures, respectively. Numerical results confirm the accuracy of the derived limits for discrete planar arrays, providing practical engineering guidelines for the design of finite-aperture Bessel beams in future near-field communications.

</details>


### [238] [RFLocMatch: Reference-Conditioned Residual Learning from Angle-Delay Spectra for Accurate Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41757)
*Leihao Zhao,Xiucheng Wang,Junxi Huang,Conghao Zhou,Yuxiang Zhang,Huixin Xu,Nan Cheng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: RFLocMatch 通过联合匹配与链路特定匹配互补生成候选，再用参考条件残差学习与候选上下文建模对候选打分，结合尾误差聚焦训练和验证校准的替换阈值，缓解无线指纹定位中的空间模糊与测量扰动导致的尾误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线电指纹定位可在卫星定位不可靠时支撑移动机器人与位置感知服务。角度-时延功率指纹易受空间模糊与测量扰动影响：远处位置可能因反射与遮挡产生相似指纹；查询指纹噪声会改变查询-参考排序，即使参考库干净，也可能让远处参考排到首位，造成尾误差。相似度分数只能产生候选，可靠选择需要候选特定的残差证据与候选间比较。

Method: 提出 RFLocMatch：(1) 用联合匹配与链路特定匹配互补，保留由不同链路支持的候选位置；(2) 参考条件残差学习，以查询指纹、参考指纹、二者绝对差及逐元素乘积为输入为每个候选打分；(3) 候选上下文建模实现候选间比较；(4) 尾误差聚焦训练强调高代价错误；(5) 推理时仅当候选分数优势超过验证集校准阈值才替换初始匹配。

Result: 在射线追踪生成数据上，链路与接收机扰动下，双发射机 99 分位误差相比最佳基线降低 75.2%。在真实单发射机 CIR 测量并结合运动辅助角度谱估计时，达到 8.25 m 的 90 分位误差与 3.83 m 的平均绝对误差。

Conclusion: 将候选生成（互补匹配）与候选选择（残差证据打分、上下文比较、尾误差聚焦训练、校准阈值门控）解耦，可有效抑制空间模糊与查询扰动带来的尾误差，并在仿真与实测中均取得优于基线的定位精度。

Abstract: Radio fingerprint localization supports mobile robots and location-aware services when satellite positioning is unreliable. Angle-delay power fingerprints capture power distributions over angles and delays, but localization remains vulnerable to spatial ambiguity and measurement perturbations. Reflections and blockage can produce similar fingerprints at distant locations, while noise in query fingerprints can alter query-reference rankings despite a clean reference library. These effects can place a distant reference first in the ranking, creating tail errors. Similarity scores can generate plausible candidates, but reliable selection requires candidate-specific residual evidence and comparison across alternatives. This separation motivates RFLocMatch, which uses complementary joint and link-specific matching to preserve candidate locations supported by different links. Reference-conditioned residual learning then scores each candidate using the query and reference fingerprints, their absolute difference, and their element-wise product, while candidate-context modeling compares alternatives. Tail-focused training emphasizes costly errors, and inference replaces the initial match only when a candidate's score advantage exceeds a validation-calibrated threshold. On ray-tracing-generated data, RFLocMatch reduces dual-transmitter 99th-percentile error by 75.2% under link and receiver perturbations relative to the best-performing baseline. On real-world single-transmitter channel impulse response (CIR) measurements with motion-assisted angular-spectrum estimation, it attains 8.25-m 90th-percentile error and 3.83-m mean absolute error.

</details>


### [239] [RSS-Based Verification of Drone Remote ID Position Claims with Unknown Transmit Power](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41783)
*Cole Dickerson,Ismail Guvenc,Wahab Khawaja,Ali Cafer Gurbuz*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对Remote ID位置可被伪造的问题，在发射功率与路损指数未知条件下，提出基于RSS的Rao得分检验，其零分布精确为卡方，检测概率与门限有闭式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广播式Remote ID上报位置为自证信息，可用低成本硬件伪造，需由接收端利用接收信号强度进行独立校验；而实际中发射功率和路损指数均未知，构成复合假设检验难题。
