Method: 将未知链路参数视为冗余参数，推导Rao得分检验；利用其对任意包数均精确服从卡方分布的性质，给出闭式门限与检测概率；进而推导任意对未知链路参数不变的检测器检测概率上界，并据此给出最差方位角、所需观测长度与接收机布设规则的闭式解。

Result: 所提检验在低虚警率下显著优于“先估计再门限”的检测器；检测概率上界随伪造位置沿固定方位远离而饱和，表明足够大的位置伪造可能无法被检测。

Conclusion: RSS校验可有效检测Remote ID位置伪造，但存在原理性可检测性极限；通过最差方位角、观测长度与接收机布设规则的设计可缓解该极限。

Abstract: Broadcast Remote Identification (Remote ID) is a key enabler for integrating unmanned aircraft into the airspace. However, the position it reports is self-declared and can be falsified with low-cost hardware. In this paper, we study how Remote ID receivers can verify the declared position from the received signal strength (RSS) when neither the transmit power nor the path-loss exponent is known. In particular, we derive a Rao score test whose null distribution is exactly chi-squared for any number of packets. Its threshold and detection probability therefore follow in closed form. We then bound the detection probability of any detector that is invariant to the unknown link parameters. This bound saturates as the falsified position recedes along a fixed bearing, so a sufficiently large falsification can remain undetectable. From this limit, we obtain the worst-case bearing in closed form, the observation length needed to close it, and a receiver placement rule. Simulation results show that the proposed test considerably outperforms an estimate-then-threshold detector at low false-alarm rates.

</details>


### [240] [RadioTraj3D: Height-Aware Adaptive Sampling for 3D Radio-Map Reconstruction from Ultra-Sparse Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41786)
*Zixuan Guo,Xiucheng Wang,Wenshuo Chen,Conghao Zhou,Chuan Huang,Ruijin Sun,Nan Cheng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: RadioTraj3D：利用建筑高度信息协调测量选择与逐层重建，从AAV轨迹测量中构建三维无线电地图，在仿真与真实数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中网络需要在未访问的位置与高度上获知无线传播信息，以进行航路规划、链路选择和资源分配。3D无线电地图（3D RM）可支持飞行前决策，但仅沿AAV轨迹可获得无线电测量，而目标覆盖整个3D服务体积。现有方法采用固定轨迹，忽略建筑分布与高度对电磁传播的影响，遗漏了对RM重建最具信息量的高度。

Method: 提出RadioTraj3D：利用建筑高度信息协调测量选择与逐层重建。建筑高度变化揭示传播敏感层，引导高度自适应采样；提出G-METS在信道采集前选择几何敏感高度，并在边缘、走廊、开阔区域间分配精确预算；采用双视图重建器，通过每层一个有界系数融合局部三维边界与全高度上下文。

Result: 在仿真室外与室内地图上，跨环境与不同垂直网格均实现SOTA重建性能。在LACN数据集的真实AAV测量上，相比Completion AE，PSNR提升0.84 dB，归一化均方误差降低17.5%。

Conclusion: 结合建筑几何先验的高度自适应采样与逐层双视图重建，可有效弥补轨迹稀疏测量与三维体覆盖之间的差距，显著提升3D无线电地图重建质量。

Abstract: Aerial networks require radio propagation information at unvisited positions and heights to plan routes, select links, and allocate wireless resources. A three-dimensional radio map (3D RM) represents propagation conditions over the service area, enabling these decisions before flight. However, constructing such a map from autonomous aerial vehicle (AAV) measurements is challenging, since radio measurements are available only along the trajectory, whereas the target covers the 3D service volume. Existing methods use fixed AAV trajectories that ignore how building distributions and heights affect electromagnetic propagation, missing the heights most informative for RM reconstruction. To address these issues, we propose RadioTraj3D, which uses building-height information to coordinate measurement selection and layer-wise reconstruction. Building-height variations reveal propagation-sensitive layers that guide height-adaptive sampling. We therefore propose G-METS to select geometry-sensitive heights and allocate an exact budget across edge, corridor, and open regions before channel acquisition. A dual-view reconstructor then integrates local three-dimensional boundaries with full-height context through one bounded coefficient per layer. Simulated outdoor and indoor maps demonstrate state-of-the-art reconstruction across environments and vertical grids. On real-world AAV measurements from the LACN dataset, RadioTraj3D improves peak signal-to-noise ratio by 0.84 dB and reduces normalized mean squared error by 17.5% over Completion AE.

</details>


### [241] [Improving Spectral Resolution via Simultaneous Extracochlear and Apical Ground Contacts: A Neural Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41791)
*Jared A. Rybarczyk,Erin L. Bratu,Robert F. Labadie,Jack H. Noble*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该回顾性研究基于6例人工耳蜗患者构建个体化容积传导模型与听神经纤维房室模型，比较4种接地配置（含联合接地）下的激活阈值与纤维比例标准差，发现联合使用蜗尖与耳蜗外接地可降低通道交互、提升频谱分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工耳蜗虽能提供良好言语感知，但通道交互导致噪声下言语识别持续受限。近期将返回电极置于蜗孔（helicotrema）的蜗尖接地在临床上可行，为新型刺激方案提供了可能，因此需明确蜗尖与耳蜗外接地共同使用时对听神经纤维动力学的影响及其改善频谱分辨率的潜力。

Method: 回顾性纳入6例人工耳蜗患者；构建包含患者特异性电解剖特征的容积传导模型，并结合定义神经动力学的听神经纤维（ANF）房室模型，为每个建模耳蜗内75个均匀分布的纤维束、4种接地配置（含联合接地）计算激活阈值；以阈值偏移与纤维比例标准差（反映通道交互）进行患者内比较。

Result: 全部6例患者中，联合接地模型的ANF激活阈值发生偏移，提示每个触点产生更特异的纤维激活；与标准耳蜗外接地或蜗尖接地相比，联合接地模型在刺激阵列的多个触点上纤维比例标准差降低。

Conclusion: 激活阈值偏移与标准差降低表明，联合使用耳蜗外接地与蜗尖接地的刺激方案可减少通道交互并在阵列特定触点上增强频谱分辨率，为改进人工耳蜗噪声下言语识别提供建模依据。

Abstract: Objectives: While cochlear implants (CI) can provide excellent speech perception outcomes, speech recognition in noise remains a consistent problem for many patients due to channel interaction. Recent work shows the clinical feasibility of an apical return contact placed in the cochlear helicotrema, which presents new possibilities in terms of experimental stimulation schemes. This study explores the interactions between apical and extracochlear ground contacts, aiming to characterize the effects of dual ground usage on auditory nerve fiber (ANF) dynamics and determine their utility in improving spectral resolution. Design: 6 patients with CIs were used in this retrospective study. Volume conduction models encompassing patient specific electro-anatomical characteristics and ANF compartment models defining neural dynamics were used to generate activation thresholds for 75 evenly spaced fiber bundles throughout each modeled cochlea and for 4 different grounding configurations, including the use of combined grounds. We compare our intra-patient models in terms of threshold shifts and standard deviation of fiber proportions to determine the effects of ground configuration used on channel interaction. Results: For all 6 patients, we observe shifts in ANF activation thresholds for the combined ground models indicative of more specific fiber stimulation per contact. Additionally, our combined ground models exhibit reduced fiber proportion standard deviation for several contacts of the stimulating array when compared to the standard extracochlear ground configuration or apical ground configuration. Conclusions: The shifts in activation thresholds and reduction in standard deviations suggest that stimulation schemes utilizing extracochlear and apical grounds in conjunction can provide reduced channel interaction and enhanced spectral resolution for certain contacts in an array.

</details>


### [242] [B-ExposNet: Bayesian Deep Learning for EMF Exposure Mapping with Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41913)
*Yukun Liu,Shanshan Wang,Paul Lagouanelle,Yarui Zhang,Joe Wiart*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出B-ExposNet，一个基于贝叶斯神经网络的城市电磁场空间暴露估计方法，可给出不确定性量化的概率预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市电磁场暴露高度变化，传统暴露地图仅给出确定性估计而忽略不确定性，缺乏对估计不确定性的刻画。

Method: 在ExposNet基础上，将其回归头权重视为随机变量，通过变分推断进行后验近似，由后验预测分布输出概率性估计；在法国三座城市的路测测量数据上评估，并与通用克里金等传统方法在预测精度与不确定性质量两方面对比。

Result: 在测试区域上，B-ExposNet的MAPE为28.19%，CRPS为0.198 V/m，在精度与不确定性量化两方面均优于常规方法。

Conclusion: 将贝叶斯推断引入深度暴露回归模型可有效提供不确定性感知的空间暴露估计，性能优于通用克里金等经典地统计方法。

Abstract: Electromagnetic field exposure in urban environments is highly variable, and conventional exposure maps provide a deterministic estimate, without considering its uncertainty. To address the gap, this paper proposes B-ExposNet, a Bayesian neural network for spatial exposure estimation with uncertainty quantification. B-ExposNet extends ExposNet by treating the weights of regression head as random variables, inferred by variational inference, so that the model provides probabilistic estimates from posterior predictive distribution. The method is evaluated on drive-test measurement campaigns conducted in three French cities, and compared with conventional method such as universal Kriging, in both predictive accuracy and the quality of the estimated uncertainty. On the test region, B-ExposNet achieves a mean absolute percentage error of 28.19\% and a continuous ranked probability score of 0.198~V/m, improving on canonical methods in both accuracy and uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [243] [Grid-Refinement SBL for AFDM Channel Estimation Under Fractional Delay and Fractional Doppler Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41997)
*Qu Luo,Limin Liu,Zhe Li,Qihao Peng,Haowei Wu,Pei Xiao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究分数时延与分数多普勒（FDFD）信道下AFDM系统的信道估计，提出精确FDFD信道模型与两种SBL算法（FLA-GR-SBL、2D-GA-SBL）以实现高精度时延-多普勒估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AFDM系统在FDFD信道下存在时延-多普勒耦合，现有on-grid与off-grid SBL方法建模不精确，估计精度受限。

Method: 先建立精确FDFD信道模型并推导输入-输出关系；针对时延-多普勒耦合，提出一阶线性近似网格细化稀疏贝叶斯学习（FLA-GR-SBL）；为进一步解决一阶近似精度不足导致的收敛与稳定性问题，提出二维网格近似SBL（2D-GA-SBL），迭代细化时延-多普勒网格。

Result: 仿真表明，所提算法在归一化均方误差（NMSE）和误码率（BER）上均显著优于现有on-grid与off-grid SBL基准方法。

Conclusion: 通过精确FDFD建模与二维网格迭代细化，2D-GA-SBL可更准确刻画信道，且相较一阶近似方法具有更好的收敛性与稳定性。

Abstract: This paper investigates channel estimation for affine frequency division multiplexing (AFDM) systems over channels with fractional delay and fractional Doppler (FDFD) shifts. An exact FDFD channel model is first established, and the corresponding input--output relationship is also derived. To address the FDFD-induced delay--Doppler coupling, a first-order linear approximation grid-refinement sparse Bayesian learning (FLA-GR-SBL) method is developed. To further remedy the convergence and stability issues caused by the limited accuracy of the first-order approximation, we propose a two-dimensional grid-approximation SBL (2D-GA-SBL) algorithm, which iteratively refines the delay--Doppler grid and thereby characterizes the channel more accurately. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed algorithms significantly outperform existing on-grid and off-grid SBL benchmarks in terms of both normalized mean square error (NMSE) and bit error rate (BER).

</details>


### [244] [Embodied Movable Antennas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42078)
*Yewen Cao,Dengke Wei,Yulin Shao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出"具身可移动天线"(embodied MA)概念：不依赖专用天线驱动器，而复用物理智能体自身的机械自由度来重构天线位置与姿态；以冗余机械臂为例，证明任务保持型自运动可在不改变既定物理任务的前提下重构无线信道，并将机械冗余量化为一种新的信道重构资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可移动天线(MA)依赖额外的专用天线驱动装置，带来硬件复杂度、成本与能耗开销。作者思考：天线运动性的来源是否可以不必是"专用驱动器"。

Method: 1) 将MA分为两类：任务耦合型(task-coupled)与任务保持型(task-preserving)，重点研究后者；2) 以冗余机械臂为典型实现，刻画任务保持型自运动所固有的"平动-转动耦合"；3) 推导由此产生的多径信道的精确Fourier表示；4) 基于分析结论设计跨任务的天线安装方案，实现固定物理安装下的无线适配；5) 数值仿真验证。

Result: 分析表明，具身移动性的无线价值不仅取决于天线可移动的距离，更取决于物理任务下仍可行的构型集合，以及这种耦合运动与极化、多径传播的相互作用。数值结果显示，通过任务保持型运动可获得显著的通信增益；跨任务安装设计可在固定安装条件下实现无线适配。

Conclusion: 机械冗余可作为一种可量化的新型无线信道重构资源，拓展了MA的设计范式，将天线移动性与机器人本体运动学统一起来。

Abstract: This paper introduces embodied movable antennas (MAs), a new MA architecture that rethinks the source of antenna mobility. Unlike conventional MAs that rely on dedicated antenna actuators, embodied MAs harness the mechanical degrees of freedom already present in physical agents to reconfigure antenna position and orientation. We distinguish two modes of embodied MAs: task-coupled and task-preserving. Focusing on the latter, we show how wireless channels can be reconfigured by exploiting task-equivalent host configurations without altering the prescribed physical task. Using a redundant robotic manipulator as a canonical realization, we characterize the intrinsically coupled antenna translation and orientation induced by task-preserving self-motion and derive an exact Fourier representation of the resulting multipath channel. Our analysis reveals that the wireless value of embodied mobility depends not merely on how far an antenna can move, but on which configurations remain feasible under the physical task and how their coupled motion interacts with polarization and multipath propagation. Building on this insight, we develop a cross-task antenna mounting design that enables wireless adaptation with a fixed physical installation. Numerical results demonstrate substantial communication gains through task-preserving motion. These findings establish mechanical redundancy as a new, quantifiable resource for wireless channel reconfiguration.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [245] [Metadevices that compute: mathematical foundations and experimental implementations for neuromorphic nanophotonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40903)
*Xian Hadia,Odysseas Tsilipakos,Dimitrios C. Tzarouchis,Raphaël Pestourie*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该综述聚焦波基模拟计算，系统梳理线性算符与波散射的映射、实现方程求逆的反馈架构、逆设计与科学机器学习工具链，并将这些数学基础与自由空间衍射处理器到可重构导波超结构的实验进展联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向特定线性代数与机器学习负载，数字电子面临能效与带宽瓶颈；波基模拟计算有望实现大规模并行、高带宽、高能效的信息处理，但相关数学基础、设计方法与实验成果分散，亟需系统整合。

Method: 采用聚焦式综述方法，从三方面进行归纳：线性算符到波散射的映射关系；实现方程求逆的反馈架构；使器件综合与训练可行的逆设计与科学机器学习工具链。并对照分类实验演示。

Result: 将超材料、超表面与集成超结构（统称超器件）在波域中实现的数学运算、积分与微分方程求解、矩阵乘法及可训练光子神经网络，与相应的数学框架和设计工具链建立了系统对应，并梳理了从自由空间衍射处理器到可重构导波超结构的实验进展。

Conclusion: 波基模拟计算已形成涵盖理论映射、反馈架构、设计工具与实验验证的完整图景，并指向“神经形态纳米光子学”生态中的开放问题与未来机遇。

Abstract: Wave-based analog computing has re-emerged as a compelling alternative to digital electronics for specific linear-algebraic and machine-learning workloads, promising massively parallel, high-bandwidth, and energy-efficient information processing. Metamaterials, metasurfaces, and integrated metastructures (collectively, metadevices) now perform mathematical operations, solve integral and differential equations, multiply matrices, and implement trainable photonic neural networks directly in the wave domain. This focused review consolidates the mathematical foundations that underpin such devices, i.e., the mapping between linear operators and wave scattering, the feedback architectures that realize equation inversion, and the inverse-design and scientific machine-learning toolchains that make their synthesis and training tractable, and connects them to the rapidly growing body of experimental demonstrations, from free-space diffractive processors to reconfigurable guided-wave metastructures. We conclude with open problems and opportunities for this emerging neuromorphic nanophotonics ecosystem.

</details>


### [246] [Comparison of melanin subtypes using pump-probe microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40969)
*Emma Bao,David Grass,Martin C. Fischer,Warren S. Warren*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文利用泵浦-探测显微镜(PPM)表征黑色素激发态动力学，发现黑色素异质性可区分皮肤与非皮肤病变，但与恶性程度无关，并可用于组织光学分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑色素在光保护、皮肤与眼睛色素沉着及黑色素瘤发病机制中作用关键，但其结构与化学复杂性使其难以分析；需要一种能无标记探测其激发态动力学的新方法以产生传统显微术无法提供的对比。

Method: 应用泵浦-探测显微镜(PPM)对六类病变来源的黑色素成像：皮肤痣、雀斑样痣(lentigo)、皮肤恶性黑色素瘤、结膜黑素细胞病变、外阴黑素细胞病变及小鼠黑色素瘤模型，共21个独立生物样本、109个感兴趣区域；采用模型拟合、改进的相量分析(phasor analysis)与支持向量机分类，将激发态动力学作为化学与结构异质性的替代指标进行表征与量化。

Result: 黑色素异质性可将皮肤病变与非皮肤病变区分开，但与恶性程度不相关；良性雀斑样痣是数据集中异质性最高的群体，超过皮肤恶性黑色素瘤，而结膜病变尽管直接受阳光照射却是最均一的；基于完整瞬态吸收曲线的分类在多数亚型上显著优于六分类随机水平。

Conclusion: PPM能够揭示黑色素在不同组织环境中的细微光物理差异，对组织光学分类及黑色素瘤发病机制的理解具有意义。

Abstract: Pump-probe microscopy (PPM) is used to probe excited state dynamics of endogenous chromophores to generate contrast that is unavailable in traditional forms of microscopy. Here we apply PPM to characterize melanin, a pigment central to photoprotection, skin and eye pigmentation, and melanoma pathogenesis. Melanin is notoriously difficult to analyze due to its structural and chemical complexity. We imaged melanin from six different lesion categories: cutaneous nevi, lentigo, cutaneous malignant melanoma, conjunctival melanocytic lesions, vulvar melanocytic lesions, and a murine melanoma model, across 21 independent biological samples and 109 regions of interest. Using model fitting, adapted phasor analysis, and support vector machine classification, we characterize and quantify excited-state dynamics as a surrogate for chemical and structural heterogeneity. Heterogeneity of melanin separates cutaneous from non-cutaneous lesions, but is not correlated with malignancy. Benign lentigo is the most heterogeneous population in the dataset, exceeding cutaneous malignant melanoma, while conjunctival lesions are the most homogeneous despite receiving direct solar exposure. Classification on full transient absorption curves separates most subtypes well above the six-class chance level. These findings demonstrate the ability of PPM to reveal subtle differences in melanin photophysics across diverse tissue environments, with implications for optical tissue classification and the understanding of melanoma pathogenesis.

</details>


### [247] [Microscopic Theory of Spin-Orbit Interactions of Light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40979)
*Saeed Rahmanian Koshkaki,Ding Xu,Michael Fowler,Milan Delor,Arkajit Mandal*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文从非相对论量子电动力学出发建立微观量子理论，揭示光腔中光学自旋-轨道相互作用源于受限光子与材料激发之间的虚相互作用，并给出调控其各分量的材料设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前光学自旋-轨道相互作用（SOI）仅用唯象模型描述，无法揭示其微观量子起源，材料电子结构与光学SOI之间缺乏严格联系。

Method: 基于光-物质相互作用的非相对论量子电动力学（QED）描述构建微观量子理论，将光学SOI归因于受限光子与材料激发之间的虚相互作用。

Result: 理论框架给出调控光学SOI各分量（光学双折射、光子自能、TE-TM劈裂、光学塞曼劈裂）的微观材料设计原则，可精确工程化有效磁场、狄亚波点（diabolical points）与赝自旋织构。

Conclusion: 该微观理论的预测能力可用于光子自旋电子学与光学技术中材料体系的系统筛选与设计。

Abstract: Optical spin-orbit interactions (SOI), demonstrated experimentally in optical cavities, enable the polarization degrees of freedom of light to be described and controlled as an effective pseudospin, providing a foundation for spintronic-like control of light and the development of next-generation optical technologies. However, prior works describe this emergent effect using phenomenological models that fail to uncover its microscopic quantum origins, and as a result, a rigorous connection between material electronic structure and the optical SOI remains elusive. In this work, we address this fundamental challenge by developing a microscopic quantum theory starting from the non-relativistic quantum electrodynamical (QED) description of light-matter interactions. We show that this effect arises from virtual interactions between confined photons and material excitations. Crucially, our theoretical framework provides microscopic material-design principles for tuning the distinct components of optical SOI, which include the optical birefringence, photonic self-energies, TE-TM splitting, and optical Zeeman splitting, enabling precise engineering of effective magnetic fields, diabolical points, and pseudospin textures. We anticipate that the predictive capability of our microscopic theory will enable systematic screening and design of material systems for photonic spintronics and optical technologies.

</details>


### [248] [Intensity-Domain Demultiplexing of Orbital Angular Momentum Channels for Optical Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41259)
*Junsu Kim,Jiwon Jung,Hyunchae Chun,Seung Ryong Park*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 利用OAM花瓣图案的方位角谐波作为解复用基，通过一次傅里叶变换即可实现全部信道的解复用，构建了无需光栅或模式分选器的两信道OAM通信系统，在515 nm下达到5.2 Gb/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OAM复用在实际应用中受限于接收端分离各信道元件的复杂度和光损耗，需要一种更简单、低损耗的解复用方案。

Method: 以OAM花瓣图案的方位角谐波作为解复用基，使接收端成为无线电OAM链路中圆形天线阵列的光学对应物；拓扑荷为ℓ的花瓣图案只携带一个2ℓ阶方位角谐波，因此对方位强度分布做一次傅里叶变换即可返回所有信道，无需光栅或模式分选器。花瓣由单个q-plate产生的矢量涡旋光束投影到线偏振上形成。系统使用16位圆形光电接收阵列。

Result: 两信道系统在515 nm下、采用16位圆形光电接收阵列，实现5.2 Gb/s传输且低于前向纠错阈值，单信道速率2.6 Gb/s（基于1.4 GHz光电接收器）。

Conclusion: 该方案提供了一种简化、低损耗的OAM解复用途径，验证了花瓣方位角谐波解复用的可行性，为OAM复用通信的实用化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Orbital angular momentum (OAM) multiplexing has been limited in practice by the complexity and optical loss of the elements that separate the channels at the receiver side. Here we adopt the azimuthal harmonics of OAM petal patterns as the demultiplexing basis, which makes the receiver the optical counterpart of the circular antenna arrays used in radio OAM links: the petal pattern of topological charge $\ell$ carries a single azimuthal harmonic of order $2\ell$, so one Fourier transform of the azimuthal intensity profile returns every channel, without gratings or mode sorters. The petals are formed by projecting a vector vortex beam from a single q-plate onto a linear polarization. A two-channel system at 515 nm with a 16-position circular photoreceiver array reaches 5.2 Gb/s below the forward error correction threshold, 2.6 Gb/s per channel from a 1.4 GHz photoreceiver.

</details>


### [249] [From proof of concept to practice: the next decade of artificial intelligence in metasurface photonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41000)
*Sensong An,Haoyue Jiang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文是一篇观点性/综述性论文，深入研读100篇高被引光学研究，评估AI是否真正加速了完整的电磁器件设计流程并带来更优器件，认为答案取决于具体任务，并呼吁该领域从"任务层面的成功"转向"可积累的领域基础"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年AI已能快速预测电磁响应并生成高性能超表面设计，但一个关键问题仍未解答：相比不含学习模型的传统设计流程，AI是否让完整设计流程更快、生成器件更好。作者提出该评估缺失，导致AI在光学设计中的价值难以被确证。

Method: 定性的深度文献分析（观点/评述方法）。作者精读100篇高被引光学研究，按AI在设计任务中的作用进行分类：不可替代、明显有利、无明显优势，并对每类任务的证据进行审视。摘要未给出定量实验或指标。

Result: 按任务类型区分出AI具有不可替代性、明显优势与无明显优势的三类设计场景；核心结论是AI的收益高度依赖任务，不能一概而论地宣称"更快更好"。

Conclusion: 下一个十年的关键不在于在孤立任务上取得更多成功，而在于让每项成功成为领域基础，通过共享数据、可复用训练模型、将物理嵌入学习等转变实现。AI能否在该领域留下持久价值，取决于其收益能否被验证、方法能否被迁移、设计能否经实验校正。

Abstract: Over the past decade, artificial intelligence has learned to predict electromagnetic responses rapidly and generate high-performance metasurface designs. However, one question remains open: compared with conventional design workflows that contain no learned model, has it made the complete design workflow faster, and are the resulting devices better? We argue that the answer depends on the task. Reading 100 highly cited optical studies in depth, we distinguish the design tasks in which artificial intelligence is irreplaceable, clearly advantageous, or without clear advantage. In our view, the key to the next decade is not more successes in isolated tasks but making each success a foundation for the field, through shifts such as shared data, trained models, and physics written into learning. Whether artificial intelligence leaves lasting value in this field will depend on whether its benefits can be verified, its methods transferred, and its designs corrected by experiment.

</details>


### [250] [Non-contact temperature sensing and optical scatter in cryogenically-cooled crystalline silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41261)
*Disha Pankaj Kapasi,Travis Counihan,Kevin Kimes,Alina Sendowsky,Alexandra Gruson,Daniel Martinez,Michael Rezac,Kevin Silverio,Sean Sorto,Joshua R. Smith*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文设计并实施了一套基于前后表面反射干涉条纹的非接触测温实验，测量平板未镀膜低温晶体硅样品的温度，并表征其低温真空下的光学散射，提出将该技术用于爱因斯坦望远镜与LIGO Voyager等未来引力波探测器低温测试质量的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低温晶体硅因低机械损耗（热噪声驱动因素）、高热导率及123 K处零热膨胀系数，是下一代引力波探测器理想光学材料，但缺乏对低温测试质量温度的非接触、可靠监测手段，且低温真空环境下样品散射与冰层生长情况有待表征。

Method: 采用基于前后表面反射形成的干涉（光学条纹）的非接触传感方法测量平板-平板未镀膜晶体硅样品的温度；并在低温真空条件下用高灵敏度红外相机构建散射测量系统，获取双向反射分布函数（BRDF）。

Result: 在冷板温度123–294 K范围内，散射基本保持恒定，在散射角θ_s=7.5°处平均BRDF为(4.43±0.2)×10⁻³ sr⁻¹；当前装置下未观察到低温分子层（冰）在样品表面生长。

Conclusion: 干涉条纹测温方法可行，并可为未来引力波探测器中的低温测试质量提供实施方案；低温下样品散射稳定、无明显冰层污染，支持晶体硅作为低温光学材料的应用前景。

Abstract: Cryogenic crystalline silicon is a promising optical material for next generation gravitational-wave detectors due to its low mechanical loss (a driver of thermal noise), high thermal conductivity and zero coefficient of thermal expansion at 123 K. In this paper, we report on the design and results of an experiment to measure the temperature of a flat-flat uncoated crystalline silicon sample using a non-contact sensing method relying on interference (optical fringes) made from reflections between the front and back surfaces. We also propose a scheme for implementation of this technique for cryogenic test masses in future gravitational-wave detectors such as Einstein Telescope and LIGO Voyager. Additionally, we characterize the optical scattering of the sample in vacuum at cryogenic temperatures by using a high-sensitivity infrared camera. We measured relatively constant scatter with an average bidirectional reflectance distribution function (BRDF) of $(4.43\pm0.2)\times 10^{-3}\,\mathrm{sr^{-1}}$ at a scattering angle of $θ_{s} = 7.5^\circ$ over the (cold-plate) temperature range of 123-294 K and saw no evidence for growth of cryogenic molecular layers (ice) on the sample in our current setup.

</details>


### [251] [Dual-rate optical frequency comb based on a cylindrical microresonator with a nanoscale radius variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41315)
*Alena Kolesnikova,Andrey Gelash,Ilya Vatnik*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 圆柱形微谐振器存在纳米级半径变化时，可形成二维轴向-角向耗散克尔孤子（2D DKS），产生具有两个可独立调控重复频率的相干微梳，示例为约329 GHz与6 GHz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统微梳通常只有单一重复频率，难以同时覆盖不同频率尺度的应用需求；作者希望利用器件几何自由度实现双重复频率的紧凑相干微梳源。

Method: 对非线性耦合的Lugiato-Lefever方程进行数值模拟，采用贴近实际的微谐振器参数；考察半径近抛物线变化、与SNAP平台兼容的圆柱谐振器，分析不同轴向阶数下角向模式的色散差异及自发同步机制。

Result: 模拟得到2D DKS，角向重复频率约329 GHz、轴向重复频率约6 GHz；尽管不同轴向阶数的角向模式色散特性不同，自发同步机制仍使孤子重复频率统一；两个重复频率可通过选择器件几何按需调节。

Conclusion: 纳米级半径变化的圆柱微谐振器可作为双重复频率相干微梳的紧凑光源，在同一器件上同时工作在多个频率尺度，为跨频段应用提供新方案。

Abstract: We theoretically find that a cylindrical microresonator with nanoscale radius variation can be a source of coherent microcombs with two repetition rates, independently controlled by the device geometry. This phenomenon arises from the formation of two-dimensional axial-azimuthal dissipative Kerr solitons (2D DKS), which we discovered through numerical simulations of the nonlinearly coupled Lugiato-Lefever equations with realistic microresonator device parameters. We demonstrate that, despite the variation in the dispersion characteristics of azimuthal modes at different axial numbers, the spontaneous synchronization mechanism leads to a unified soliton repetition rate. We consider near-parabolic variations in the radius of a cylindrical resonator, compatible with the surface nanoscale axial photonics (SNAP) platform. In our example, we generate 2D DKS with an azimuthal rate of approximately 329 GHz and an axial rate of 6 GHz, which can be adjusted on demand by selecting the appropriate resonator geometry, thereby promising compact sources of frequency combs operating simultaneously across different frequency scales.

</details>


### [252] [Non-Hermitian Spectral Topology Shaped by Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41370)
*Hao Pan,Bo Xue,Wanyue Xiao,Qiuyang Li,Yuqiong Cheng,Yumeng Wang,Kun Ding,Alex M. H. Wong,Shubo Wang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文将耦合不对称性从空间推广到时间，揭示记忆（时域非局域性）如何塑造非厄米谱拓扑：时空非互易与记忆诱导的谱多值性在多叶黎曼面上产生受空间连通性与相互作用历史共同支配的非平庸谱拓扑，并经全波仿真与微波实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米谱拓扑重塑了人们对开放系统体—边对应关系的理解，但既有研究基本局限于瞬时耦合的马尔可夫区域，记忆（时间非局域性）对非厄米谱拓扑的作用尚不清楚。

Method: 把耦合不对称性从空间扩展到时间，构建具有长程记忆通道的时空非互易开放系统模型；在多叶黎曼面上分析记忆诱导的谱多值性与谱绕数，考虑通道耗散与格点衰减；采用全波数值仿真与微波实验进行验证。

Result: 发现长程记忆通道间的相干干涉可产生反常谱绕数与逆于主导耦合方向的趋肤局域；仅凭非对称延迟、无需空间耦合不对称即可产生非厄米谱拓扑与趋肤积累；通道耗散驱动拓扑相变并使趋肤效应反转，临界点出现在通道衰减率与格点衰减率相等处；上述反常趋肤效应及耗散控制的翻转得到全波仿真和微波实验证实。

Conclusion: 时间非局域性构成非厄米拓扑的基本自由度，可产生马尔可夫范式之外无法获得的谱拓扑与趋肤现象，为记忆型开放系统的体—边对应研究提供新框架。

Abstract: Non-Hermitian spectral topology has transformed our understanding of bulk--boundary correspondence in open systems, yet its exploration has largely remained within the Markovian regime of instantaneous coupling. Here, we reveal how memory shapes non-Hermitian spectral topology by extending coupling asymmetry from space to time. Spatiotemporal nonreciprocity and memory-induced spectral multivaluedness give rise to nontrivial spectral topology on multi-sheeted Riemann surfaces, governed by both spatial connectivity and interaction history. Coherent interference among long-range memory channels can induce anomalous spectral winding and skin localization opposite to the dominant-coupling direction. Moreover, asymmetric retardation alone can generate non-Hermitian spectral topology and skin accumulation even in the absence of spatial coupling asymmetry. Channel dissipation further drives topological transitions that reverse the skin effect, with a critical point at matched channel and lattice decay rates. We validate the anomalous skin effect and its dissipation-controlled reversal through full-wave simulations and microwave experiments. Our results establish time nonlocality as a fundamental degree of freedom in non-Hermitian topology, revealing phenomena inaccessible within the Markovian paradigm.

</details>


### [253] [Reconfigurable Generation of Terahertz Spiral Vector Beams Based on All-Dielectric Cascaded Metasurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41392)
*Hongjun Chen,Yuxin Tang,Jianming Li,Jitao Li,Li Luo,Tingting Tang,Jie Li,Jianquan Yao*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出全介质级联超表面方案，通过双层相对旋转实现对LCP/RCP分量的独立调控，在0.9 THz附近可重构生成螺旋数1–3可切换、旋向可逆的螺旋矢量光束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂结构太赫兹场（尤其矢量光束）的可重构生成仍是显著难题，现有方案难以同时实现偏振态独立调控与结构参数动态切换。

Method: 全介质级联超表面：各向同性相位补偿层＋各向异性圆偏振敏感层，配合针对螺旋光束设计的新相位分布；利用两层间相对旋转角独立控制LCP与RCP分量。

Result: 在线偏振THz波照射下，0.9 THz附近实现螺旋数1–3可切换、圆偏振旋向可逆的THz矢量光束；三种光束沿传播方向分别呈现279.9°、125.7°、75.5°的方位角旋转。

Conclusion: 该方案兼具可重构性与纵向演化特性，有望用于下一代无线通信与THz成像，并为高维矢量光束的可重构生成提供参考。

Abstract: The reconfigurable generation of complex structured terahertz (THz) fields, and vector beams in particular, remains a significant challenge. Here, we propose a novel all-dielectric cascaded-metasurface scheme for the reconfigurable generation of THz spiral vector beams. By combining an isotropic phase-compensation layer with an anisotropic circular-polarization-sensitive layer, together with a new phase design tailored to spiral beams, we achieve independent control of the left- and right-handed circularly polarized (LCP and RCP) components through the relative rotation angle between the two layers. Under linearly polarized THz wave illumination, THz vector beams with switchable spiral numbers of 1-3 and reversible circular-polarization handedness are demonstrated at frequencies around 0.9 THz. Moreover, owing to the longitudinal evolution characteristic of spiral beams, the three generated THz vector beams exhibit azimuthal rotations of 279.9°, 125.7° and 75.5°, respectively, along the propagation direction. Such complex THz vector beams, which combine reconfigurability with longitudinal evolution, are expected to find applications in emerging fields such as next-generation wireless communications and THz imaging. The proposed generation scheme also offers a useful reference for the reconfigurable generation of high-dimensional vector beams.

</details>


### [254] [Ultraviolet Raman spectroscopy using multimode aluminum oxide waveguides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41503)
*Roman Zakoldaev,Nikhil Jayakumar,Elie Vennat-Bresson,Jean-Claude Tinguely,Firehun Tsige Dullo,Balpreet Singh Ahluwalia*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 研究氧化铝（Al₂O₃）波导在532 nm与325 nm激发下作为低背景拉曼光谱平台的表现：532 nm处其归一化背景比Si₃N₄低两个数量级以上，325 nm下背景弱且可重复，并能检出分析物（浸油）的拉曼指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数波导拉曼平台工作在近红外，但向可见/紫外扩展激发波长可显著增强拉曼散射并利用共振增强；这一转变对光子材料的光学损耗、吸收及波导自身光谱背景提出更高要求，因此需要低背景、可短波长工作的波导材料。

Method: 采用背向收集的激发—检测几何，在532 nm和325 nm下表征Al₂O₃波导；将测得波导背景对入射激发功率-时间乘积归一化后与Si₃N₄对比；在不同波导宽度下评估325 nm背景的可重复性；以浸油为模型分析物沉积于波导表面，用信背比（SBR）和光谱对比度量化分析物能带相对波导背景的可见度。

Result: 532 nm下Al₂O₃归一化背景比Si₃N₄低两个数量级以上；325 nm下各宽度波导背景弱且可重复；两种波长下均可在波导背景之上恢复分析物特异性拉曼指纹，SBR与光谱对比度一致确认分析物谱带被清晰检出。

Conclusion: Al₂O₃兼具低实测光谱背景与从可见到紫外的可工作性，是向更短激发波长拓展集成拉曼光谱的有前景材料平台。

Abstract: Waveguide-based Raman spectroscopy offers a route toward compact and integrated photonic sensors. While most waveguide Raman platforms operate in the near-infrared, extending excitation toward the visible and ultraviolet is attractive because shorter excitation wavelengths can provide substantially stronger Raman scattering and access to resonance-enhanced molecular responses. This transition, however, places greater demands on the photonic material because of optical loss, absorption and waveguide-generated spectral background. Here, we investigate aluminum oxide ($Al_2O_3$) waveguides as a low-background platform for Raman spectroscopy at 532 nm and 325 nm using a back-collected excitation and detection geometry. At 532 nm, after normalization to the incident excitation power-time product, the measured $Al_2O_3$ background was more than two orders of magnitude lower than that of $Si_3N_4$. Under 325 nm excitation, the waveguides maintained a weak and reproducible spectral background across the investigated widths. Using immersion oil as a model analyte deposited over the waveguide surface, we demonstrate that analyte-specific Raman fingerprints can be recovered above the waveguide background at both wavelengths. Signal-to-background ratio and spectral contrast were used to quantify analyte visibility relative to the waveguide background, and both metrics consistently confirmed clear detection of the analyte bands. These results demonstrate that $Al_2O_3$ combines low measured spectral background with operation from the visible into the ultraviolet, providing a promising route toward integrated Raman spectroscopy at shorter excitation wavelengths.

</details>


### [255] [The Generalized Twin Image: Understanding Regularized Phase Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41531)
*Zhiyuan Hu,Michael Unser,Jonathan Dong*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 论文提出"广义孪生像"(generalized twin image)，将经典孪生像模糊从傅里叶相位恢复推广到任意可逆传感算子，并据此给出评估图像先验正则化有效性的准则：先验应在真值与其广义孪生像之间偏好真值。作者以全变差比值(TV ratio)量化该偏好，在结构化随机相位恢复模型下给出闭式估计，并用预训练去噪器实验验证该比值可预测正则化重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像先验能显著改善相位恢复，但其收益随成像模态差异极大，缺乏解释这一差异的理论工具。作者希望理解先验何时有效、何时失效，并把经典的孪生像模糊从傅里叶情形推广出去，从而得到可操作的先验有效性判据。

Method: 1) 定义广义孪生像：对任意可逆传感算子构造与真值产生相同测量、但结构互异的"孪生"解，并分析其在欠采样与过采样（含编码傅里叶测量）下的精确性；2) 提出判据：图像先验需在真值与广义孪生像之间偏好真值；3) 以全变差比值作为该偏好的简单度量，并对结构化随机相位恢复模型推导闭式估计；4) 使用预训练去噪器作为先验，在不同传感模型下开展正则化重建实验。

Result: 广义孪生像在欠采样下保持精确，在过采样的编码傅里叶测量下亦成立；全变差比值对结构化随机相位恢复模型有闭式估计；实验表明该比值能跨不同传感模型预测正则化重建的性能，即比值越有利，先验正则化效果越好。

Conclusion: 广义孪生像为理解相位恢复中先验效能的模态依赖性提供了统一视角，并给出了简便、可计算（含闭式估计）的先验适配性判据；TV比值可作为在实验前预判先验收益的实用指标。

Abstract: Image priors can dramatically improve phase retrieval reconstruction, but their effectiveness strongly varies between imaging modalities. We introduce the generalized twin image, which extends the classical twin-image ambiguity from Fourier phase retrieval to any invertible sensing operator, to understand this effectiveness. The ambiguity extends to undersampled and oversampled measurements, remaining exact under undersampling and for oversampled coded Fourier measurements. We show that it provides a simple criterion to assess regularization effectiveness: the image prior should favor the ground truth over its generalized twin image. We propose the total-variation ratio as a simple measure of this preference, and derive closed-form estimates for structured random phase retrieval models. Experiments with a pretrained denoiser demonstrate that the ratio predicts regularized reconstruction performance across different sensing models.

</details>


### [256] [Photonic Crystal Integrated Graphene-Silicon Schottky Diodes for Resonant Infrared Photodetection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41701)
*Lorenzo Pettorosso,Michael Horodynski,Dmitry K. Polyushkin,Thomas Mueller*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 提出一种石墨烯-硅肖特基结结合光子晶体板的短波红外窄带光电探测器，光子晶体使光电流增强两个数量级以上，且增强峰可由数值模拟预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代智能低功耗光电探测器需要窄带红外（IR）探测能力，而现有方案在波长选择性与CMOS兼容、可扩展性方面存在不足。

Method: 构建石墨烯-硅肖特基结器件，并在其中集成光子晶体（PhC）平板，实现对石墨烯吸收的波长相关调控；通过光电流测量进行表征，并用数值模拟验证增强行为。

Result: 器件在短波红外（SWIR）波段呈现窄带响应；PhC集成使光电流增强超过两个数量级，所得尖锐增强峰可由数值模拟准确预测。

Conclusion: 该方案提供了一种高度可扩展、可调谐、CMOS兼容的窄带红外光电探测架构，为下一代低功耗红外传感器铺平道路。

Abstract: Narrowband infrared (IR) photodetection has become a critical technology for the next generation of smart low-power photodetectors. In this work, we present a device based on a graphene-silicon Schottky junction with narrowband response in the short-wave IR (SWIR) regime. Integrating a photonic crystal (PhC) slab in the device enables a wavelength dependant tailoring of graphene absorption. The devices were characterized by photocurrent measurement, which was enhanced more than two orders of magnitude by the PhC integration. The sharp enhancements obtained can also be predicted by numerical simulation. This approach offers a highly scalable, tunable, CMOS-compatible architecture for narrowband IR photodetection, paving the way for next-generation, low-power IR sensors.

</details>


### [257] [Focusing through coupled multimode fibers for miniaturized single-atom tweezer arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41917)
*Benjamin P. Maddox,Daniel K. Ruttley*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文利用2×1多模光纤熔融耦合器实现共享同一出射端面的双独立光路，各自形成近衍射极限光镊，并通过引入小频差抑制干涉，使光镊间距对输入耦合失配不敏感，为低SWaP-C环境下的可重构单原子阵列提供路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子技术实用化要求系统具备低尺寸、重量、功耗与成本（SWaP-C）。基于超冷原子的平台通常用经笨重物镜聚焦的光镊操控原子，物镜昂贵且对失准敏感；多模光纤（MMF）虽可紧凑聚焦，但此前仅限单根光纤构建光镊，缺乏多通道可重构能力。

Method: 采用2×1多模光纤熔融耦合器构建两条共享同一远端端面的独立控制通道；对每通道分别形成近衍射极限光镊；在两通道间施加小频率偏移以抑制干涉；并测试光镊间距对输入耦合失准的敏感性。

Result: 每条通道均可形成近衍射极限光镊；通道间频偏有效抑制干涉；光镊间距对输入耦合失准不敏感。

Conclusion: 基于多模光纤耦合器的多通道光镊方案可实现紧凑、抗失准的可重构光镊，为低SWaP-C环境中的单原子阵列构建提供可行技术路线。

Abstract: Real-world deployment of quantum technologies requires systems in low SWaP-C packages. Quantum platforms based on ultracold atoms typically trap and control atoms with optical tweezers using light focused through bulky objective lenses, which are costly and sensitive to misalignment. Multimode fibers (MMFs) offer a compact route to focusing light, but their use in forming optical tweezers for cold atoms has been limited to a single fiber. Here we use a 2x1 MMF fused coupler to obtain two independent control paths sharing a common distal facet. We form near-diffraction-limited tweezers through each channel, suppress interference by applying a small frequency offset between the channels, and demonstrate that the tweezer separation is insensitive to misalignment of the input coupling. This work provides a route to reconfigurable single-atom arrays in low SWaP-C environments.

</details>


### [258] [Gearbox cavities: Ultrahigh wave confinement via broken rotational symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42003)
*Alessio Amaolo,Sean Molesky,Alejandro W. Rodriguez,George Shaker,Steven G. Johnson,Pengning Chao*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 结合逆设计与物理极限分析表明,无损二维腔中最大化局域态密度必须破除圆柱对称性;对称破缺设计可超越所有柱对称结构的上限达8个数量级,其机制类似"齿轮箱",将点源辐射转化为被离心势垒囚禁的高角动量波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统提升点源辐射的腔体增强方案(如圆布拉格光栅)依赖保持圆柱对称性的结构,但此类结构是否存在性能上限、是否必须破缺对称性尚不明确,需从基本极限角度给出判据。

Method: 将逆设计方法与腔体局域态密度的基本极限理论相结合,先推导柱对称无损二维腔的上限,再在对称性破缺的设计空间中优化局域态密度。

Result: 对称破缺腔的局域态密度可超越所有圆柱对称结构的上限,提升幅度最高达8个数量级;其物理图像是腔体充当"齿轮箱",把源的辐射转换为高角动量波,并被离心势垒所囚禁。

Conclusion: 圆柱对称性并非增强点源发射的必要条件,反而构成限制;打破对称性是获得极端局域态密度增强的关键自由度,离心势垒型囚禁为设计高效点源腔提供了新范式。

Abstract: Cavity enhancement of emission from a point source typically relies on structures that preserve its cylindrical symmetry, such as circular Bragg gratings. Combining inverse design with fundamental limits, we show that breaking this symmetry is necessary to maximize the local density of states in lossless two-dimensional cavities. Symmetry-broken designs exceed limits over all cylindrically symmetric structures by up to eight orders of magnitude, acting as ``gearboxes'' that convert the source's emission into high-angular-momentum waves trapped behind a centrifugal barrier.

</details>


### [259] [Field-resolved observation and quantification of the instantaneous Kerr response in semiconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42098)
*Haifeng Lin,Hannes Kempf,Hinduja Bhuvanendran,Aitor De Andrés,Markus Ludwig,Nicolò Maccaferri*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 利用场分辨简并泵浦-探测光谱在5 μm处直接测量锗的泵致相移及其时间演化，得到瞬时Kerr响应的非线性折射率 n2=(0.87±0.16)×10⁻¹⁷ m²/W。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于强度的技术（如z-scan）只能从光束传播或光谱变化间接推断非线性相移，且无法分辨非线性响应的时间演化，难以区分瞬时Kerr非线性与自由载流子等延迟贡献，因此需要一种直接、时间分辨的非线性折射率测量方法。

Method: 采用场分辨简并泵浦-探测光谱，测量透射探测光电场随泵浦-探测延迟的变化，直接提取泵致相移及其时间演化，同时保留对吸收变化的灵敏度；通过相移随泵浦强度的线性依赖拟合n2。

Result: 观测到跟随泵浦强度包络、在泵浦与探测不再重叠时消失的交叉相位调制，符合瞬时Kerr响应；相移随泵浦强度线性变化，测得 n2=(0.87±0.16)×10⁻¹⁷ m²/W。

Conclusion: 在实验条件下瞬时Kerr效应是所测非线性相移的主导贡献；场分辨泵浦-探测光谱可作为直接、时间分辨地测定非线性折射率并区分瞬时与延迟光学响应的方法。

Abstract: The optical Kerr effect, whereby the refractive index of a material changes with light intensity, underpins nonlinear photonics and emerging mid-infrared technologies. Accurate determination of the nonlinear refractive index is therefore essential, yet conventional intensity-based techniques infer the nonlinear phase shift indirectly from changes in beam propagation or optical spectra. Moreover, methods such as the $z$-scan cannot resolve the temporal evolution of the nonlinear response, making it difficult to distinguish instantaneous Kerr nonlinearities from delayed contributions, including free-carrier effects. Here, we investigate the nonlinear optical response of germanium at $5 μ\mathrm{m}$ using field-resolved degenerate pump-probe spectroscopy. By measuring the transmitted probe electric field as a function of pump-probe delay, we directly retrieve the pump-induced phase shift and its temporal evolution while retaining sensitivity to changes in absorption. We observe cross-phase modulation that follows the pump intensity envelope and vanishes when the pump and probe no longer overlap, consistent with an instantaneous Kerr response. The phase shift scales linearly with pump intensity, yielding a nonlinear refractive index of $n_2=(0.87\pm0.16)\times10^{-17} \mathrm{m}^2/\mathrm{W}$. Our results establish the instantaneous Kerr effect as the dominant contribution to the measured nonlinear phase shift under our experimental conditions and demonstrate field-resolved pump-probe spectroscopy as a direct, time-resolved method for determining the nonlinear refractive index and distinguishing instantaneous from delayed optical responses.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [260] [Automatic Construction of Strong Simple Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40700)
*Caleb N. Ellington,Jiaqi Wang,Sohan Addagudi,Elijah Cole*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出用大语言模型为科学领域深度学习研究自动构建基线，在36个任务上自动基线可恢复95.5%中位性能差距，12项研究中简单基线达到或超过原深度学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在物理与生命科学中日益普及，许多"表面进展"实为省略或缺失基线的假象；而同行评审难以应对快速增长的论文数量，需要可扩展的基线核查手段。

Method: 从Nature、Science和Cell系列期刊的同行评审研究中汇编36个监督分类与回归任务，均涉及面向科学应用的新型深度学习方法；随后用语言模型为每个任务自动构建简单基线，并分析跨科学领域与发表期刊的差异，以及推理时计算量与可获得研究上下文层级的影响。

Result: 自动构建的基线表现强劲：相对于平凡预测器与原深度学习模型之间的性能差距，中位恢复率95.5%（均值85.7%）；在12项研究中，简单基线（中位）达到或超过深度学习模型性能。

Conclusion: 自动基线构建是一种可行且可扩展的检查机制，可有效审计物理与生命科学中深度学习贡献的真实性，弥补人工同行评审的不足。

Abstract: To rigorously establish the value of a novel deep learning method, it must be compared against appropriate baselines. As deep learning has become increasingly common in the physical and life sciences, there have been numerous cases in which apparent progress has turned out to be an artifact of omitted or missing baselines. In this work, we investigate whether this problem can be mitigated by using large language models to automate the construction of baselines. We assemble 36 supervised classification and regression tasks from peer-reviewed studies in Nature, Science, and Cell-family journals that investigate novel deep learning methods for scientific applications. We then use a language model to construct simple baselines for each task. These automatically constructed baselines are strong, recovering a median of 95.5% (mean 85.7%) of the gap between a trivial predictor and the score of the deep learning model. The median simple baseline matches or exceeds the performance of the deep learning model in 12 studies. We further examine the variation across scientific domains and publication venues and we characterize the effects of test-time compute and access to different levels of study context. As human peer review has struggled to keep up with the rapidly increasing volume of research articles in recent years, our results suggest that automated baseline construction is a practical and scalable check on deep learning contributions in the physical and life sciences.

</details>


### [261] [Bayesian Distribution System State Estimation with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40728)
*Nan Lin,Peter Palensky,Pedro Vergara Barrios*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PoSE：用流匹配学习变电站注入先验、采样时结合潮流方程与量测进行噪声感知的Kalman型校正，实现物理一致的贝叶斯配电系统状态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中压馈线实时量测稀少，DSSE依赖二次变电站伪量测（贝叶斯先验）。加权最小二乘虽保持潮流精确，却把先验压缩为均值与方差；监督式深度学习估计器同时学习潮流且绑定单一量测集，泛化性差、不确定性不可靠。

Method: 保留DSSE的贝叶斯结构，仅学习DSO未知的部分——变电站注入分布，用flow matching在历史数据上训练神经网络先验；潮流方程、拓扑与量测仅在采样阶段通过噪声感知的Kalman型校正引入，网络从不近似潮流。

Result: IEEE 33节点：12种设置下CRPS较全协方差高斯先验降低6.7-7.5%；90%区间覆盖率接近精确后验，而监督估计器仅37-78%。240节点不平衡三相Iowa馈线：注入、电压、支路电流误差均最低；快速版每快照0.2-0.6s且精度相当，无需重训即在7种重构拓扑上优于高斯先验与重训监督估计器。

Conclusion: 将先验学习与物理约束解耦，使单个训练先验可服务任意量测集、噪声水平与拓扑，兼顾物理一致性与可靠不确定性量化。

Abstract: Medium-voltage feeders have few real-time measurements, so distribution system state estimation (DSSE) rests on pseudo-measurements of the secondary substations, a prior in Bayesian terms. Weighted least squares keeps the power flow exact but reduces this prior to a mean and variance, while supervised deep learning estimators also learn the power flow and are tied to one measurement set. We propose PoSE (posterior sampling for state estimation), which keeps the Bayesian structure of DSSE and learns only what the distribution system operator does not know, namely the distribution of substation injections, modelled by a neural network trained with flow matching on historical data. The power flow equations, grid topology and measurements enter only at sampling, through a noise-aware Kalman-type correction. The neural network therefore never approximates the power flow, but every posterior sample is a physically consistent operating point, and one trained prior serves any measurement set, noise level and topology. On the IEEE 33-bus feeder, PoSE lowers the probabilistic injection error (CRPS) by 6.7-7.5% against a full-covariance Gaussian prior in all 12 tested settings. It provides reliable uncertainty: the 90% intervals contain the true injection almost exactly as often as exact posterior samples would, whereas those of supervised estimators contain it in only 37-78% of cases. On the 240-bus unbalanced three-phase Iowa feeder, it gives the lowest injection, voltage and branch-current errors of all estimators. A fast variant matches this accuracy at 0.2-0.6s per snapshot and, without retraining, beats Gaussian priors and retrained supervised estimators on seven reconfigured topologies.

</details>


### [262] [Don't Just Impute, Detect: Task-Aware Diffusion Pipelines for Edge Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40732)
*Jeongsu Park,Taehyeon Yun,Geonwoo Kim,Yuji Lim,Moohong Min*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TADR：用冻结检测器的任务感知损失训练条件扩散精修器，修复IIoT缺失数据块填补偏差，无需反向传播采样轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IIoT边缘流水线常因断网/硬件故障丢失多传感器数据块。常规"先填补后检测"串联方案中，填补器仅以MSE训练，导致填补偏差：部分观测的异常块被平滑回正常形态，且正常数据上的边界伪影引发误报。

Method: 提出任务感知扩散精修器（TADR），条件扩散模型以下游冻结检测器损失景观为训练目标。因沿采样轨迹反传不可行，任务感知项作用于噪声预测已有的一步干净窗口估计上，训练开销与普通扩散训练持平。评估采用严格Point-to-Point协议，五个随机种子并用配对显著性检验；消融分析异常增强目标与增益条件。

Result: SMD上F1比未精修检测器提升0.338（p=0.0015），仅异常增强目标在消融中贡献0.463。增益随连续、互相冗余的通道数量及断连所移除的异常证据而增大；在一热编码命令通道主导的遥测数据集及PSM上无增益。十步采样下单CPU核41.4 ms/窗口，4.03 MB模型可支撑约24路并发1 Hz数据流。

Conclusion: 任务感知扩散精修可有效消除填补偏差并在特定数据特性下显著提升检测F1，且边缘部署成本低；但方法有效性依赖通道冗余与异常证据保留，存在明确适用边界。

Abstract: Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) edge pipelines routinely lose contiguous blocks of multi-sensor data to network dropouts and hardware faults. The standard remedy is a disjointed "impute-then-detect" pipeline whose imputer is trained only for Mean Squared Error (MSE). Such an imputer fills a missing block with the most probable normal pattern: a partially observed anomaly is smoothed back toward normality, and boundary artifacts on normal data raise false alarms. We call this imputation bias and propose the Task-Aware Diffusion Refiner (TADR), a conditional diffusion refiner trained against the loss landscape of a frozen downstream detector. Because back-propagating through the sampling trajectory is intractable, the task-aware terms act on the one-step clean-window estimate that the noise prediction already yields, so training costs no more than plain diffusion training. Under strict Point-to-Point evaluation over five seeds with paired significance tests, TADR raises F1 by $0.338$ over the unrefined detector on SMD ($p = 0.0015$), and the anomaly enhancement objective alone contributes $0.463$ in ablation. We also characterise when the method helps: the gain tracks the continuous, mutually redundant channels available and the anomaly evidence the outage removes, and it is absent on telemetry datasets dominated by one-hot command channels and on PSM. At ten sampling steps TADR runs in $41.4$ ms per window on a single CPU core, sustaining about $24$ concurrent 1 Hz streams from a $4.03$ MB model. Code and per-seed results are available at https://github.com/int-park422/TADR.

</details>


### [263] [Unsupervised Feature Selection Using Local Intrinsic Dimensionality -- Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40736)
*Oussama Chelly,Michael E. Houle,Ken-ichi Kawarabayashi,Muhammad Rajabinasab,Arthur Zimek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无监督特征选择方法 LIDFS，基于局部内在维度（LID）与距离测度不可区分性的形式等价，将特征视为点、按 k 近邻距离极大似然估计每个特征的 LID，并以低 LID 对应高局部可区分性给出重要性评分，同时通过邻域内去冗余得到稳定排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据同时损害学习算法的效率与效果，而许多应用缺乏类别标签，无法用监督方式指导降维。

Method: 利用 LID 与距离测度不可区分性的形式等价：转置数据矩阵使每个特征成为一个点，用其到 k 个最近邻特征的距离以极大似然估计 LID；LID 越低表示局部可区分性越强、得分越高；随后进行精炼步骤，在每个特征邻域中仅保留一个代表并将局部冗余特征降权，形成完整排序，任意数量特征可直接截取而无需重跑。方法不使用标签或类别数信息，时间复杂度为 O(d²n)（n 为样本数，d 为特征数）。

Result: 在 23 个高维数据集（经典基准集合）上，与经典方法、先进方法及随机基线比较；评估涵盖五种下游分类器的分类准确率与 AUC，以及聚类准确率和归一化互信息，覆盖完整与激进选择范围。LIDFS 在监督评估中排名第一，在基于聚类的评估中排名第一或并列第二；与需要类别数的方法同处最优统计组，且比最接近的竞争者运行更快；若干近期方法未能超过随机基线。敏感性分析表明小邻域规模效果最佳。

Conclusion: LIDFS 提供了一种无需标签与类别数、计算高效且输出全排序的无监督特征选择方案，在分类与聚类评估中均具竞争力，并揭示出邻域规模宜取较小值的实用经验。

Abstract: High dimensionality degrades both the efficiency and the effectiveness of learning algorithms, and in many applications no class labels are available to guide dimensionality reduction. This paper proposes LIDFS, an unsupervised feature selection method based on Local Intrinsic Dimensionality (LID). The method builds on the formal equivalence between LID and the indiscriminability of distance measures. The data matrix is transposed so that each feature is treated as a point, and the LID of each feature is estimated by maximum likelihood from the distances to its $k$ nearest neighboring features. Features with lower LID exhibit greater local discriminability and receive higher importance scores. A refinement step then retains one representative per feature neighborhood and demotes locally redundant features. The result is a complete ranking from which any number of features can be selected without rerunning the procedure. LIDFS does not use any information about labels or the number of classes, and runs in $\mathcal{O}(d^2 n)$ time for $n$ instances and $d$ features. LIDFS is compared with classic and state-of-the-art methods and a random baseline on 23 high-dimensional datasets, the classic benchmark collection. The evaluation uses classification accuracy and AUC with five downstream classifiers, as well as clustering accuracy and normalized mutual information, over both full and aggressive selection ranges. LIDFS ranks first in the supervised evaluations and first or tied second in the clustering-based evaluations. It belongs to the top statistical group together with methods that require the number of classes, and it runs faster than its closest competitor. Several recent methods, by contrast, fail to outperform the random baseline. A sensitivity analysis shows that small neighborhood sizes yield the best results.

</details>


### [264] [Certified Behavioral Retention for Continual Adaptation with Held-Out Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40740)
*Ahanaf Hasan Ariq,Abrar Shahid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CABA：一种与候选生成器无关的更新后门控机制，通过冻结表征上的锚点KL散度预算认证，决定是否接受持续适应性更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续适应不仅需决定"如何更新模型"，还需在保护行为的变更预算约束下决定"是否提交更新"，即需要可审计的保留性验证。

Method: 从候选优化中预留历史观测作为锚点；在冻结表征上比较旧策略与候选策略；选取最坏锚点前向KL散度在预算内的最大插值系数；仅需有限次前向检查，无需动作标签或完整轨迹；并建立理论认证框架，区分实测锚点保留与条件性离锚保证。

Result: 在显式表征覆盖与Lipschitz假设下，被接受的更新在保护支撑集上满足一致行为散度界；给出仿射softmax头正则性界、概率覆盖与压缩保证、插值单调性条件、网格分辨率界及轨迹级与序列复合保证。UCI HAR代理实验中，ε=0.05时平均遗忘率由1.01%降至0.00%，但最终平均准确率由93.04%降至88.48%。

Conclusion: CABA为具有可比策略分布和声明更新路径的候选生成器提供可审计的保留性门控，体现保留—可塑性权衡；不构成基座模型或机器人性能证据，部署仍需单独验证当前任务效用。

Abstract: Continual adaptation requires deciding not only how to update a model, but whether to commit an update without exceeding a declared budget for changes to protected behavior. We introduce Certified Anchor-Based Adaptation (CABA), a candidate-agnostic post-update gate that reserves historical observations from candidate optimization, compares old and candidate policies on frozen representations, and selects the largest tested interpolation coefficient whose worst-anchor forward KL divergence remains within budget. The finite check requires neither action labels nor complete trajectories. We develop a theory-led certification framework that separates measured anchor retention from conditional off-anchor guarantees. Under explicit representation-coverage and Lipschitz assumptions, an accepted update satisfies a uniform behavioral-divergence bound on protected support. The analysis gives regularity bounds for affine softmax heads, probabilistic coverage and compression guarantees, interpolation monotonicity conditions, grid-resolution bounds, and trajectory-level and sequential-composition guarantees. In a controlled UCI Human Activity Recognition study used as a non-stationary sensor-policy proxy, CABA reduces mean forgetting from 1.01 percent for unconstrained adaptation to 0.00 percent at budget epsilon = 0.05, while reducing final average accuracy from 93.04 percent to 88.48 percent. The experiment illustrates a retention-plasticity trade-off and does not establish foundation-model or robotic performance. CABA provides an auditable retention check for candidate generators exposing comparable policy distributions and a declared update path, while deployment also requires separate evidence of current-task utility.

</details>


### [265] [AutoHebb: Self-Modifying Neural Networks that Zero-Shot Infer and Continually Learn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40757)
*Daniel Levine,David van Dijk,Kenneth Kay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"自主Hebbian网络"(AutoHebb)——一类在线学习的前馈网络,通过元学习优化固定权重、以内部活动生成全局"神经调质"信号门控三因子Hebbian局部可塑性,在网络中同时实现强归纳偏置与自主学习控制,在零样本推理(ZI)与持续学习(CL)任务上涌现出泛化能力并超越现代模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ZI与CL建模存在双重局限:一是缺乏脑内关键成分——ZI所需的强归纳偏置、CL所需的自主学习控制;二是模型复杂度与任务适配度难以兼顾,组件过复杂则缺乏可分析性(simplicity),过于定制则缺乏表达力(expressivity)。

Method: AutoHebb为在线学习前馈神经网络:不依赖外部梯度下降优化,而是将外部反馈与内部生成活动结合,产生全局学习信号(神经调质)反馈至网络;该调质门控生物合理的局部三因子Hebbian规则以更新可塑性权重。使用元学习搜索固定的非可塑性权重。

Result: AutoHebb网络(1)在ZI任务上成功;(2)表现出涌现的、未经训练的ZI/CL能力;(3)可被逆向工程为显式算法;(4)在ZI/CL任务上优于现代学习模型。与相关模型的对比表明元学习、Hebbian可塑性与自主学习控制三个组件均不可或缺。

Conclusion: AutoHebb兼具简单性与表达力,同时具备强归纳偏置与自主学习控制,可作为生成假设、分析与发现生物合理学习与泛化形式的工具。

Abstract: Humans and animals have a unique capacity to generalize and learn in novel circumstances, including "0-shot" inference (ZI) and continual learning (CL) -- two powerful cognitive abilities. Despite this importance, efforts to model ZI and CL are seriously limited in several ways. First, existing models typically lack components critical to ZI and CL in the brain, namely, strong inductive biases (for ZI) and autonomous control of learning (for CL). Second, models can be less tractable to analysis if model components are overly complex (lacking simplicity), or too task-restricted if model components are overly bespoke (lacking expressivity). Here we introduce autonomous Hebbian networks ("AutoHebb"), a class of online learning feedforward neural networks that realize strong inductive biases and autonomously controlled learning, and are both simple and expressive. Unlike standard models that learn via externally controlled optimization (e.g. gradient descent), AutoHebb networks combine external feedback with internally generated activity to produce a global learning signal ('neuromodulator') fed back to the network. The neuromodulator in turn gates a biologically grounded local learning rule (3-factor Hebbian rule) that updates networks' plastic weights. Using meta-learning to search for fixed (non-plastic) weights, we find that AutoHebb networks (1) succeed on ZI tasks, (2) show emergent (untrained) ZI/CL abilities, (3) admit reverse-engineering into explicit algorithms, and (4) outperform modern learning models on ZI/CL tasks. Lastly, performance comparisons to related models implied an essential role for three model components: meta-learning, Hebbian plasticity, and autonomous control of learning. These findings indicate that AutoHebb may be useful in hypothesis generation, analysis, and discovery of biologically grounded forms of learning and generalization.

</details>


### [266] [Frozen in Time: How a Physics Foundation Model Forgets Under Sim-to-Real Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40857)
*Haaris Mian,Miles Cranmer,Alessandro Favero,Payel Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究科学基础模型 Walrus-1.3B 在"仿真→真实"两阶段微调中的灾难性遗忘问题，发现微调会严重损害原始预训练性能，并证明仅回放 1% 仿真数据即可大幅缓解遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学基础模型（SciFM）可通过微调适应未见数据分布，但微调存在灾难性遗忘风险，即适应新任务会损害模型原有知识。然而，这一问题在科学基础模型的仿真到真实（sim-to-real）适配场景中尚未被系统研究。

Method: 以连续介质动力学的科学基础模型 Walrus-1.3B 为对象，开展两阶段 sim-to-real 适配：先在有圆柱绕流的仿真数据上微调，再适配同一系统的实验数据。测量微调过程中各预训练数据集上的 rollout 性能与一步误差变化，并分析数据集依赖性与任务表示相似性等潜在遗忘驱动因素；随后在真实数据微调时混入少量（1%）仿真数据进行回放。

Result: 在仿真数据上微调会迅速破坏原有预训练数据集上的 rollout 性能，仅 20 个 epoch 后一步误差最多上升 35 倍；后续向真实数据适配会进一步削弱仿真任务上的性能。在真实数据微调中回放仅 1% 的仿真数据，即可恢复大部分损失的仿真性能，同时保持对实验动力学的有效适配。

Conclusion: 灾难性遗忘是科学基础模型 sim-to-real 适配的实际障碍，而轻量级回放策略能显著提升知识保留，为科学基础模型的实际部署提供了简单有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Scientific foundation models (SciFMs) have achieved remarkable performance on a wide range of PDEs, including the ability to efficiently adapt to unseen data distributions with additional finetuning. As with all neural architectures, finetuning such models comes with the risk of catastrophic forgetting, whereby adapting to new tasks degrades prior knowledge of the model. We study catastrophic forgetting in Walrus-1.3B, a SciFM for continuum dynamics, over the course of a two-stage sim-to-real adaptation: first finetuning on simulated flow around a cylinder and then adapting to experimental data of the same system. We find that finetuning on the simulations rapidly degrades rollout performance on the original pretraining datasets, with one-step errors increasing up to 35x after just 20 epochs. Subsequent adaptation to real data further erodes performance on the simulated task. We characterize the potential drivers of forgetting, such as dataset dependence and task representation similarity. We then show that replaying as little as 1% simulated data during real-data finetuning recovers most of the lost simulation performance while retaining effective adaptation to the experimental dynamics. Our results identify catastrophic forgetting as a practical obstacle to sim-to-real adaptation of SciFMs and show that lightweight replay strategies significantly improve retention.

</details>


### [267] [FARO: Function-Space Adaptive Return-to-Risk Optimization via Cross-Block Equal-Risk-Contribution Budget Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40858)
*Xiaojun Wu,Cehao Yang,Xuhui Jiang,Chengjin Xu,Jia Li,Jian Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出函数空间自适应优化器 FARO：以参数分块为决策单元，按各块的反事实收益赋予几何，再用跨块等风险贡献(ERC)重分配冻结预算，在 GPT 类模型上无需人工设定逐层几何即可复现最优手工配方，并带小幅稳健增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型训练配方依赖人工先验，将参数按层类型划分：注意力与 MLP 矩阵用正交化动量，其余用 Adam 式更新；这种划分基于人对“何种层该用何种几何”的主观判断，且所有参数块共享一个固定的全局更新预算。

Method: FARO 将参数分块作为决策单元：1) 依据每个块的实际反事实收益(counterfactual return)为其分配几何(更新规则)；2) 冻结各块路由后，按投资组合理论中的风险平价原则——跨块等风险贡献(ERC)——重新分配冻结的逐块预算。

Result: 在 FineWeb 上训练的 GPT 风格模型：FARO-Adaptive 在完整调度末达 3.2780 nats，与最佳手工配方 NorMuonAndAdam(3.2794)持平，即无需手工设定逐层几何即可复现人类先验；FARO-ERC 进一步提升，在全部 6 个随机种子上均降低干净数据损失，平均降低 0.0136 nats，符号检验、配对检验与置信区间结论一致，且超出运行间噪声，不增加时间与内存开销。其机制是削减风险最高的块而非扩张最安全的块；在受损数据流下仍降低平均损失并赢得多数种子。

Conclusion: FARO 以函数空间自适应与风险平价预算重分配替代手工的逐层几何与全局预算设计，可在不增开销的前提下匹配甚至小幅优于最佳人工配方，并具备稳健性；代码已开源。

Abstract: Training a large language model is compute-bound, and current recipes divide the work between optimizers by hand: orthogonalized momentum for attention and MLP matrices, Adam-style updates for the rest. This division rests on human priors about which layer type deserves which geometry, and it fixes a single global update budget that every parameter block shares. We introduce FARO, a function-space adaptive optimizer that makes the partition of parameters into blocks the unit of decision. FARO gives each block a geometry by its realized counterfactual return, then freezes the routes and reallocates the frozen per-block budget by cross-block equal risk contribution (ERC), the risk-parity principle from portfolio theory. On a GPT-style model trained on FineWeb, the framework gives two results. First, its FARO-Adaptive arm reaches 3.2780 nats at the full schedule, matching NorMuonAndAdam (3.2794), the best hand-designed recipe: it recovers the human prior without hand-setting per-layer geometry. Second, FARO-ERC improves on that arm: it lowers the clean-data loss at every one of 6 seeds, by 0.0136 nats on average, a modest but robust gain. Its sign test, paired test, and confidence interval all agree, and it exceeds the measured run-to-run noise at no added time or memory. It works by trimming the riskiest blocks rather than expanding the safest, and it holds up under a corrupted stream, still lowering the mean loss and winning the seed majority. The source code is available at https://github.com/wxj630/FARO.

</details>


### [268] [Near-Optimal First-Order Regret for Bandits with Feedback Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40861)
*Mengxiao Zhang,Yuheng Zhang,Haipeng Luo,Paul Mineiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了已知有向反馈图下对抗性多臂老虎机的first-order regret（一阶后悔）保证。针对强可观测图，作者构造了采样分布使单轮后悔余项由学习器损失和图独立数α控制，得到一个期望后悔界$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{\alpha L^\star}+\alpha)$，弥合了worst-case与first-order保证之间的图依赖差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强可观测图上，最坏情况（minimax）后悔为$\widetilde{\Theta}(\sqrt{\alpha T})$，其中α为独立数；而现有最优first-order界为$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{(\kappa+1)L^\star})$，κ为团划分数量且$\kappa+1\geq\alpha$，$L^\star$为最优臂累积损失。worst-case与first-order界在图的依赖上存在差距（前者依赖α，后者依赖κ+1），本文旨在弥合该差距。

Method: 1）先处理self-aware图（每个顶点带自环）的特例：将EXP3的更新与受"exploration by optimization"启发的采样规则结合，构造一个映射，其不动点给出采样分布，使单轮后悔余项可由学习器损失和图独立数α控制；2）证明可通过几何收敛的迭代在多项式时间内计算出足够精确的不动点；3）通过适配[LLZ20]的两层聚合方法，将算法推广到一般强可观测图。

Result: 在self-aware图及一般强可观测图上，均获得期望后悔界$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{\alpha L^\star}+\alpha)$，其图依赖为α，与minimax最优界的图依赖一致；不动点可由多项式时间的几何收敛迭代求得。

Conclusion: 本文提出了首个图依赖达到独立数α的first-order后悔保证，弥合了worst-case与first-order界之间的图依赖差距，并通过固定点迭代与两层聚合技术给出了可计算、可推广的算法。

Abstract: We study first-order regret guarantees for adversarial bandits with a fixed, known directed feedback graph. For strongly observable graphs, [ACBDK15] establishes a minimax regret of $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{αT})$, where $α$ denotes the independence number of the graph and $T$ denotes the horizon. On the other hand, the best existing first-order bound scales as $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{(κ+1) L^\star})$ [LLZ20], where $κ$ denotes the clique partition number, satisfying $κ+1\geqα$, and $L^\star$ denotes the cumulative loss of the best arm, leaving a gap between the graph dependence in worst-case and first-order guarantees. In this paper, we bridge this gap by obtaining an expected regret bound of $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{αL^\star}+α)$. Our first result is an algorithm for the special case of self-aware graphs, in which every vertex has a self-loop. The algorithm combines the update of EXP3 [ACBFS02] with a sampling rule inspired by exploration by optimization [LS20]. More specifically, we construct a map whose fixed points yield sampling distributions that control the one-round regret remainder in terms of the learner's loss and the graph's independence number, which is crucial in deriving optimal first-order guarantees. We further show that a sufficiently accurate fixed point can be computed in polynomial time by a geometrically convergent iteration. Finally, we extend the algorithm to general strongly observable graphs and achieve the same $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{αL^\star}+α)$ guarantee by adapting the two-level aggregation approach of [LLZ20].

</details>


### [269] [TensorSSM: Memorizing Spatial Position in Dynamic State Space for Tensor Core Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40871)
*Sheng Zhang,Xinyi Wang,Maolin Wang,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TensorSSM：将高阶张量按K-ary规则分解为多模态坐标序列，并扩展状态空间模型以显式建模跨模态空间依赖，实现更高精度、更低存储开销的函数式张量分解（INR压缩）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维张量存储开销巨大；函数式张量分解（INR）用神经网络映射坐标到张量元素以大幅降参，但现有方法将高阶张量展平为1D序列，导致局部坐标序列高度重叠、难以捕捉复杂跨模态空间依赖，表达力受限。

Method: 提出TensorSSM：1）按K-ary规则将各模态坐标分解为序列，由粗到细编码空间token，捕捉空间变化；2）扩展SSM状态空间以显式映射跨模态空间依赖，将前一隐藏状态的跨模态信息融入当前状态转移，实现模态间交互以增强各模态状态更新；表示沿序列维（粗到细token）与状态维（跨模态记忆）双维扩展。

Result: 大量实验显示TensorSSM取得显著更高的重建精度与存储效率，在多种张量数据集上表现稳健的表达能力，且不依赖严格的空间假设。

Conclusion: 将空间信息有效融入状态空间与张量核心，提升了状态自适应性与压缩表达力，为高阶张量函数式分解提供了新范式。

Abstract: High-dimensional tensors are ubiquitous in real-world applications but lead to massive storage overhead. Functional tensor decomposition addresses this by employing neural networks to map element coordinates to tensor cores, significantly reducing the parameters for data compression. However, existing functional methods typically flatten high-order tensors into 1D sequences. This folding operation causes highly overlapping local coordinate sequences and struggles to capture complex cross-mode spatial dependencies, severely limiting their expressiveness on diverse datasets. To tackle these challenges, we propose TensorSSM, a novel Implicit Neural Representation (INR) for functional decomposition method that memorizes spatial positions within a dynamic State Space Model (SSM). Specifically, TensorSSM first decomposes mode-wise coordinates into sequences spit by K-ary rule, encoding spatial tokens from coarse to fine, which effectively captures spatial variations. Furthermore, we extend the SSM's state space to explicitly map cross-mode spatial dependencies, allowing inter-mode interactions to enhance the updates of each mode. By integrating cross-mode information from previous hidden states into the current state transitions, TensorSSM effectively expands tensor representation along two dimensions: the sequence dimension for coarse-to-fine spatial tokens, and the state dimension for cross-mode memorization. This design effectively integrates spatial information into the state space and the tensor core, substantially advancing the state adaptability and enhancing the expressiveness of the data compression. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TensorSSM achieves significantly high reconstruction accuracy and storage efficiency, exhibiting robust expressiveness across diverse tensor datasets without relying on strict spatial assumptions.

</details>


### [270] [TSFLab: A Self-Improving Agent-Agnostic Workbench for Time Series Forecasting Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40901)
*ChengAo Shen,Yanjun Zhao,Haoxin Liu,Bonnie Chen,Miles Ma,Dongjin Song,Hanghang Tong,Qingsong Wen,Jingrui He,Jingchao Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TSFLab——一个自改进、agent无关的"工作台"，为兼容的编码智能体提供共享资源与工具，以低成本支撑时间序列预测（TSF）的可复现、基准测试与自动研究（autoresearch）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TSF文献与产品激增，但仅约20%的arXiv TSF论文提供代码链接，已发布代码也需人工维护；研究者被迫不断自行适配或重实现，而独立实现间实验设置不一致、难以维护。直接调用Claude Code、Codex等现有编码智能体又成本高昂、难以扩展到大规模研究项目（如基准测试），且缺乏自我维护能力。

Method: 设计agent无关的"工作台"TSFLab，将共享资源与工具（统一实验设置、可复用实现、维护机制）注入兼容的编码智能体，支持研究资源的持续维护，并覆盖复现、基准测试与新方法开发三类任务。

Result: 复现与基准测试中，任务完成率较基线提升20–163%，每个已完成任务的新增token消耗降低11–66%；新方法开发的初步研究中，预测精度提升，且相比其他设置减少32.8–73.6%的新增token与9.2–68.8%的开发时间。

Conclusion: 以共享工作台赋能通用编码智能体，是低成本、可扩展地推进TSF基准测试与自动研究的有效路径，兼具效率与效果优势。

Abstract: As publications and products on time series forecasting (TSF) proliferate, researchers face a growing burden of analyzing existing work. First, not all publications release code. For instance, only about 20% of arXiv TSF papers have a code link in the surveyed period. Second, released code also requires manual maintenance. Thus, TSF researchers must constantly adapt or implement existing works. However, independent implementations can differ in experimental settings and are difficult to maintain. We therefore propose to use AI agents for benchmarking TSF research and even supporting autoresearch, e.g., developing new TSF models. Yet directly applying existing coding agents, such as Claude Code and Codex, is suboptimal: prohibitive costs, limited scalability to large research projects (e.g., benchmarking), and lack of self-maintenance. To address these challenges, we propose TSFLab, a self-improving agent-agnostic "workbench" that equips compatible coding agents with shared resources and tools for low-cost TSF research and continuous maintenance of research resources. Our experiments cover reproduction, benchmarking, and new method development. In reproduction and benchmarking, TSFLab improves task completion by 20-163% and cuts fresh tokens per completed task by 11-66% across baselines. A pilot study of agent-driven method development also shows improved forecasting accuracy via TSFLab, with 32.8-73.6% fewer fresh tokens and 9.2-68.8% less development time than the other settings.

</details>


### [271] [No Channels Left Behind: Understanding Diffusion Acceleration through Residual Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40914)
*Junha Park,Jaemyung Yu,Jaehui Hwang,Jong-Seok Lee,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 扩散Transformer训练加速方法（REPA、dispersive正则）的加速机制被认为源于残差门控通路的重塑，而非仅来自辅助目标施加的特征结构；据此提出的免教师径向目标在SiT-B/2上超越dispersive loss。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对REPA等训练加速方法的解释集中于额外监督、空间结构先验或改进的特征几何，但这些解释不完整。作者发现加速方法显著重组了AdaLN-Zero残差门控α，从而提出加速部分源于门控通路重塑的假设。

Method: (1) 分析AdaLN-Zero门控α在加速方法下的变化，测试模型对α扰动的敏感性，考察门控几何与类别语义的对应关系；(2) 提出归一化参与比 nPR 度量门控的通道级组织；(3) 提出简单免教师径向目标，通过特征范数正则促进更宽的门控参与，无需成对特征比较。

Result: 加速模型对α扰动明显更敏感，其门控几何反映类别语义关系；在多种REPA教师、投影器架构与径向目标下，更宽的门控参与对应更好的生成质量。在ImageNet 256×256、SiT-B/2上，方法FID优于dispersive loss，且各时间步与类别上的平均门控参与度提升。

Conclusion: 残差门控重组织是扩散训练加速的一个可测量维度，并指出了进一步加速的简洁方向：通过范数正则拓宽门控参与。

Abstract: Training acceleration methods for diffusion transformers, such as REPA and dispersive regularization, are commonly explained by additional supervision, spatial-structure priors, or improved feature geometry. We revisit this view and uncover a striking internal change: the AdaLN-Zero residual gate $α$, which directly controls how conditioned updates enter the residual stream, is substantially reorganized by these methods. This observation motivates our hypothesis that acceleration arises, at least in part, from reshaping the residual gating pathway rather than solely from the feature structures explicitly imposed by auxiliary objectives. Supporting this view, accelerated models are markedly more sensitive to perturbations of $α$, and their gate geometry reflects semantic relationships among classes. We further introduce the normalized participation ratio (nPR) to characterize the channel-wise organization of the gates and find that, across REPA teachers, projector architectures, and radial-objective targets, broader gate participation accompanies better generation quality. Guided by this insight, we propose a simple teacher-free radial objective that promotes broader gate participation through feature-norm regularization, without pairwise feature comparisons. On ImageNet $256\times256$ with SiT-B/2, our method improves FID over dispersive loss while increasing mean gate participation over timesteps and classes, further supporting the link between gate organization and training acceleration. Our results identify residual-gate reorganization as a measurable axis of diffusion training acceleration and suggest a simple direction for further acceleration.

</details>


### [272] [A Theoretical Framework for Understanding and Building "Any-task inference" Capable Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40934)
*Matthew McDermott,Florent Pollet,Payal Chandak,Gregory Kondas,Liat Friedman Antwarg,Noa Dagan,Isaac Kohane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为"任意任务推理"(ATI)类基础模型建立形式化框架与合作数学定义，提出"任务空间"与接口式定义，并给出编码器-模型-解码器的"通用任务归约"分解及误差分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型研究缺乏对"任意任务推理/零样本"这一类模型的统一定义，难以区分其与预训练微调范式的差异，也无法理论化地判断某模型结构能否达到最优。

Method: 将"感兴趣的任务空间"纳入问题域，给出ATI基础模型的接口式形式定义（样本x与任务t映射到标签空间预测）；证明所有ATI模型可经"通用任务归约"分解为编码器、模型、解码器三部分，并据此将误差分解为"设计项"（结构性质决定可达最优性）与"实现模型超额项"（学习组件与程序性组件交互）；提出生成式与判别式两类原型配方。

Result: 给出可操作的误差分层分解，可预先判断某ATI模型结构的可实现最优性；提出两类原型实现路径；以5个跨领域真实基础模型案例贯穿验证定义的适用性。

Conclusion: 该形式化定义简洁且具普适性，为ATI基础模型的分析、设计与误差归因提供统一理论视角，可推广至多样化真实场景。

Abstract: In this work, we provide a formal framework and mathematical definition for the subset of foundation models focused on enabling the property of "any-task inference" (ATI) (similar to zero-shot inference), rather than those designed for pre-training fine-tuning. To achieve this mathematical definition, we formalize the notion of a "space of tasks of interest," which we include in the problem domain, then provide an interface-based formal definition for ATI foundation models as those that map samples of data $x$ and tasks $t$ to predictions over the space of labels directly. While simple, this definition allows us to show that all ATI foundation models can be decomposed through a "universal task reduction" lens into encoder, model, and decoder aspects. We can leverage this decomposition to also decompose foundation model error into terms corresponding to different aspects of this reduction, stratified into a collection of "design terms," which are theoretical properties that can inform one of whether a particular proposed ATI foundation model can achieve optimality or not due to the structure of the proposed task reduction, and "realized model excess" terms which capture interactions between learned and programmatic components of foundation model systems. Lastly, we can use this perspective to offer two "prototypical" recipes for realizing ATI foundation models in diverse domains, which we term generative and discriminative variants of ATI foundation models. This exercise is grounded throughout by 5 real-world examples of foundation models in different settings, varying greatly in how these capabilities are realized, showing the applicability of this definition to real-world settings.

</details>


### [273] [Fair Recommender Systems for Intersecting Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40936)
*Christine Ling,Juba Ziani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究含交叉人口群体的动态推荐系统中的群体感知问题：证明群体无关策略线性遗憾，并提出交集拟合与两种群体级启发式（Inner-Outer UCB、Group-Item UCB）算法，实验表明二者适用条件互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向平均用户优化的推荐系统难以刻画多样人口群体的偏好；且用户可同时属于多个交叉群体，现有方法未处理群体交集下的动态推荐与遗憾问题。

Method: 形式化带交叉群体的动态推荐问题；证明任意群体盲策略（推荐 K≥1 项）在温和条件下产生线性总遗憾；提出三类基于 UCB 的群体感知算法：i) Intersection-Fitting（对每个交集运行 UCB，具亚线性群体级与交集级遗憾，但对群体数指数依赖）；ii) Inner-Outer UCB 与 iii) Group-Item UCB 两种启发式，将不同臂分配给不同群体，并用 sleeping expert 元学习器决定某交集在每步继承哪个活跃群体的推荐。

Result: 在 Goodreads 与 BoardGameGeek 两个数据集上实验：当交集级偏好与群体级偏好高度相关时，两种启发式优于 Intersection-Fitting；当同一群体内不同交集偏好的物品不同时，Intersection-Fitting 表现更优。

Conclusion: 群体盲策略在交叉群体场景下存在线性遗憾下界，群体感知是必要的；单一算法无普适最优，应根据交集偏好与群体偏好的相关结构，在交集拟合与群体级启发式之间做选择。

Abstract: Recommendation systems that optimize for the average user can fail to capture the preferences of diverse demographic groups. We consider a dynamic recommendation system, where arriving users can belong to different demographic groups. Importantly, these groups can intersect, and a user may belong to several groups. We first show that over time, any group-blind policy (which excludes all current-user group information) recommending $K \geq 1$ items incurs linear total regret under mild conditions. In turn, we introduce three group-aware algorithms based on the classical Upper Confidence Bound algorithm (UCB): i) Intersection-Fitting, which runs UCB on each intersection, but has a sublinear group- and intersection-level regret guarantee with an exponential dependence in the number of groups; and ii) two group-level heuristics, Inner-Outer UCB and Group-Item UCB which allocate different arms to different groups, and use a sleeping expert meta-learner to choose which active group's recommendation an intersection should inherit at any given time step. We provide experimental results on two datasets, Goodreads and BoardGameGeek, and highlight that our heuristics can be preferable to the intersection-fitting algorithm when intersection-level preferences are closely related to group-level preferences, but that intersection-fitting performs better when different intersections within a same group prefer different items.

</details>


### [274] [Kernel-based Interpolatory Methods for Context Compression of Attention Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40943)
*Michel Fabrice Serret,Laura Grigori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将注意力机制置于其核对应的Hilbert空间中，把注意力表示为两个线性映射之比（其插值仍保持比值结构），据此提出基于GEIM与Q-DEIM的两种KV-cache压缩方法GAIM与Q-DAIM，并用Taylor级数、Performer和随机傅里叶特征构造代理模型将压缩代价从二次降为线性，进一步扩展到流式注意力场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制的核心瓶颈是随上下文长度呈二次复杂度增长，KV-cache压缩因此成为热点。作者希望在严谨的数学框架下理解并实现上下文压缩：通过在核关联的Hilbert空间中重新表述注意力，使线性映射的插值理论可直接用于压缩，从而为KV-cache压缩提供有理论依据、可解释且能在线化（流式）的新方法。

Method: 1) 将注意力形式化为核Hilbert空间中的表述：注意力等于两个线性映射之比，且该比值等于其插值之比，故可直接对线性映射做插值。2) 基于广义经验插值法（GEIM）和Q-离散经验插值法（Q-DEIM）提出GAIM与Q-DAIM两种KV-cache压缩方法。3) 由于原方法代价对上下文长度为二次，构建基于Taylor级数、Performer和Random Fourier Features的代理模型，使压缩在线性时间内完成。4) 推广到流式注意力：依据已插值上下文对插值节点选择施加惩罚，并利用新信息更新既有插值。

Result: 给出了残差收敛性与后续查询输出误差的数值结果；在LongBench-E基准上的初步结果显示其性能与当前最优方法相当；实验表明Q-DAIM所揭示的取值range（范围）有助于识别KV cache中信息集中的位置，从而实现高效压缩。

Conclusion: 在核Hilbert空间插值框架下，KV-cache压缩可被表述为线性映射的插值问题，GAIM/Q-DAIM及线性时间代理模型与流式扩展构成一套有理论支撑且实用的压缩方案；Q-DAIM的range信息可用于定位KV cache中的信息集中区域，为高效压缩提供指导。

Abstract: The bottleneck of attention mechanisms, which are the fundamental building block of modern language models, is their quadratic complexity in terms of context size. There has thus been an increasing interest in the compression of the context, also called KV-cache compression. In this article, we consider the problem of compressing the context of attention mechanisms by first formulating it in the Hilbert space associated with its kernel. In this formalism, attention can be represented as the ratio of two linear maps whose interpolant is also the ratio of interpolants, this allows us to consider the interpolation of the linear maps directly. We thus introduce two novel KV-cache compression methods which we call GAIM and Q-DAIM, based on the Generalized Empirical Interpolation Method (GEIM) and the Q-Discrete Empirical Interpolation Method (Q-DEIM), respectively. As the cost of these methods is quadratic in terms of context length, we propose surrogate models based on the Taylor series, the Performer and the Random Fourier Features method which allow the compression to be done in linear time. Finally, we extend this result to the streaming attention setting, providing new methods which allow one to penalize the selection of interpolation nodes based on the previously interpolated context and to update the previous interpolation based on new information. We then provide numerical results on the convergence of the residuals and the output error in future queries and preliminary results on the LongBench-E benchmark showing performance comparable to the state-of-the-art methods.
