Abstract: Historically rare ''one-in-a-generation'' storms are occurring with unprecedented frequency, causing catastrophic damage and mounting casualties, and exposing the vulnerability of infrastructure designed around short observational records. To overcome the limitations of sparse data, we present StormGen, a generative machine-learning model that transforms an observed storm's initial hours into complete, physically plausible hourly rainfall lifecycles in minutes. Evaluated on a decade of southeast Texas rainfall, StormGen produced close counterparts for 4\5% of the most severe 2\% of storms--compared to just 3\% for stochastic storm transposition--and maintained spatial coherence over full 24-hour sequences. Crucially, from a single hour of initial rainfall, the model successfully reconstructed the specific lifecycle, spatial pattern, and extreme catchment totals of Hurricane Harvey itself, despite the event being entirely absent in training. Because storm timing alone can more than double peak flows, these complete generated scenarios provide a vital mechanism to stress-test flood protections against unprecedented extremes.

</details>


### [310] [What We Keep of What We Forget: Replacement Operators and Routing Semantics in Training-Free MoE Expert Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41366)
*Ezza Ali,Sehrish Bibi,Momina Moetesum,Faisal Shafait*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在固定剪枝掩码下，系统比较免训练专家剪枝中"路由仍选中被剪专家时的替代算子"，发现最大的效应来自常被隐式忽略的幸存者门控缩放；提出闭式低秩"倾斜新手"算子，并指出评估设置会改变结论，给出五项报告清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 免训练专家剪枝涉及两个决策：剪掉哪些专家，以及当路由器仍选中被剪专家时该层计算什么。现有方法同时改变两者，导致无法归因；且许多实现细节（如幸存者门控缩放）被隐式处理，评估协议也可能系统性影响结论。

Method: 固定掩码，隔离"替代算子"这一变量，在 OLMoE、DeepSeek-V2-Lite、Moonlight（并以 Granite 作为第四种路由器）上比较三类替代算子家族，采用配对、多重比较校正的统计检验。提出"tilted novice"：对被剪专家输入的闭式低秩映射（秩-0 退化为 MoNE 的常数输出）。并考察门控缩放（是否对幸存者重新归一化）、校准数据、量化（4-bit/3-bit）与不同评估基准的影响。

Result: 在未做 top-k 重归一化训练的稀疏路由上，对幸存者门控做重归一化（常见实现选择）损失 0.8–8.5 分，并反转"重路由"与 MoNE 常数替代的优劣；在两种经重归一化训练的路由上，50% 剪枝时保留原训练尺度更优。tilted novice 在 50% 剪枝下增加 1.2–1.8% 参数，在 OLMoE 与 Moonlight 上相对常数基线提升 3.45 与 1.37 分，跨校准抽样稳定，且基本不受 4-bit/3-bit 量化影响。无单一算子占优：DeepSeek-V2-Lite 上训练尺度重路由最佳；每个被剪专家的部分拷贝在两模型上与 tilted novice 无统计差异。25% 剪枝时 MoNE 常数保留 92–97% 密集多选题准确率，但仅保留 5–65% GSM8K 与 0–17% 代码分数；校准领域对掩码的影响是重抽样影响的 4–9 倍。

Conclusion: 替代算子的"门控缩放"选择影响巨大且长期被隐含；tilted novice 以极小参数代价优于常数替代，但并不存在普适最优算子。评估基准与校准数据会实质改变结论，作者因此提出五项报告清单以规范免训练专家剪枝的评测与汇报。

Abstract: Training-free expert pruning makes two decisions: which experts to remove, and what the layer computes when its router still selects a removed expert. Published methods change both at once. Holding the mask fixed, we compare replacement operators from three families on OLMoE, DeepSeek-V2-Lite and Moonlight, with Granite as a fourth router, using paired, multiplicity-corrected tests. The largest effect is a choice usually left implicit: the survivors' gate scale. On routers trained without top-k renormalization, renormalizing the survivors' gates, a common implementation choice, costs 0.8-8.5 points and reverses whether rerouting beats MoNE's mean-output replacement; on two routers trained with renormalization, the trained scale wins at 50% pruning. We introduce the tilted novice, a closed-form low-rank map of the pruned expert's input whose rank-0 case is MoNE's constant. At 50% pruning it adds 1.2-1.8% parameters and gains 3.45 and 1.37 points over the constant on OLMoE and Moonlight, stable across calibration draws and largely unchanged by 4- and 3-bit quantization. No operator dominates: trained-scale rerouting is best on DeepSeek-V2-Lite, and a partial copy of each pruned expert is statistically indistinguishable from the tilted novice on two models. Evaluation can change conclusions: at 25% pruning MoNE's constant keeps 92-97% of dense multiple-choice accuracy but only 5-65% of GSM8K and 0-17% of code scores, and the calibration domain moves the mask 4-9 times more than resampling. We propose a five-item reporting checklist.

</details>


### [311] [Anomalies as Residuals: Cauchy Displacement Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41387)
*Yang Cao,Zihao Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Cauchy Displacement Network (CDN)，将KNN/LOF/LUNAR等邻域型检测器所利用的邻域几何"编译"为小型单隐层网络，以预测位移长度作为异常分数，实现单次前向的常数复杂度推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KNN、LOF、LUNAR等邻域型表格异常检测器精度高，但需在部署时保存全部训练集并逐点比对，推理开销与内存随数据规模增长。作者希望把这类检测器依赖的邻域几何压缩进一个小网络，使查询只需一次前向传播。

Method: CDN对每个正常点回归其到k个最近训练邻居均值的位移，用单隐层Cauchy激活网络实现，查询时以预测位移的模长作为异常分。该位移是经验mean-shift向量，其固定半径的总体对应量局部近似log密度的缩放梯度。理论上证明Cauchy单元可精确表示一类结构化Student-t专家密度的score场，并对相应的总体邻域均值映射给出单隐层Cauchy网络的构造性逼近界；还证明有界输出保证沿任意固定方向残差分数渐近线性增长。

Result: 在46个基准数据集、28个基线上，CDN在AUC-ROC与AUC-PR下均取得最佳平均排名；单CPU线程几毫秒内可打分数10^4个查询，比邻域型检测器快至多三个数量级，且模型规模与训练集大小无关。

Conclusion: 邻域几何可被有效编译为轻量前向网络，CDN在保持领先检测精度的同时大幅降低推理成本与内存占用，为部署友好、与训练集规模解耦的表格异常检测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Neighbourhood-based detectors such as KNN, LOF and LUNAR remain among the most accurate methods for tabular anomaly detection, but they compare every query with the stored training set, so their inference cost and memory grow with the data and the data must be kept at deployment. We ask whether the neighbourhood geometry these detectors exploit can be compiled into a small network that scores a query with a single forward pass. We propose the Cauchy Displacement Network (CDN), which regresses each normal point onto the mean of its $k$ nearest training neighbours with a single-hidden-layer network of Cauchy activations and scores a query by the length of its predicted displacement. This displacement is an empirical mean-shift vector, whose fixed-radius population counterpart locally approximates a scaled gradient of the log density. We show that Cauchy units exactly represent the score fields of a structured family of Student-$t$ expert densities, and establish a constructive approximation bound for the corresponding population neighbourhood-mean map using a single-hidden-layer Cauchy network. We further show that the bounded network output guarantees asymptotically linear residual-score growth along every fixed direction. On 46 benchmark datasets against 28 baselines, CDN obtains the best mean rank under both AUC-ROC and AUC-PR, while scoring $10^4$ queries in a few milliseconds on a single CPU thread, up to three orders of magnitude faster than neighbourhood-based detectors, with a model whose size does not depend on the training set.

</details>


### [312] [Certifying Retraining Regret through Residual Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41410)
*Peida Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究区间值表格在任意连续补全下，固定岭回归预测器相对全特征重训练的后悔值（regret）认证问题，给出可验证的原始行残差条件、独立于冗余特征尺度与秩的一致误差上界及可计算认证流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区间值表格中特征被部分观测，需在补全不确定下保证模型决策与全特征重训练一致。已有方法难以给出同时与冗余特征尺度、秩无关且计算可验证的误差界，且需保留全部模型特征。

Method: 以固定岭预测器为对象：主定理给出原始行残差条件，建立均匀误差界（渐近尖锐首项常数、量化泄漏稳定性）；构造满秩实例分离认证维数与特征维数；用观测行锚点使条件可计算，结合精确行几何与全局搜索得到有效上界与可行原始表见证；辅以有限尺度收缩与公共域矩回退实现结构结果；并采用联合残差精化。

Result: 四个真实任务保留全部模型特征，将20个秩三实例认证到宽度10^-5，主要中位宽度优于中心化矩；联合残差精化在76.21秒内以同等精度认证全部20个新响应无关掩码；对固定点预测器契约，纯能量法10秒内认证15个停止决策中的13个（矩法为5个），精化在91.68秒内以相同目标阈值认证全部15个。

Conclusion: 所提残差条件与认证框架可给出与尺度、秩无关的后悔界，兼具可计算性与实证有效性；精化策略显著提升认证覆盖与效率，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: We certify a fixed ridge predictor's regret against full-feature retraining over every continuous completion of an interval-valued table. Our main theorem gives verifiable original-row residual conditions for a uniform error bound independent of nuisance-feature scale and rank, with an asymptotically sharp leading constant and quantitative leakage stability. Full-rank constructions separate certification dimension from feature dimension, including examples requiring directions beyond the candidate. Observed-row anchors make the conditions computable, while exact row geometry and global search return valid upper bounds and feasible original-table witnesses. A finite-scale contraction and common-domain moment fallback implement the structural result. Four real-world tasks retain all model features and certify twenty rank-three instances to width $10^{-5}$. Their primary median widths improve over centered moments. Joint residual refinement additionally certifies all twenty new response-independent masks to the same precision within 76.21 seconds. For a concrete fixed-point predictor contract, pure energy certifies thirteen of fifteen stopping decisions in ten seconds, versus five for moments. Refinement certifies all fifteen within 91.68 seconds at the same objective threshold.

</details>


### [313] [SparseGDN: Chunk Sparse in Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41420)
*Jiedong Zhuang,Peng Zhao,Jialin Tang,Haoji Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SparseGDN：面向 GDN 长上下文预填的硬件感知稀疏化框架，通过块内结构化近似与输入相关的循环事件选择，在仅保留约 29% 循环事件的情况下实现 1.50–1.94× 预填加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GDN 具备线性时间序列建模能力，但长上下文预填仍受三重开销制约：块内 dense Delta 求解计算量大、循环状态更新频繁、中间状态存储带来大量显存与带宽消耗。

Method: 提出 SparseGDN 框架：(1) 块邻接求解器（block-adjacent solver）降低局部 Delta-solve 计算；(2) 动态选择器按 head pairs 分配循环更新预算，实现输入相关的循环事件选择，同时仍为每个 token 产生输出；(3) GPU 实现仅为被选事件物化更新，借助紧凑状态快照与组内局部交互重建 token 输出，减少计算与访存流量。

Result: 在 Qwen3.5-4B 与 Qwen3.5-9B 上、RULER 任务、4K–128K 上下文长度评测：平均保留约 29% 循环事件，相较原生 FLA kernel 取得 1.50–1.94× 的 GDN-core 预填加速，且已包含在线选择开销。

Conclusion: 结构化块内近似结合输入相关的稀疏循环事件选择，可在几乎不牺牲 token 级输出的前提下显著降低 GDN 长上下文预填开销，为线性注意力/循环模型的推理效率提供了可行的硬件感知优化路径。

Abstract: Gated Delta Networks (GDNs) offer linear-time sequence modeling, yet long-context prefill remains costly due to dense intra-chunk computation, recurrent-state updates, and intermediate state storage. We introduce SparseGDN, a hardware-aware framework that combines structured intra-chunk approximation with input-dependent recurrent-event selection while producing outputs for every token. A block-adjacent solver reduces local Delta-solve computation, while a dynamic selector allocates recurrent-update budgets across head pairs. The GPU implementation materializes updates only for selected events and reconstructs token outputs using compact state snapshots and group-local interactions, reducing computation and memory traffic. We evaluate SparseGDN on Qwen3.5-4B and Qwen3.5-9B across RULER tasks and context lengths from 4K to 128K. Retaining approximately 29% of recurrent events on average, SparseGDN achieves 1.50-1.94x GDN-core prefill speedups over native FLA kernels, including online selection overhead.

</details>


### [314] [A Unified XAI-NSAI Analytical Framework for Trustworthy IoT Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41424)
*Hugues Leger,Giovani R. Abuaitah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个围绕五个互补维度组织的统一分析框架，用于联合考察XAI与神经符号AI（NSAI）方法在可信物联网异常检测中的互补作用，并通过三个代表性研究与一个医疗物联网系统分别验证其分析效用与设计效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网设备广泛部署于关键环境，异常检测虽必要但仅有准确检测并不充分；实际部署还需提供解释、融合领域知识与推理、满足物联网资源约束并支持人类决策。现有工作虽日益引入可解释AI（XAI）或神经符号AI（NSAI），但二者常被独立看待，缺乏对其互补作用的系统理解，因而需要统一视角。

Method: 提出一个统一分析框架，围绕五个互补维度组织，用于刻画现有方法、识别能力缺口与设计权衡；具体维度内容摘要未明确给出。采用案例应用方式进行双重验证：以三个代表性研究检验框架的分析效用，以医疗物联网异常检测系统的应用说明其设计效用。

Result: 框架可为现有方法提供结构化的比较与刻画基础，能够识别能力缺口与设计权衡，并指导面向不同物联网应用的新型异常检测系统设计；在三个代表性研究与医疗物联网场景中分别展示了分析效用与设计效用。

Conclusion: XAI与NSAI应被联合而非孤立地考虑，以理解其在支持可信物联网异常检测中的互补角色；所提五维统一框架可作为方法刻画、缺口识别与系统设计的通用依据。

Abstract: The widespread deployment of Internet of Things (IoT) devices in critical environments makes anomaly detection essential for identifying abnormal behaviors and supporting timely investigation and response. However, accurate detection alone is insufficient. Practical IoT deployments must also provide explanations, incorporate domain knowledge and reasoning, operate under IoT constraints, and support human decision making. Although recently proposed approaches increasingly incorporate Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) or Neuro-Symbolic Artificial Intelligence (NSAI), XAI and NSAI methods are often considered independently. We argue that they should be considered together to better understand their complementary roles in supporting trustworthy IoT anomaly detection. To address this gap, we propose a unified analytical framework organized around five complementary dimensions. The framework provides a structured basis for characterizing existing approaches, identifying capability gaps and design trade-offs, and guiding the design of new anomaly detection systems for different IoT applications. We demonstrate the framework's analytical utility by applying it to three representative studies and illustrate its design utility through its application to a Medical IoT anomaly detection system.

</details>


### [315] [From Representation to Readout: Mechanistic Analysis of Membership Signals in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41445)
*Wenxi Dai,Jingyu Hu,Yanting Yang,Mengnan Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对LLM中的成员信号做机制性研究，提出DUG三项互补指标分别量化内部表征中的可解码成员信息与读出可及性，发现在SFT与RL类成员推断方法下成员特异性效应分别体现在"信息量+读出"与"读出"层面，并用激活补丁定位到中间层；在RLVR数据污染场景下，行为变化可在不伴随似然类成员信号上升的情况下出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常把成员推断信号笼统描述为"记忆化"，但其底层机制涉及内部信息如何表征与被模型读出两条路径，二者未被区分，导致对成员信号来源与可检测性的理解不充分。

Method: 提出DUG指标组：Decodable Information Score（内部表征可解码的成员信息量）、Used Information Score（模型实际使用的成员信息量）、Readout Accessibility Gap（可解码信息与读出暴露度之间的差距）；在SFT与基于RL的成员推断方法上对比分析，并用激活补丁进行层级定位；进一步扩展到可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）数据污染场景。

Result: SFT下成员特异性效应同时体现在可解码信息量与读出两方面；RL类成员推断方法下主要体现于读出；激活补丁将成员特异性效应定位到中间层；RLVR污染中，即使训练奖励与参考解无关，行为变化仍可出现，而基于似然的成员推断信号并未相应增强。

Conclusion: 成员信号应被分解为"表征可解码性"与"读出可及性"两个维度理解；不同训练范式在机制上作用于不同环节；行为层面的训练影响可能与似然类成员推断信号解耦，提示现有成员推断指标存在盲区。

Abstract: Membership inference aims to determine whether a particular example was included in a model's training data. Such membership signals are often broadly described as memorization, while the underlying mechanisms can involve both how information is represented internally and how it is accessed by the model to produce behaviors. We therefore present a mechanistic study of membership signals in large language models (LLMs). We introduce Decodable Information Score, Used Information Score, and Readout Accessibility Gap (DUG), a set of complementary measures that separately quantify membership information decodable from internal representations and the extent to which it is exposed through the model's readout. Across supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL)-based membership inference methods, membership-specific effects appear in both the amount of decodable information and readout under SFT, while under RL-based membership inference methods they appear primarily in readout. Activation patching further localizes this membership-specific effect to intermediate layers. To examine whether these findings extend to a more general RL setting, we further study reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) contamination, where the training reward is independent of the reference solution. In this setting, we find that behavioral changes can emerge without a corresponding increase in likelihood-based membership inference signals.

</details>


### [316] [When Everyone Can Build a Model: Mechanistic Inference in the Age of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41454)
*Marvin van Aalst,Adèle H. Ribeiro,Anna Matuszyńska*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AI辅助与深度学习降低了生物系统数学模型构建门槛，使"灰箱"（半机制+学习结构）模型普及；但可构建性≠可解释性。文章主张用因果推断处理"变量间因果结构"的可识别性问题，用集合（ensemble）分析处理"响应函数形式"的不确定性，从而把单一良好拟合转化为关于"被支持/仍开放/可由何种实验判定"的明确说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低的技术门槛使具备领域知识但缺乏建模训练的研究者能构建半机制灰箱模型，但预测成功不能证明所推断机制即真实生成机制，学习组件可能吸收机制部分的误差；且许多生物建模问题欠定，可存在多个甚至无穷多个等价模型。该歧义来自两类建模选择（因果结构、响应函数），需在方法论上予以正视与处理。

Method: 提出双管齐下的框架：（1）对变量间影响关系（因果图结构）采用因果推断，明确哪些因果关系与干预效应可从数据与先验知识中被识别；（2）对响应函数形式的不确定性采用集合（ensemble）分析，量化同等拟合优度模型所允许的不确定性与行为范围。

Result: 概念性/方法论述文，未报告具体实验或定量结果；其"结果"为论证因果推断与集合分析能互补地界定可识别部分、开放部分与判别性实验。

Conclusion: 灰箱模型的预测成功不足以确立机制真实性；应以因果可识别性分析刻画结构不确定性、以集合分析刻画函数形式不确定性，将单一最优拟合升级为对知识边界与关键判别实验的显式陈述。

Abstract: Modern deep learning and AI-assisted code generation are lowering the technical barrier to building mathematical models of biological systems that reproduce complex observations. This lets researchers with domain expertise build semi-mechanistic, hybrid models constrained by prior knowledge and augmented with learned structure without extensive training in modelling. However, easier construction does not make interpretation easier: predictive success of such so-called grey-box models does not establish that an inferred mechanism corresponds to the one that generated the data, and the learned component can absorb errors in the mechanistic part. Many biological modelling problems are underdetermined, so several, possibly infinitely many, models may reproduce the same data equally well. This ambiguity enters at two modelling choices: which variables can affect others, and which response function describes the underlying mechanism. For the first choice, causal inference provides a formal framework stating which causal relationships and intervention effects are identifiable from the data and available knowledge. For the second, ensemble analyses quantify the uncertainty and the range of behaviours still allowed by models that fit equally well. Together, they turn a single good fit into an explicit account of what is supported, what remains open, and which experiment would decide.

</details>


### [317] [Improving the Optimization Landscape of Matrix Completion with $ε$-close Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41455)
*Ziye Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作指出在矩阵补全（MC）这一非凸恢复问题中，通过向观测掩码的零空间注入 ε 级扰动，可构造带噪声的矩阵感知替代问题，其解与真实矩阵保持 O(ε) 距离，且满足有效的 RIP 常数，从而可在无需显式采样率与不相干性假设下获得定量恢复保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸优化是机器学习与 AI 的核心，但相比凸优化仍缺乏通用且有原则的理论框架。作者希望在矩阵补全这一经典非凸恢复设定下，从数学上阐明"噪声扰动 + 过参数化"组合的重要性。MC 因观测条目有限而极具挑战性。

Method: 向观测掩码的零空间注入 ε 级扰动，将 MC 转化为带噪声的矩阵感知（matrix sensing）替代问题；证明其解与真实解 O(ε) 接近且具备有效的受限等距性质（RIP）常数，进而借助 RIP 应用张量框架并获得理论保证。在每条矩阵元素独立观测的假设下建立定量恢复保证。

Result: 证明了扰动后替代问题的解与 ground truth 保持 O(ε) 距离，且具有有效 RIP 常数；由此可在不显式要求采样率或不相干性条件的情况下给出定量恢复保证，并讨论了额外假设如何进一步增强结果。

Conclusion: 随机性/噪声可改善优化景观的曲率，使过参数化（大）模型更好地分离信号与噪声；这为结合噪声扰动与过参数化的非凸优化提供了原则性数学框架。

Abstract: Non-convex optimization is central to machine learning and AI, yet unlike its convex counterpart, it still lacks a general and principled framework. In this work, we try to highlight the importance of combining noisy perturbations with over-parametrization mathematically under the classic setting of matrix completion (MC). Matrix completion is an important non-convex recovery problem in which only a subset of a matrix's entries is observed, and the remaining entries must be recovered using low-rank constraints. This problem is notoriously difficult because observations are limited. However, we show that by injecting $ε$-level perturbations into the nullspace of the observation mask, we can construct noisy matrix sensing surrogates whose solutions remain $\mathcal{O}(ε)$-close to the ground truth with valid restricted isometry property (RIP) constants. The existence of RIP further enables powerful tensor frameworks to be applied with theoretical guarantees. This approach reflects a broader empirical trend in which stochasticity or noise improves the curvature of the optimization landscape, allowing over-parameterized (a.k.a large) models to better separate signal from noise. Assuming each matrix entry is observed independently, we establish quantitative recovery guarantees without explicit requirements on sampling rate or incoherence, and discuss how such assumptions can further strengthen our results.

</details>


### [318] [NormDetect: Anchored Calibration for Low-False-Positive Detection of Machine-Generated Web Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41459)
*Hanshuai Cui,Fanshuai Meng,Zhiqing Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 NormDetect：将学习得到的分章节检测头锚定到归一化零样本分数，通过在人写文本 FPR 上限下选择混合权重并仅用人类文本校准单一阈值，在 RAID 的 8 个章节、11 种攻击下于 0.1% 校准 FPR 处将召回率较归一化 Binoculars 提升 5.0 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平台需以极低假正率（FPR）筛查大模型生成文本，因为每一次误报都会伤害真实人类作者。零样本检测器（如 Binoculars）依赖冻结语言模型的打分，但同形字等不可见编辑会破坏分词，使机器文本落入人类分数区间。Unicode 归一化虽能消解此类编辑，但在严格 FPR 下仍漏检约一半机器文本，且在"无可清理"的攻击下失效。

Method: 观察发现 Falcon-7B 打分对下基于 token 统计的注意力头在同等 FPR 下召回低于归一化分数，且部分章节对人类帖误报过多。据此提出 NormDetect：以归一化零样本分数为锚，结合学习得到的 per-section 检测头；在训练折上于人类 FPR 上限约束下选取混合权重融合二者分数，并仅在人类文本上校准单一阈值。

Result: 在 RAID 基准 8 个章节、11 种攻击设定下，NormDetect 在 0.1% 校准 FPR 处相比归一化 Binoculars 召回率提升 5.0 个百分点；各章节人类 FPR 上界均不超过 1%，且在该约束下无其他检测器召回率更高。

Conclusion: 将学习型分章节检测头锚定到归一化零样本分数，并配合仅用人类文本的阈值校准，可在严格低 FPR 约束下稳健提升机器文本召回，同时严格控制人类误报。

Abstract: Web platforms screen posts for text written by large language models, and every false flag hits a human writer. Detectors must therefore run at a very low false positive rate (FPR). Zero-shot detectors such as Binoculars score text with frozen language models, but invisible edits such as homoglyphs shatter tokenization and push machine text into the human range. Unicode normalization undoes these edits, yet the normalized score still misses half of the machine text at a strict FPR, under attacks that leave nothing to clean. We find that with the Falcon-7B scoring pair, heads on token statistics recall less than the normalized score at this FPR, and several flag too many human posts in some sections. We propose NormDetect, which anchors a learned per-section head to the normalized zero-shot score. A blend weight chosen on training folds under a human FPR cap combines the two scores, and one threshold is calibrated on human text only. On eight sections of the RAID benchmark with eleven attacks, NormDetect raises recall at a 0.1% calibrated FPR by 5.0 points over normalized Binoculars. Its human FPR bound stays at most 1% in every section, and no other detector within this bound recalls as much.

</details>


### [319] [RetryRoute: Retry-Aware Model Routing for Agentic Image Editing Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41460)
*Hanshuai Cui,Zhiqing Tang,Zhi Yao,Jun Luo,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现开放图像编辑模型存在"粘性失败"现象（失败后再试成功率仅为首次的17-53%），据此提出RetryRoute——一个考虑失败历史的检索/路由框架，将重试、升级和放弃统一规划进成本中，在成功率和SLO满足率上均显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web智能体通过"批评-重试"循环调用开放编辑模型，模型成本相差一个数量级以上，服务需在每次编辑被拒后决定重试同模型、升级到别的模型还是放弃。现有路由器每次请求只选一个模型，级联方法按固定顺序升级，二者都把重试当作独立抽样，忽略了失败对后续重试的影响。

Method: 作者先在六个开放模型、三个基准、两个评判器上量化"粘性失败"：失败后再试的成功率仅为首次尝试的17-53%。基于此提出RetryRoute，在各模型的失败状态条件上估计成功概率，并在失败状态空间上规划重试、升级与放弃，同时把实时队列等待计入成本，使同一规划在负载下转化为负载感知的调度。

Result: 在主预算设置下，RetryRoute在环内评判器上的成功率在八组配置中平均比十一个先验基线中最强的高出2.8个百分点；在GPU服务测试平台上，各负载下SLO内完成编辑的比例比最佳先验基线提升5.0-6.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 失败并非独立同分布，重试具有强相关性（粘性失败），忽视这一点会使现有路由器与级联策略次优；将失败历史纳入建模并进行显式规划、结合负载感知的队列等待，可同时提升编辑成功率与服务级目标达成率。

Abstract: Web agents increasingly edit images by calling open editing models in a critique-and-retry loop, and these models differ in cost by more than an order of magnitude. After every rejected edit, a service must decide whether to retry the same model, escalate to another or give up. Existing routers pick one model per request, and cascades escalate in a fixed order. However, both treat a retry as an independent draw. We identify sticky failures, meaning that a retry after a failure succeeds only 17-53% as often as a first try across six open models, three benchmarks and two judges. Based on this finding, we propose RetryRoute, a retry-aware router that estimates each model's chance of success given the request's failure history. It plans retries, escalations and abandonment over failure states and adds live queue waits to the cost, so the same plan becomes load-aware dispatch. At the main budget, RetryRoute's success under the in-loop judge exceeds the strongest of eleven prior baselines by 2.8 points on average over eight groups. On a GPU serving testbed it raises the share of edits delivered within the service-level objective by 5.0-6.3 points over the best prior baseline at every load.

</details>


### [320] [When Links Mislead: Label-Free Structural Polarity for Zero-Shot Classification of Web Pages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41464)
*Hanshuai Cui,Zhiqing Tang,Zhi Yao,Weijia Jia,Jun Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出零样本网页分类方法 ZeroPolar，用可无标签计算的"结构极性"统计量对一次带符号传播进行门控，训练-free 融合文本原型分数与任意数量 LLM 标签；半监督版本 ZeroPolar-T 在 11 个图上平均准确率 73.99%，比 LLM-BP 高 6.01 点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字图书馆、在线商店、高校站点等平台需在人工标注前对新页面分类。LLM 可直接从文本打标但噪声大；链接信息仅在相连页面同类时才有矫正作用。现有零样本方法要么忽略链接，要么像标签传播/GNN 那样把节点拉向邻居（异配时有害），或以 LLM 估计的同配率加权传播（需额外调用且跨模型漂移）。

Method: 定义"结构极性"：沿链接方向上 LLM 标签与文本原型预测的一致性超额量，无需真实标签即可读出。据此提出 ZeroPolar：用该统计量对一次带符号传播步骤进行门控，训练-free 地混合原型分数与任意数量 LLM 标签；ZeroPolar-T 在过半页面带标签时，用文本分类器对标签去噪。

Result: 每页一个 GPT-4o 标签时，ZeroPolar-T 在 11 个图上取得最高平均准确率 73.99%，较 LLM-BP 提升 6.01 点，并显著优于全部 18 个基线；仅 5% 页面有 LLM 标签时，训练-free 的 ZeroPolar 比在这些标签上训练的 GNN 高 10.80 点。

Conclusion: 链接进入预测的符号（结构极性）可无需真实标签读出，据此做单步带符号门控传播，优于无差别拉近邻居或依赖 LLM 估计同配率的做法，在极低标签率下仍显著超过监督 GNN。

Abstract: Web platforms such as digital libraries, online stores and university sites must classify new pages before anyone labels them. Large language models (LLMs) can label each page from its text, but these labels are noisy, and links could correct them only where linked pages share classes. Existing zero-shot methods ignore the links, or pull a page toward its neighbours as label propagation and graph neural networks (GNNs) do, which hurts where linked pages differ in class. Others weight propagation by an LLM-estimated homophily that costs extra calls and drifts across LLMs. We find that the sign with which links should enter a prediction, which we call structural polarity, is readable without true labels. It is the excess agreement of LLM labels and text-prototype predictions along links. We thus propose ZeroPolar, which gates one signed propagation step by this statistic. Training-free ZeroPolar mixes prototype scores with any number of LLM labels, and ZeroPolar-T denoises the labels with a text classifier once half of the pages carry one. With one GPT-4o label per page, ZeroPolar-T reaches the highest mean accuracy on eleven graphs (73.99%), 6.01 points above LLM-BP and significantly above all eighteen baselines we compare. With LLM labels on only 5% of the pages, training-free ZeroPolar leads a GNN trained on these labels by 10.80 points.

</details>


### [321] [Enhancing Sentiment Classification on Small Datasets through Data Augmentation and Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41518)
*Mahmoud S. Mayaleh,Samer A. Mayaleh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在低资源情感分类任务上系统比较了EDA、回译与上下文词替换三种数据增强策略，并分别搭配传统分类器（LR、RF）与BERT，发现所有增强均有效，但上下文增强对BERT增益最稳定，EDA与回译对传统分类器更有利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小规模情感分类面临数据稀缺问题，限制模型泛化能力；现有研究缺乏针对不同模型类型与数据集规模的增强方法系统对比，本文旨在填补这一空白，为方法选择提供指导。

Method: 采用经验性对比研究：以低资源情感数据集为对象，结合近期研究结果总结与针对性的一对一（head-to-head）实验，横向比较三种增强策略——Easy Data Augmentation (EDA)、回译（back-translation）、上下文词替换（nlpaug风格），并分别与传统机器学习分类器（逻辑回归、随机森林）及Transformer模型（BERT）组合评估。

Result: 所有数据增强方法均带来性能提升；上下文增强（contextual token substitution）对BERT模型增益最一致；EDA与回译对传统分类器收益更大。

Conclusion: 数据增强的效果依赖于模型类型与数据集规模，应据此选择适配的增强技术；本研究为小数据集情感分类的增强策略选择提供了实证依据与实践指导。

Abstract: Small-scale sentiment classification often suffers from data scarcity, which limits the generalization ability of the models. This study evaluates and compares the effectiveness of three data augmentation strategies: Easy Data Augmentation (EDA), back-translation, and contextual token substitution (nlpaug-style), with both traditional machine learning classifiers (Logistic Regression, Random Forest) and transformer-based models (BERT). We perform a comprehensive empirical comparison with low-resource sentiment datasets by summarizing the results of recent studies and performing targeted head-to-head experiments. Our findings indicate that all augmentation methods improve performance. Contextual augmentation yields the most consistent gains for BERT models, while EDA and back-translation provide greater benefits for traditional classifiers. These insights help guide the selection of data augmentation techniques tailored to model type and dataset size, filling a critical gap in research on data augmentation for sentiment classification on small datasets.

</details>


### [322] [Spectral Haar Binarizer: Compact Boolean Contrast Representations for Hyperspectral Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41478)
*Michał Romaszewski,Przemysław Głomb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Spectral Haar Binarizer (SHB)，用类Haar对比特征对高光谱信号做布尔化，替代逐波段独立二值化，并配合AdaBoost式特征选择获得紧凑判别性谓词。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tsetlin Machine可解释、硬件友好，但处理连续高光谱数据需转为布尔表示；常规逐波段离散化会丢失相邻波长间的关系。

Method: SHB基于选定光谱区间之间的差分构造布尔谓词（受Haar-like对比特征启发），并用AdaBoost风格的选择流程筛选出少量强判别性光谱谓词；在五类异质高光谱数据集（血迹检测、农业与城市遥感、雨林树种分类）上，对二分类与多分类任务与逐波段二值化基线比较，涉及TM与RF等分类器，并用贝叶斯与秩次分析验证稳健性。

Result: SHB在二分类与多分类任务上均稳定优于标准逐波段二值化，并在各分类器中取得最强或接近最强结果；二分类下TM-SHB平均表现最佳，多分类下RF-SHB最强；贝叶斯与秩次分析表明优势跨数据集稳健，非个别案例驱动。

Conclusion: 连续高光谱测量的表示方式是高效分类的关键环节；基于对比的布尔特征可在信息丰富的传感测量与紧凑的逻辑化处理之间搭建有效桥梁。

Abstract: Tsetlin Machines offer an interpretable, hardware-friendly alternative to conventional machine learning models, but applying them to continuous hyperspectral measurements requires converting spectral signals into Boolean representations. Standard binarisation methods typically discretise each spectral band independently, potentially discarding important relationships between neighbouring wavelengths. In this paper, we introduce the Spectral Haar Binarizer (SHB), a contrast-based spectral representation method that constructs Boolean predicates from differences between selected spectral intervals. Inspired by Haar-like contrast features, the method uses an AdaBoost-style selection procedure to obtain a compact set of discriminative spectral predicates. We evaluate SHB on five heterogeneous hyperspectral datasets covering forensic blood detection, agricultural and urban remote sensing, and rainforest tree-species classification. Across binary and multiclass tasks, SHB consistently outperforms standard per-band binarisation strategies and provides the strongest or near-strongest results across classifiers. In binary tasks, TM-SHB achieves the best average performance, while RF-SHB is strongest in multiclass settings. Bayesian and rank-based analyses confirm that SHB's advantage is robust across datasets and not driven by isolated cases. The results show that representing continuous hyperspectral measurements is a central processing step for efficient classification. That contrast-based Boolean features provide an effective bridge between information-rich sensor measurements and compact, logic-based processing.

</details>


### [323] [PCD: Predictive Coding Dynamics for Irregularly Sampled Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41506)
*Tianrui Gou,Jiayangmei Xu,Xingzhou Zhang,Lei Sha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出预测编码动力学（PCD），一种用于不规则连续动态模型的残差反馈机制，通过对掩码符号残差施加能量惩罚来改进隐状态识别，在ODE-RNN与Latent ODE上提升稀疏不规则采样下的预测与轨迹恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景观测稀疏、不规则且异步，而动态系统连续演化，未来值依赖部分观测的轨迹，因此需推断观测间的隐状态演化；现有连续时间模型缺乏对预测误差的有效反馈利用。

Method: PCD维护先前的重建结果，由重建预测已观测条目，并将掩码符号残差通过能量惩罚项融入状态识别；机制以ODE-RNN和Latent ODE为载体实现，支持基于事件的隐状态更新与潜状态识别。

Result: 在预测与轨迹恢复任务上，PCD在稀疏不规则采样条件下改进连续动态骨干模型；结果表明当观测约束当前轨迹时，预测误差反馈可提升隐状态估计。

Conclusion: PCD提供了一个残差反馈框架，在连续时间序列建模中同时支持轨迹生成、观测一致性检查与状态精化。

Abstract: Dynamic systems evolve continuously over time, whereas observations in real scenarios are often sparse, irregular, and asynchronous. Accurate prediction therefore requires inferring latent state evolution between observations, as future values depend on partially observed trajectories. We propose Predictive Coding Dynamics (PCD), a residual feedback mechanism for irregular continuous dynamic models. PCD maintains previous reconstructions, predicts observed entries from them, and incorporates masked signed residuals into state recognition through an energy penalty. The mechanism is implemented with ODE-RNN and Latent ODE, supporting event-based hidden updates and latent state recognition. Experiments on forecasting and trajectory recovery tasks show that PCD improves continuous dynamic backbones under sparse and irregular sampling. The results further indicate that predictive error feedback improves latent state estimation when observations constrain the current trajectory. These results suggest that PCD provides a residual feedback framework that jointly supports trajectory generation, observation consistency checking, and state refinement in continuous-time series modeling. The source code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/PCD-for-paper-92F1/.

</details>


### [324] [Does Reasoning Make Language Models Agree?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41691)
*Mila Gorecki,Moritz Hardt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文区分"推理作为干预"与"推理作为观察行为"，发现主动增加推理努力提升模型间一致性，而观察到的推理链更长则对应更低的跨模型一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型存在"单一化"（错误相关）与"多元性"（同样准确却决策冲突）两种对立隐忧；推理模型的兴起使这一争论复杂化：推理可能因共同锚定显著特征或相似路径而提高一致性，也可能因随机推理轨迹引入分支点而增加多样性。作者旨在化解这一张力。

Method: 以"一致性"为度量，并与由模型准确率推导的期望一致性作基准比较；区分两种设定：(1) 受控改变推理努力的干预式比较；(2) 按观察到的推理长度分组的行为式比较。实验覆盖两家开发者的九个模型、三项分类任务，并在控制准确率差异后检验结论稳健性。

Result: 启用推理后，同一批个体上模型间一致性上升；但诱发更长推理链的个体，其跨模型预测反而更不一致。两种模式在扣除准确率差异后依然存在。

Conclusion: "要求模型多推理"促进一致性，"观察到模型推理更长"则预示分歧；推理对模型同质化的影响取决于将其视为干预还是观察行为，二者不可混同。

Abstract: Language models face two opposing concerns: monoculture, in which models err in correlated ways, and multiplicity, in which equally accurate models make conflicting decisions. The rise of reasoning models has complicated this debate: On the one hand, reasoning may increase model agreement if models anchor on the same salient features or follow similar reasoning paths. On the other hand, stochastic reasoning traces may introduce additional branch points leading to more diverse predictions. We resolve this tension by distinguishing reasoning as an intervention from reasoning as an observed behavior. We compare agreement under controlled changes in reasoning effort with agreement across observed reasoning lengths, measuring both against the agreement expected from model accuracy. Across nine models from two developers and three classification tasks, enabling reasoning increases agreement between models evaluated on the same individuals. Yet the individuals who elicit longer reasoning traces receive less consistent predictions across models. Both patterns persist after accounting for differences in accuracy. Asking models to reason more thus promotes agreement, while observing them reason longer signals disagreement.

</details>


### [325] [Infinite ResNets are Gaussian Mean ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41514)
*Louis-Pierre Chaintron,Lénaïc Chizat,Javier Maass*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文给出ResNet在大规模极限下训练动力学的一个简洁描述：极限动力学等价于对带高斯初始权重的Neural Mean ODE做梯度下降，避免了DMFT的非马尔可夫复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作用动力学平均场理论(DMFT)刻画深度网络大规模极限下的训练，但得到非马尔可夫动力系统，难以分析且掩盖了原动力学的优化结构。作者希望获得更简单、结构更清晰的极限描述。

Method: 针对标准形状缩放以及可实现最大(局部)特征更新的1/depth分支缩放下的ResNet，从随机初始化出发用梯度下降(GD)训练；在宽度、深度、嵌入维、输入输出维联合趋于无穷的极限下进行分析，覆盖总体风险与经验风险GD，推导依赖ResNet形状与数据协方差谱的近似标度律；并推广讨论混合专家(MoE)模型。

Result: 极限动力学被刻画为在高斯初始化权重下对Neural Mean ODE的普通GD；给出以ResNet形状和数据协方差谱为函数的近似标度律；MoE模型同样具有直观的嵌套Mean ODE描述。

Conclusion: 大规模极限下ResNet训练可用简洁的Mean ODE视角理解，保留了优化结构且便于分析，并具有向MoE等结构的可扩展性。

Abstract: Understanding the training dynamics of deep neural networks in large-scale limits is a question both of foundational and of practical importance. Prior works have obtained such descriptions through dynamical mean field theory (DMFT), yielding a system of non-Markovian dynamics that are challenging to analyze and that obscure the optimization structure of the original dynamics. In this paper, we obtain instead a simple description of the large-scale limit training dynamics for ResNets in standard shape scalings and in the $1/\text{depth}$ branch scaling that allows maximal (local) feature updates. For a model trained with gradient descent (GD) from a random initialization, the limit dynamics is simply GD on a Neural Mean ODE with Gaussian initial weights. Our analysis covers population or empirical risk GD in the joint limit of large width, depth and embedding, input, and output dimensions. We obtain approximation scaling laws that depend on the shape of the ResNet and the covariance spectrum of the data. We also illustrate the flexibility of our approach by discussing Mixture of Expert models, where likewise the limit admits an intuitive (nested) Mean ODE description.

</details>


### [326] [UpLoop: Training Looped Transformers via Upcycling at No Extra Pretraining Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41720)
*Cheng Liang,Tengfei Zhang,Ya Zhang,Yanfeng Wang,Zhouhan Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UpLoop是一种预训练调度策略：先用浅层Transformer，再通过复制已学层逐步增加深度（upcycling），把早期节省的算力用于后期共享中间块的循环重复计算。在最终参数量、训练token总数和训练FLOPs与标准稠密基线完全一致的条件下，提升循环Transformer的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型通过共享权重的重复计算增强推理，可在不增加独立参数的情况下提升执行深度；但每多一次前向都会增加训练FLOPs，使这些收益的训练成本更高。论文希望在训练成本不增加的前提下获得循环模型的收益。

Method: 提出UpLoop预训练调度：①从浅层Transformer开始训练；②逐步通过复制已学习的层来扩展深度（层复制式upcycling）；③将训练早期因模型较浅而节省的算力，用于训练后期对共享中间块的重复执行。整个方案在最终参数量、总训练token数与训练FLOPs上与标准稠密基线严格匹配，从而构成等成本对比。

Result: 在多个模型规模上，语言建模与下游任务性能均有提升：1.3B参数规模下，22个下游任务的平均得分从22.99提升至27.52。经监督微调后，这一增益也延伸到数学推理任务。

Conclusion: 在训练算力严格对齐的公平设定下，UpLoop能有效释放循环Transformer的推理增益，说明"先浅后深+算力复用的循环计算"是一种可行的性能提升预训练策略。

Abstract: Looped language models can strengthen reasoning through repeated computation with shared weights, increasing execution depth without adding independent parameters. However, each additional pass increases training floating-point operations (FLOPs), making these gains more expensive to obtain. To address this cost, we propose UpLoop, a pretraining schedule that uses upcycling to train looped Transformers while exactly matching a standard dense baseline in final parameter count, total training tokens, and training FLOPs. Specifically, UpLoop begins with a shallow Transformer, progressively grows its depth by copying learned layers, and uses the computation saved early to support repeated execution of a shared middle block later in training. Experiments across model scales show improvements in language modeling and downstream performance, with the average score across 22 downstream tasks increasing from 22.99 to 27.52 at 1.3B parameters. These gains also extend to mathematical reasoning after supervised fine-tuning.

</details>


### [327] [FOCUS: First-Order Control Using Optimal Transport Supervision for Imitation from Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41540)
*João A. Cândido Ramos,Lionel Blondé,Hugues Vinzant,Jacopo Castellini,Alexandros Kalousis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FOCUS 将局部最优传输对齐与短时域可微 actor-critic 学习相结合，在仅有状态观测、无时间对应关系的示范下实现稳定高效的模仿学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从观测模仿（IfO）需在无已知时间对应关系、仅有状态示范的条件下恢复行为；可微模拟器虽提供路径式策略梯度，但对长时域、接触丰富的轨迹整体求导会导致优化不稳定。

Method: 用最优传输将智能体 rollout 窗口与多个采样的专家片段进行局部对齐；采用软 best-of-K 目标强调局部相容匹配；由传输代价构造可微模仿奖励；再用学习到的 critic 在 rollout 时域之外进行价值自举。方法无需时间对齐轨迹、专家对齐初始化或 teacher forcing。

Result: 在 DFlex 的接触丰富运动任务（含由 152 个肌肉激活控制的肌肉骨骼人形）上，FOCUS 相比对抗式模仿观测方法与已有可微模仿基线，提升了样本效率与训练稳定性。

Conclusion: 局部传输对齐加短时域可微 AC 是缓解长轨迹求导不稳定、实现无对齐 IfO 的有效范式，可扩展至高维肌肉控制等复杂接触任务。

Abstract: Imitation learning from observations must recover behavior from state-only demonstrations without known temporal correspondence. Differentiable simulators provide pathwise policy gradients, but differentiating through long, contact-rich trajectories can make optimization unstable. We introduce FOCUS, which combines local optimal-transport alignment with short-horizon differentiable actor-critic learning. FOCUS matches agent rollout windows to multiple sampled expert segments using a soft best-of-$K$ objective that emphasizes locally compatible matches. The resulting transport-based costs provide differentiable imitation rewards, while a learned critic bootstraps value beyond the rollout horizon. FOCUS requires neither temporally aligned trajectories nor expert-aligned initialization or teacher forcing. Across contact-rich locomotion tasks in DFlex, including a musculoskeletal humanoid controlled by 152 muscle activations, FOCUS improves sample efficiency and training stability over adversarial imitation-from-observation methods and prior differentiable imitation baselines.

</details>


### [328] [Leakage-Controlled Structure-Preserving Augmentation and Template-Aware Evaluation for Bangla Symptom-Based Disease Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41733)
*Sabah Nushra,Shovan Samanta Turzo,Moinul Hossain,Md Kishor Morol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在含725个条目、85种疾病、172个二值症状的孟加拉语症状-疾病小样本基准上，检验"仅训练集非对称伯努利扰动"数据增强对不同模型族的效果：提升因模型族而异（树模型显著、朴素贝叶斯不稳健），且标准基准存在严重模板邻近性，需结合Hamming距离审视准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有孟加拉语症状-疾病预测研究多在小规模、类别不平衡的结构化基准上评测，且样本间常仅相差1–2个症状，导致数据增强与评测的可信度存疑；作者希望系统检验训练期非对称伯努利扰动是否能真正改善分类，并区分增强效应与调参效应。

Method: 采用725个唯一画像、85类疾病、172个二值症状的数据集；10次重复的"真实画像"留出评测，配合按父组分组的内层交叉验证；2×2析因设计将数据增强与超参数调优分离；对照设置包括精确复制、随机过采样、类别加权与SMOTEN；另进行Hamming距离审计、同病1-bit净化挑战集评测，并考察缺失/伪症状鲁棒性及朴素贝叶斯解释的忠实性。

Result: BNB取得最高平均准确率97.50%，但相对调优后原始训练的增益未通过Holm校正；提升树模型收益显著，XGBoost、LightGBM、CatBoost分别提升18.10、28.45、10.09个百分点；Hamming审计显示91.0%的标准留出画像与训练数据在1个症状比特以内；在同病1-bit净化挑战集上准确率下降，但XGBoost与LightGBM仍保留较大增益。

Conclusion: 伯努利扰动对部分模型族（尤其是提升树）有用，但对BNB的增益不具统计稳健性；由于基准样本与训练模板高度邻近，报告准确率时必须同时披露模板邻近性（如Hamming距离）与净化挑战集结果，避免高估模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Bangla symptom-based disease prediction is commonly evaluated on a small, imbalanced structured benchmark containing many profiles that differ by only one or two symptoms. We test whether train-only asymmetric Bernoulli perturbation improves classification using 725 unique profiles spanning 85 diseases and 172 binary symptoms. Experiments use ten repeated real-profile holdouts and parent-grouped inner cross-validation. The 2 x 2 design separates augmentation from hyperparameter tuning, with exact duplication, random oversampling, class weighting, and SMOTEN as balancing controls. For Bernoulli Naive Bayes (BNB), the gain over tuned raw training does not withstand Holm correction, although BNB attains the highest mean accuracy (97.50%). The boosted-tree models benefit substantially: XGBoost, LightGBM, and CatBoost improve by 18.10, 28.45, and 10.09 percentage points, respectively. A Hamming-distance audit places 91.0% of standard held-out profiles within one symptom bit of the training data. Accuracy decreases on a same-disease 1-bit-purged challenge; nevertheless, XGBoost and LightGBM retain large augmentation gains. We also evaluate robustness to missing and spurious symptoms and the faithfulness of BNB explanations. Overall, Bernoulli perturbation is useful for some model families, but benchmark accuracy should be interpreted together with template proximity.

</details>


### [329] [ORION: An Outcome-Driven LLM Advisor for RL-Based Multi-Domain 6G Slice Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41563)
*Mahmoud S. Mayaleh,Fetia Bannour,Nour-El-Houda Yellas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ORION分层编排器，用LLM智能体做意图解析与计划生成、协作多智能体RL做跨域划分与域内放置，并引入结果驱动的情景记忆将已验证可行/失败的计划反馈给规划器，在受控评估下使接纳率提升4.6–11.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络切片跨管理域部署需在线联合决定VNF域归属、跨域流量路径与切片是否接纳，该联合问题是NP-hard；实验表明不存在在所有拓扑族上都占优的固定规划策略，且多数被拒请求实际存在可行放置却未被发现，说明"计划层"才是决定性性能杠杆。

Method: ORION分层编排：两个LLM智能体将服务意图转为结构化请求并生成抽象放置计划；协作式多智能体RL协调器选择跨域划分并将计划作为持久软先验，各域智能体负责功能放置。核心为计划层的结果驱动情景记忆，为每次尝试记录网络状况画像、所提计划与经验证的可行性结果，将已验证成功与带标签失败回灌规划器，以部署可行性而非标量奖励闭合自适应回路；并采用重要性保留策略替代FIFO存储。

Result: 在保持下游选择与执行机制固定的受控设置下，于留出拓扑族上评估规划器：情景记忆相较无记忆的同款规划器将接纳率提升4.6–11.5个百分点，且基于重要性的保留机制贡献了相对FIFO存储的大部分增益。

Conclusion: 计划层是跨域切片编排的关键性能瓶颈，以可行性结果驱动的情景记忆为规划器提供学习信号，可显著提升接纳率；策略无普适最优，需具备跨拓扑族的自适应能力。

Abstract: Deploying 6G network slices across administrative domains requires an online decision for each arriving request: which domain hosts each virtual network function, how traffic crosses domain boundaries, and whether the slice is admitted. This joint decision problem is NP-hard under continuous arrivals. Experiments on a parametrized family of multi-domain topologies show that no fixed planning strategy dominates across all regimes: the performance ranking reverses between topology classes, and feasible placements exist for most rejected requests but remain undiscovered. These findings identify the plan layer as the decisive performance lever. We introduce ORION, a hierarchical orchestrator in which two LLM agents translate service intents into structured requests and generate abstract placement plans. A cooperative multi-agent RL coordinator selects the cross-domain partition, treating the plan as a persistent soft prior, while per-domain agents place the functions. ORION main contribution lies at the plan layer: an outcome-driven episodic memory that records, for each completed attempt, the network-condition profile, the proposed plan, and its verified feasibility outcome. The memory feeds verified successes and labelled failures back to the planner, thereby closing the adaptation loop using deployment feasibility rather than a scalar reward assigned to the planner's own proposals. To isolate the effect of the plan quality, we evaluate the planner on held-out topology families while keeping the downstream selection and execution mechanisms fixed. Under this controlled setting, the proposed memory improves admission by $4.6$--$11.5$ percentage points over the same planner without memory, while importance-based retention accounts for most of the improvement over a first-in-first-out store.

</details>


### [330] [Task Vectors Can Be Linearly Decomposed into Interpretable and Controllable Components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41743)
*Yukiya Horiba,Hakaze Cho,Takahiro Yakoh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将任务向量（TV）从"原子化方向"重新理解为可分解的功能结构，提出基于候选标签unembedding将其分解为任务识别（TR）、任务学习（TL）与残差三部分，并用贝叶斯优化搜索TR/TL系数以改进干预效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务向量通常被视为从隐状态中提取的整体方向，其内部功能结构缺乏理解；受将上下文学习分解为Task Recognition与Task Learning的已有工作启发，作者希望探究单个TV是否也能被分解为功能上有意义的成分。

Method: 从候选标签的unembedding出发构建TR/TL分解，将每个TV拆分为TR分量、TL分量与残差分量；再用贝叶斯优化调节TR与TL的系数以获得更有效的干预方向。实验覆盖多个模型家族、多种分类任务，以及基于指令和基于示例的两类TV。

Result: TR/TL优化得到的干预方向优于原始TV；对随机方向的优化也能提升性能，但TR/TL搜索的增益更稳定，尤其在基于指令的TV上更明显；对分解成分的单独干预产生与各自功能角色一致的、可区分的logit层面效应。

Conclusion: 任务向量并非单一整体的引导方向，而是可分解为功能上有意义的成分，这些成分既能支持模型行为的解释，也能支持对模型的有效控制。

Abstract: Task Vectors (TVs) provide a compact way to steer large language models, but they are typically treated as atomized directions merely extracted from hidden states, leaving their internal functional structure poorly understood. Motivated by prior work that decomposes in-context learning into Task Recognition (TR) and Task Learning (TL), we ask whether a single TV can likewise be decomposed into functionally meaningful components. We formulate a TR/TL-based decomposition from candidate-label unembeddings, separating each TV into TR, TL, and residual components, and try to optimize the TR and TL coefficients using Bayesian optimization for more effective intervention. We evaluate this formulation across multiple model families, classification tasks, and both instruction- and demonstration-based TVs. Across these settings, TR/TL-based optimization identifies intervention directions that outperform the original TVs. Although optimization over random directions can also improve performance, TR/TL-based search yields more reliable gains, particularly for instruction-based TVs. Moreover, isolated interventions on the decomposed components produce distinct logit-level effects that are consistent with their intended functional roles. Together, these results suggest that TVs are not monolithic steering directions, but can be decomposed into functionally meaningful components that support both interpretation and control.

</details>


### [331] [A Constructive Orbit-Based Theory of Learnability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41641)
*Yunied Puig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出有限维轨道框架构造数据自适应字典、稀疏实现与预测扩展，以CCDS（偏差受控稀疏实现）为核心，把学习问题分解为骨架发现、稀疏实现与稀疏坐标预测三任务，并给出系列理论保证与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏字典学习缺乏理论可控性，MP/OMP等贪婪方法只能在特定残差几何下工作。作者希望通过有限维算子动力学（轨道字典）给出一个可分析、可泛化的统一框架，使字典构造、稀疏表示与预测具备明确的理论保证。

Method: 使用wrapped weighted shifts在独立轨道代表元上生成轨道字典与局部插值映射；提出CCDS——一种偏差受控的稀疏实现流程，在最小偏差边界退化为MP/OMP型行为，同时允许具有指定残差几何的可行内点路径；并证明轨道独立性、精确块插值与范数控制、稀疏逼近畸变的可观测界、选定支撑上的实现保证及固定路径稳定性。

Result: 证明了一般轨道独立性、块插值精确性与范数控制、稀疏逼近畸变的可观测上界、选定支撑上的稀疏实现保证，以及块插值在容许扰动下的固定路径稳定性。实验显示：所选轨道骨架在匹配秩投影行为上显著优于随机轨道或无结构替代方案；CCDS在最小偏差边界达到近零实现误差；由稀疏CCDS证书训练的预测器具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 该工作通过数据诱导轨道字典的构造与分析，将稀疏逼近、插值与有限维算子动力学联系起来，提供了可控的字典学习与预测路径；CCDS在理论与实验上均展现了相对于MP/OMP的灵活性优势和良好性能。

Abstract: We develop a finite-dimensional orbit framework for constructing data-adapted dictionaries, sparse realizations, and predictive extensions. The method uses wrapped weighted shifts to generate orbit dictionaries and local interpolation maps from independent orbit representatives, turning the learning problem into three explicit tasks: scaffold discovery, sparse realization on the discovered dictionary, and prediction through learned sparse coordinates. We introduce CCDS as the main constructive ingredient: a deviation-controlled sparse realization procedure that recovers MP/OMP-type behavior at the minimal-deviation boundary while allowing feasible interior paths with prescribed residual geometry that is inaccessible to MP/OMP. We prove generic orbit independence, exact block interpolation with norm control, observable bounds on sparse approximation distortion, sparse realization guarantees on selected supports, and fixed-path stability under admissible perturbations of the block interpolants. These results connect sparse approximation, interpolation, and finite-dimensional operator dynamics through the construction and analysis of data-induced orbit dictionaries. Experiments on synthetic geometry, controlled realization tests, and standard classification benchmarks show that selected orbit scaffolds have substantially better matched-rank projection behavior than random orbit or unstructured alternatives, that CCDS attains near-zero realization error at the minimal-deviation boundary, and that predictors trained from the sparse CCDS certificates give competitive performance.

</details>


### [332] [On the Generation Order in GFlowNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41648)
*Timofei Gritsaev,Evgeny Katasonov,Ildus Sadrtdinov,Esmeralda S. Whitammer,Dmitry Vetrov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究GFlowNet中生成顺序对收敛的影响，提出能无监督学习可解释生成顺序的Learning-Order GFlowNet训练方案，并用梯度信噪比解释顺序涌现机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GFlowNet按序逐次添加离散分量构造对象，顺序决定网络需学习的条件分布，不同顺序难度不同；但现有算法是否能发现并利用可解释的生成顺序尚不清楚。

Method: 构造生成顺序可实验控制的问题，证明顺序影响收敛且标准GFlowNet难以发现有用顺序；进而提出Learning-Order GFlowNet，在无顺序标注下学习可解释生成顺序；并分析梯度信噪比（SNR）作为顺序涌现的解释。

Result: 在受控问题上，生成顺序显著影响收敛，标准GFlowNet难以自发发现有 用顺序；Learning-Order GFlowNet学到可解释顺序并改善收敛；收敛更快的顺序在相同验证损失下具有更高SNR。

Conclusion: 生成顺序是GFlowNet训练的关键因素，可通过无标注学习获得；梯度SNR既提供了偏好优质顺序的机制解释，也构成对"好生成顺序"的刻画指标。

Abstract: Sampling from discrete distributions specified by unnormalised probability functions is a classical statistical problem. GFlowNets address this problem by constructing objects sequentially, adding one discrete component at a time in either a predefined or a learned order. The choice of order determines which conditional distributions the neural network must learn, some of which may be easier to learn than others. Yet whether existing GFlowNet algorithms discover interpretable generation orders and make use of them during training remains unexplored. We introduce controlled problems where generation order is experimentally shown to affect convergence and standard GFlowNet training struggles to discover useful orders. To address this difficulty, we develop Learning-Order GFlowNet, a training recipe that learns interpretable generation orders without order annotations and improves convergence on these problems. Finally, we investigate gradient signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) as a possible explanation for order emergence: orders associated with faster convergence exhibit higher SNR at comparable validation loss, suggesting both a mechanism favouring their emergence and an SNR-based characterisation of a good generation order.

</details>


### [333] [Less Loss, Fixed Weights: Logit Corrections for Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.42000)
*Hanyu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文考察"遗忘"（微调/继续预训练后标准网络文本困惑度上升）能否用对最后一层词表预测的简单固定调整（逐token偏置/温度）来逆转。在14对1B–7B模型上，发现固定偏置单独最多仅恢复<10%，偏置+温度最多36%，数学继续预训练的模型几乎无法恢复；随机破坏的对照模拟中同类修正却能恢复29%，说明真实遗忘大部分不在受限拟合的可及范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在指令微调或在新增学科语料上继续预训练后，对标准网络文本的预测能力下降（遗忘）。作者希望检验这一损失是否只是输出层预测的系统性偏移，从而可通过极简单的固定后处理（逐token词表偏置，可叠加温度）予以撤销；这关系到遗忘的本质是"可校正的校准偏移"还是"表征层面的实质损失"。

Method: 在14对基座/派生模型（1B–7B参数）上，拟合逐token的固定奖励或惩罚（vocabulary bias），单独使用或与温度参数联合；在未参与拟合的留出文本上评估恢复率（基于top-4K词表，条件于保留词表的loss）；并设计受控模拟：对模型进行随机破坏、不植入任何偏置或温度变化，检验同一修正的恢复能力。

Result: 留出文本上，最大top-4K偏置恢复估计低于10%（loss条件于保留词表）；偏置与温度结合最多恢复36%；在数学语料上继续预训练的模型损失最大且几乎无法恢复；在无植入偏置/温度变化的随机破坏模拟中，偏置+温度仍恢复29%。

Conclusion: 真实模型的loss上升大部分超出受限固定拟合的可及范围，说明遗忘并非主要源于输出层可直接校正的固定偏置或温度偏移；仅靠简单的词表偏置与温度调整不足以逆转遗忘，其机制更可能涉及表征或分布层面的实质性变化。

Abstract: When a language model is fine-tuned to follow instructions, or further pretrained on a new subject, it often gets worse at predicting standard web text. This is known as "forgetting." We ask whether this loss can be undone by simple, fixed adjustments to the model's final token predictions. Across 14 pairs of base and derived models, from 1B to 7B parameters, we fitted a fixed bonus or penalty for each token (a vocabulary bias), alone or together with a temperature. On text held out from correction fitting, the largest top-4K bias recovery estimate is under 10%, with loss conditional on the retained vocabulary. Combining the bias with a temperature recovers up to 36%. Models further pretrained on math lose the most and recover almost nothing. Finally, in a controlled simulation where a model is damaged at random, with no planted bias or temperature change, the bias-plus-temperature fix still recovers 29%. Most of the real-model loss increase stays out of reach of our restricted fits.

</details>


### [334] [From WSIs to Bulk, Spatial and Single-Cell Transcriptomics via Multi-Scale Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41692)
*Miguel Andrade,José Almeida,Carlos Santiago,Catarina Barata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多尺度图神经网络（GNN），以病理基础模型UNI2-h提取W影像形态特征，在patch与cell两个层级构建空间图，从常规WSI中同时预测bulk、空间与单细胞转录组，并用直接监督加弱监督一致性损失的混合训练策略实现跨尺度对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组（ST）与单细胞转录组（scT）分辨率高但成本高、样本稀缺；而标准WSI与大块RNA测序数据广泛可得。本文旨在利用易获取的WSI重建多层级基因表达，缓解转录组数据稀缺问题。

Method: 用UNI2-h提取形态特征；在patch级和cell级分别构建空间图，刻画组织层级关系；训练数据整合TCGA（bulk）、Visium（空间）与Xenium（单细胞）在肺、肾、乳腺组织上的样本；采用混合训练策略，结合直接监督与弱监督一致性损失，使不同生物尺度的预测相互对齐。

Result: slide级基因表达预测准确；patch级预测中等，其中Xenium上表现最强；单细胞级相关中等。下游任务显示预测可用于生存分析、空间风险图与基于分子签名的肿瘤分类。

Conclusion: 多尺度GNN可仅从WSI同时重建bulk、空间和单细胞转录组，并在临床相关任务中体现价值；但单细胞级预测精度仍有限，跨尺度一致性监督是性能关键。

Abstract: Understanding the spatial distribution of gene expression is key to studying complex diseases like cancer. While Spatial Transcriptomics (ST) and Single-Cell Transcriptomics (scT) provide high-resolution insights, they remain expensive and scarce. In contrast, standard histopathology Whole-Slide Images (WSIs) paired with bulk RNA sequencing are widely available. This work introduces a Multi-Scale Graph Neural Network (GNN) capable of simultaneously predicting bulk, spatial, and single-cell transcriptomics directly from WSIs. By extracting morphological features using a Pathology Foundation Model (PFM) (UNI2-h) and constructing spatial graphs at both patch and cell levels, the model learns the hierarchical relationships within the tissue. The network is trained on a combined dataset of The Cancer Genome Atlas Program (TCGA), Visium, and Xenium samples across lung, kidney, and breast tissues. A hybrid training strategy is employed, leveraging direct supervision alongside weakly supervised consistency losses to ensure predictions align across biological scales. Results show the model accurately predicts gene expression at the slide level and moderately at the patch level (strongest on Xenium), and achieves moderate correlation at the challenging single-cell level. Furthermore, downstream tasks explore the clinical relevance of these predictions, demonstrating their utility in survival analysis, spatial hazard mapping, and tumor classification via molecular signatures.

</details>


### [335] [From fleet trajectories to single-cell promises: hierarchically weighted conformal prediction for battery SOH monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41696)
*Xiangyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出保形预测（conformal prediction）报告的"90%覆盖率"在嵌套电池数据上仅代表车队平均而非单个电芯，提出三级报告协议（车队/批次/电芯）量化该差距，并用层级加权保形引擎部分修复，同时明确报告无法修复的结构性边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保形预测区间通常被解读为"90%覆盖率"，但在电池这类嵌套（电芯嵌套于批次）数据上，池化覆盖率掩盖了单电芯层面的严重失效：池化覆盖看似健康，而最差电芯几乎从不被覆盖。现有报告方式缺乏对个体级承诺的可审计性，作者希望揭示并量化这一"平均掩盖个体"的问题。

Method: 在124个公开LFP电芯上采用预注册的三级报告协议（车队、批次、电芯），用精确二项检验对名义水平做检验；提出层级加权保形引擎，权重仅使用预测时刻冻结的侧信息（签名特征、批次标签、循环索引），不触碰残差；在跨化学体系外部集与域内数据上进行敏感性分析与复现。

Result: 池化覆盖率在各划分下为0.875–0.906，而最差电芯覆盖率仅0.1–2%。修复后第10百分位电芯覆盖率由0.750升至0.830（弥合53%差距），最差电芯由0.642升至0.694，零延迟且敏感性谱平坦。协议漂移下冻结权重在SOH尺度上单调恶化（结构性死路，如实报告）；修复能力受校准池电池多样性限制；跨化学外部集区间崩塌至0.286，域内骨架可复现（池化0.933，最差电芯0.686）。

Conclusion: "90%覆盖率"的个体级承诺在嵌套电池数据上不可直接成立。本文交付物是一套可审计的报告协议——锚定实测机制的承诺三级、最差电芯统计量、延迟比例——使部署前的单电芯区间声称可被验证；同时明确区分可修复部分与结构性不可修复边界，而非通过调参掩盖。

Abstract: Interval predictions from conformal prediction are usually read as "90% coverage", but on nested battery data that number promises the fleet average, not the individual cell. We measure the gap on 124 public LFP cells under a preregistered three-tier reporting protocol (fleet, batch, cell): pooled coverage looks healthy (0.875-0.906 across splits) while the worst cell is covered 0.1-2% of the time, with exact binomial tests against the nominal level. We then repair part of the gap with a hierarchically weighted conformal engine whose weights consume only side information frozen at prediction time - signature features, batch labels, cycle indices - and never touch residuals. The preregistered repair criterion passes: 10th-percentile cell coverage moves 0.750 to 0.830 (53% of the gap), the worst cell 0.642 to 0.694, with zero deferrals and a flat sensitivity spectrum. What the engine does not repair is localized rather than left open: under protocol drift the frozen weights harm monotonically on the SOH scale (the absolute scale is reported in full), a structural dead end we report instead of tuning away; and repair capacity is bounded by calibration-pool battery diversity, measured on both datasets. On a cross-chemistry external set the calibrated intervals collapse (0.286), while the within-domain skeleton replicates (pooled 0.933, worst cell 0.686). The deliverable is a reporting protocol - three tiers of promise anchored to measured regimes, worst-cell statistics, deferral share - that makes per-cell interval claims auditable before deployment.

</details>


### [336] [Hierarchical Residuals: A Minimalist Decoupling of Residual Width from Compute Width](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41711)
*Ziteng Wang,Jianqiao Lu,Xinyu Ma,Jintao Zhang,Jun Zhu,Yiyuan Ma,Jianfei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HiRes（分层残差），将残差流宽度与每层计算宽度解耦：块内用标准d维PreNorm层，块间用更宽的D维PostNorm外层残差，并由学习到的读写投影在块边界桥接，无需专用kernel即可提升FLOPs效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的残差连接隐含地把沿深度传递的残差宽度与每层内部计算宽度绑定为同一维度，限制了二者独立调节，从而无法单独扩展残差状态容量。

Method: HiRes把层划分为块：块内保持标准d维PreNorm层与恒定计算宽度；块间引入更宽D维的PostNorm外层残差连接块；在块边界用可学习的read/write稠密投影在两个空间间转换；单个Transformer层结构不变，仅由稠密投影与归一化构成。

Result: 在不同模型规模与深度下稳定优于原始残差相加；拟合scaling law显示FLOPs效率提升约1.30倍；OLMoE-1B-7B 500B token预训练中训练损失降低0.0208、下游准确率提升1.14点；相较VWN、mHC、AttnRes等方法具竞争力且激活内存与I/O开销更低。

Conclusion: HiRes以极简改动将残差宽度暴露为实用的scaling变量，仅依赖标准算子、无需专门kernel或基础设施优化，为独立扩展残差状态提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Residual connections underpin Transformers, but they implicitly tie the residual width carried through depth to the compute width used inside each layer. We introduce Hierarchical Residuals (HiRes), a minimalist architectural change that decouples these dimensions, widening the residual state while keeping per-layer compute width fixed. HiRes groups layers into blocks: standard d-dimensional PreNorm layers operate inside each block, while a wider D-dimensional PostNorm outer residual connects blocks. Learned read/write projections bridge the two spaces at block boundaries, leaving individual Transformer layers unchanged. Across different model sizes and depths, HiRes consistently outperforms vanilla residual addition, with fitted scaling laws indicating a 1.30-fold improvement in FLOPs efficiency. On OLMoE-1B-7B pretraining for 500B tokens, HiRes achieves 0.0208 lower training loss and 1.14-point higher downstream accuracy. HiRes is also competitive with other residual expansion methods such as VWN, mHC and AttnRes with less activation memory and I/O costs. Built from only dense projections and normalizations, it requires neither specialized kernels nor infrastructure-level optimizations. HiRes thus exposes residual width as a practical scaling variable using only standard operators.

</details>


### [337] [Locality-Controlled Out-of-Distribution Guidance for Robust Regulatory DNA Sequence Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41736)
*Dabin Jeong,Marwane Bourdim,Mo Lotfollahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将调控DNA序列生成中的引导问题重新表述为"局部性"问题，通过学习不变基序掩码并用OOD阻尼仅作用于背景位置的LOCOGEN方法，在冻结扩散主干下实现性质-新颖性-真实性三目标兼顾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将可控序列生成视为性质条件生成，用单一奖励评估，可能掩盖reward hacking；将评估分解为性质、新颖性、真实性后发现一个经验上鲜有探索的区域（三者同时满足），但所有现有基线都无法达到。作者认为差距源于以往方法仅把引导看作"幅度"问题，而调控DNA的序列语法在空间上非均匀——基序位置敏感、背景区可更多探索。

Method: 将引导重构为局部性（locality）问题。LOCOGEN通过序列级不变性学习获得不变基序掩码以保护基序位置，仅对背景位置施加OOD阻尼；在采样阶段作用于冻结的扩散主干，无需重训主干。

Result: 在HepG2、K562和SK-N-SH三个细胞系上，LOCOGEN达到了此前未被探索的性质-新颖性-真实性评估空间区域。

Conclusion: 调控DNA的可控生成需从"引导幅度"转向"引导局部性"；通过掩码保护功能基序、仅在背景区引导，可在不重训模型的前提下同时满足性质、新颖性与真实性，突破现有基线局限。

Abstract: Designing regulatory DNA sequences for targeted gene-expression control is an important challenge. Recent approaches frame this task as property-conditioned generation, where diffusion models trained on natural sequences are guided toward high predicted activity through reward-based fine-tuning or sampling-time guidance. We show that single-reward evaluation can obscure reward-hacking behavior. By decomposing evaluation into property, novelty, and plausibility, we identify an empirically underexplored region of the evaluation space in which all three objectives are satisfied, but no existing baseline succeeds. We postulate that this gap arises because prior methods treat guidance primarily as a matter of magnitude. In regulatory DNA, however, sequence grammar is spatially nonuniform: motif positions are sensitive to perturbation, while background regions allow more exploration. We therefore propose LOCOGEN by reformulating guidance as a problem of locality. LOCOGEN learns an invariant motif mask through sequence-level invariance learning to protect motif positions, while applying OOD damping only to background positions. It operates at sampling time on a frozen diffusion backbone, without retraining the backbone. Across HepG2, K562, and SK-N-SH, LOCOGEN reaches this previously underexplored region of the property-novelty-plausibility space. Code is available at https://github.com/Lotfollahi-lab/LocoGen.

</details>


### [338] [Do LLMs Know When They Are Wrong About Molecules? In-Context Property Prediction with Self-Reported Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41745)
*Matthias Busch,Marius Tacke,Tamadur Albaraghtheh,Sviatlana V. Lamaka,Mikhail L. Zheludkevich,Roland C. Aydin,Christian Feiler,Christian J. Cyron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 评估7个前沿LLM在分子性质预测任务上的能力：在3个无污染数据集上，各LLM随上下文示例增多而提升，30个示例时最优模型超过所有经典基线；自报不确定性与误差呈弱相关（Spearman ρ≤0.46），与基线不确定度水平相当；在被记忆的ESOL数据集上无上下文增益，且对可回忆分子报告更低的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已在众多领域广泛应用，但其在分子性质预测上的能力仍鲜有探索；同时需区分模型的真实学习与训练数据记忆（污染）效应，因而需要系统性评估并配合数据污染筛查。

Method: 选取7个前沿LLM，通过上下文示例（in-context learning）预测分子性质，并要求模型自报不确定性；与在相同分子上训练的经典基线模型对比。使用4个数据集，并逐一筛查LLM训练数据污染：3个为干净数据集，ESOL被多数模型记忆，作为对照。另外考察推理投入（reasoning effort）与上下文规模的影响。

Result: 干净数据集上所有LLM均随上下文提升，30个示例时最优LLM超越全部基线；推理投入对准确率影响很小；自报不确定性对预测误差的排序能力较弱（Spearman相关最高0.46），与基线不确定度水平大致相当；结合不确定度与预测时，亲脂性数据集上小上下文规模下最优LLM最确定的半数样本最准确，BACE、抗病毒数据集以及大上下文下的亲脂性上最优LLM与最佳基线持平；污染数据集ESOL上无上下文规模增益，且模型对其可回忆的分子报告更低的不确定性，符合记忆行为的预期。

Conclusion: 前沿LLM在无数据污染的分子性质预测上具备与经典基线相当甚至更优的少样本能力，但其自报不确定性校准较弱；污染筛查是此类评估的必要环节，ESOL上的表现印证了记忆会掩盖真实泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are used in many domains, but molecular property prediction with them is still little explored. In this work we evaluate the capability of 7 frontier LLMs to predict molecular properties together with a self-reported uncertainty from in-context examples, and compare them with classical baselines trained on the same molecules. We use 4 datasets and screen each for contamination of the training data of the LLMs: three are clean, and the fourth, ESOL, is memorised by most of the models and serves as a comparison. On the clean datasets every LLM improves with the context, at 30 examples the most accurate one beats every baseline. The requested reasoning effort changes accuracy little. The self-reported uncertainty ranks the prediction errors weakly, with Spearman rank correlations of up to 0.46, about the level reached by the uncertainties of the baselines. Combining uncertainty and prediction, on lipophilicity the most certain half of the best LLM is the most accurate at small context sizes; on BACE and antiviral, and on lipophilicity at large context sizes, the best LLMs are on par with the best baseline. On the contaminated ESOL dataset the LLMs show no improvement with the context size, as expected for a memorised dataset, and their stated uncertainty is smaller on the molecules whose value they recall.

</details>


### [339] [Latent-Lens: Visual Perception in Small Language Models Through Latent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41751)
*Mo Shahdloo,Faegheh Sardari,Mathew Salvaris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Latent-Lens 轻量编解码器，在隐空间中桥接冻结 VLM 与冻结 SLM，仅训练约 1.6M 参数即可实现纯隐状态通信，显著优于文本通信基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化多智能体系统中，独立 VLM 与 SLM 通常以离散文本交换信息，形成对空间与细粒度细节的有损瓶颈；SLM 容量有限，对输入信号质量更敏感。已有工作仅在语言模型间进行隐状态通信，VLM 向独立预训练纯文本 SLM 传递视觉信息的问题尚未探索。

Method: Latent-Lens 由两部分组成：(1) domain encoder，将冻结 VLM 的隐状态投影到冻结 SLM 的嵌入空间；(2) attention decoder，形式化为 LoRA，使 SLM 学会关注这些视觉前缀。两个骨干模型均冻结，仅训练约 1.6M 参数（占合并模型规模的 0.2%），信息交换完全通过隐状态进行。

Result: 在 VQA 任务上，CLEVR 达 81.0±7.9%、GQA 达 65.4±5.4%，同 LoRA 预算下的文本通信基线分别为 58.3±5.7% 与 61.8±1.0%（相对提升 +30.2–69.8 个百分点）；图像描述 Flickr8k 较同基线提升 +16.5（冻结基线 +18.8）。并在 TinyLlama-1.1B 与 InternVL2-1B（不同架构、词表与隐藏维度）上完成验证。

Conclusion: 跨模型隐空间通信可在不微调骨干、参数开销极低的前提下有效替代文本瓶颈，并在异构架构间具备可迁移性。

Abstract: Bridging independent vision-language models (VLMs) and small language models (SLMs) in a modular multi-agent system typically forces them to exchange information as discrete text, which creates a lossy bottleneck for spatial and fine-grained details. This constraint is especially acute for SLMs, whose limited capacity makes them more reliant on the quality of the input signal. Recent latent-communication methods exchange states between language models. How a VLM can pass visual information to a separately pretrained text-only SLM though remains unexplored. We propose \textbf{Latent-Lens}, a lightweight codec that bridges the representation gap between a frozen VLM and a frozen SLM directly in latent space, enabling them to communicate purely through hidden states. The codec consists of a domain encoder that projects hidden states from the VLM into the SLM's embedding space, and an attention decoder formalised as a LoRA that teaches the SLM to attend to these visual prefixes, while training only ${\sim}1.6$M parameters (0.2\% of the combined model size). On visual question answering (CLEVR, GQA), Latent-Lens achieves $81.0\pm7.9$\% and $65.4\pm5.4$\%, compared with $58.3\pm5.7$\% and $61.8\pm1.0$\% for text communication with the same LoRA budget (+30.2--69.8pp relative to frozen text communication), while on image captioning (Flickr8k) it improves over the same baseline by +16.5 (+18.8 frozen). We also evaluate Latent-Lens with TinyLlama-1.1B and InternVL2-1B, which use different architectures, vocabularies, and hidden dimensions.

</details>


### [340] [Implicit Assumptions About Tasks in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41792)
*Kathrin Korte,Christian Metzner,Akshay Bedhotiya,Viet Anh Khoa Tran,Eleni Nisioti,Sebastian Risi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文通过区分"底层数据—划分—学习器"三个层级，指出持续学习测量中诸多量（相似性、变化率、呈现顺序）常被隐含地归属到错误层级；教师—学生实验表明，同一轨迹的不同划分粒度、顺序与相似性结构会导致不同测量结果，而现有基准往往未明确声明这些性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习通常将数据切分为任务序列，并用任务间相似性、数据变化率、呈现顺序等解释测量结果。这些因素可分别在底层数据、数据划分、学习器三个层级上定义，三者并不必然一致，但文献中常隐含地混用，导致报告的指标含义不清、比较不可靠。

Method: 作者首先将三个层级分离，逐一识别在每一层级上取值的具体性质；随后在受控的教师—学生（teacher–student）实验中进行操纵：对同一底层轨迹采用不同粒度的划分、改变呈现顺序、改变底层数据的相似性结构，观察测量结果的差异；并据此审查代表性基准，统计其中明确声明了哪些性质。

Result: 受控实验显示，仅改变划分粒度、呈现顺序或底层相似性结构，即可产生显著不同的测量结果。对代表性基准的审查表明，这些层级相关的性质大多未被显式说明。对于底层数据不可检视的情形（如基于未公开预训练语料的基础模型），作者指出底层数据的性质由模型的初始状态继承，其上施加的训练过程仍可被描述。

Conclusion: 持续学习的评测应显式声明所报告量所处的层级，避免跨层级混用；在数据不可见时，应以模型初始状态继承数据性质、以可描述的后续训练作为替代，从而使测量与解释具有可复现性和可比性。

Abstract: Continual learning is the study of how a model accumulates knowledge from data received over time, rather than all at once. In practice, this is typically achieved by dividing a dataset into tasks and presenting them sequentially. Explanations of the resulting measurements take into account factors such as the similarity between segments, the rate at which the data changes and the order of presentation. Each of these factors can be defined in terms of the underlying data, the partition imposed on it, or the learner that encounters it. These levels do not necessarily coincide, and it is usually left implicit which level a reported quantity refers to. We separate these and identify which properties acquire a value at each level. In controlled teacher--student experiments, we demonstrate that partitioning the same underlying trajectory at different levels of granularity, changing the order of presentation, and varying the similarity structure of the underlying data all result in different measured outcomes. We review representative benchmarks to determine which of these properties are stated. Where the underlying data cannot be inspected, as in foundation models built on undisclosed pre-training corpora, its properties are inherited by the model's initial state, and the training applied on top of it can be described.

</details>


### [341] [Accurate and Auditable ECG Classification via Visibility Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41795)
*Artur Xavier,Guilherme Henrique Gomes Evangelista,Aitazaz Khan,Antonio Luiz Pinho Ribeiro,Mohammed J. Zaki,Srinivasan Parthasarathy,Wagner Meira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LYNX-ECG将每个心跳表示为时间戳拐点的稀疏可见性图，在CODE-Test上macro F1达0.889并优于ECGFounder等五个基线，存储仅约14%，且节点可映射到ECG时刻以支持临床可解释性审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医生依据波形形状与时序解读ECG，但现有高精度深度分类器很少揭示哪些波形区域驱动预测，缺乏可检验的临床解释。

Method: 提出基于图神经网络的LYNX-ECG，将每个心跳构建为由带时间戳拐点组成的稀疏可见性图，节点对应具体ECG时刻；通过删除临床定义的波形区域和改变心搏时序进行预测审计。

Result: 在CODE-Test上macro F1为0.889，超过包括ECGFounder基础模型在内的五个基线，图存储约为逐样本建图的14%。传导障碍中，临床预期区域在90%以上正确预测中为最强证据；心动过缓/心动过速判断随心率变化而保持传导间期固定；去除逐搏变异后房颤判断消失。

Conclusion: 稀疏可见性图在实现高精度ECG分类的同时提供临床依据充分、可检验的解释，兼顾性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Physicians read electrocardiograms (ECGs) from waveform shape and timing, yet accurate deep-learning classifiers rarely show which waveform regions drive their predictions. We introduce LYNX-ECG, a graph neural network that represents each heartbeat as a sparse visibility graph of timestamped turning points. On CODE-Test, LYNX-ECG reaches a macro F1 score of 0.889, outperforming five baselines including the ECGFounder foundation model, while its graphs need about 14% of the storage of graphs built from every sample. Because each node maps to a specific ECG time, we can delete clinically defined waveform regions and alter beat timing to audit predictions. For conduction disorders, the clinically expected region was the strongest evidence in more than 90% of correct predictions. Bradycardia and tachycardia calls follow heart rate with conduction intervals held fixed, and atrial fibrillation calls vanish when beat-to-beat variation is removed. Sparse visibility graphs thus pair accurate ECG classification with clinically grounded, testable explanations.

</details>


### [342] [On-device Detection of Wearer-induced NLOS on Chest-worn UWB Tags: Machine Learning, Link Indicators, or Both?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41799)
*Markus Gallacher,Vu Anh Minh Le,Matteo Trobinger,Davide Vecchia,Carlo Alberto Boano,Gian Pietro Picco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究穿戴式UWB标签中人体引起的非视距(WNLOS)问题，系统对比21种分类器（现有ML、链路指标LI及混合方法），并推出按用户约束推荐最优分类器的工具与公开数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UWB高精度测距在非视距条件下性能下降，而人体穿戴造成的WNLOS误差显著却研究不足；且检测须在标签端设备上完成，需在精度与严格运行时/能耗约束间权衡，主流ML方法难以满足，LI方法则鲜有探索。

Method: 聚焦胸戴标签，构建覆盖多信道、PHY配置、环境、受试者与朝向的150万样本数据集；评估现有on-device ML与LI分类器，并提出利用二者互补性的新型混合分类器；共分析21种分类器，并设计约束驱动的推荐工具。

Result: 所有分类器均取得良好分类精度，但相对性能（含误分类导致的测距误差）强烈依赖信道与PHY配置；运行时与能效差异巨大，部分方法精度提升有限却能耗高达39.6倍。

Conclusion: WNLOS检测不存在通用最优方案，须结合信道/PHY配置与资源约束选择分类器；作者公开推荐工具与数据集以支撑实际部署决策。

Abstract: Ultra-wideband (UWB) enables high-accuracy distance estimation (ranging), but performance degrades in non-line-of-sight (NLOS) conditions. When UWB tags are worn, the human body inevitably causes a wearer-induced NLOS (WNLOS), yielding significant errors; still, despite its relevance, WNLOS remains understudied. Moreover, WNLOS detection typically must occur on-device at the tag, requiring classification accuracy to be balanced against tight runtime and energy constraints often hard to meet with machine learning (ML) -- the dominating NLOS classification approach. Meanwhile, UWB radio link indicators (LI) offer a faster and lightweight alternative that has rarely been explored. We study existing on-device ML and LI classifiers when applied to WNLOS, and propose novel hybrid ones exploiting their complementarity. We focus on chest-worn tags and analyze 21 classifiers on a dataset of 1.5 million samples across channels, PHY settings, environments, subjects, and orientations. Results show that while all of them achieve good classification accuracy, relative performance, including ranging errors due to misclassification, depends heavily on channel and PHY configuration. Runtime and energy efficiency also vary dramatically; some techniques offer marginal accuracy gains while consuming up to 39.6x more energy. Therefore, we also introduce a recommender tool to identify optimal classifiers under user-defined constraints, publicly released with our dataset.

</details>


### [343] [Towards Out-of-Distribution Uncertainty Quantification for Machine-Learned Interatomic Potentials via Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41801)
*Stefan P. Schmid,Tobias Setzer,Maximilian Scheurer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出借鉴基础MLIPs的思路，在大型多样材料数据集上预训练不确定性估计器，以实现跨数据集的零样本与微调不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIPs实际部署需要可靠的不确定性量化（UQ），但现有事后不确定性估计方法多在低数据条件下训练，适用范围局限于狭窄领域，难以覆盖广泛化学空间。

Method: 在大型材料科学数据集上预训练多种UQ模型，用于量化不同MLIPs的不确定性；并在分布内与域外（OOD）吸附能预测任务上评估其性能，比较零样本与微调两种使用方式。

Result: 预训练的不确定性估计器可在多个数据集上提供可行的零样本不确定性估计；微调后即使在最具挑战性的数据集上，预测不确定性与预测误差之间也持续保持正相关。

Conclusion: 研究为MLIPs的基础型UQ模型奠定基础，通过为用户提供模型可靠性与适用性的先验信息，促进MLIPs在实际场景中的部署。

Abstract: Informative uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for the practical deployment of machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs), as it provides insight into their reliability across chemical space. Although numerous post-hoc uncertainty estimation methods for MLIPs have recently been proposed, most have been trained in low-data regimes, restricting their applicability to narrow domains of interest. Inspired by the emergence of foundational MLIPs, we propose pre-training uncertainty estimators on large, diverse datasets to enable broader applicability. We pre-train several UQ models on large materials science datasets to quantify the uncertainty of different MLIPs, and evaluate their performance on both in-distribution and out-of-domain (OOD) adsorption-energy predictions. We find that pre-trained uncertainty estimators provide viable zero-shot uncertainty estimates across multiple datasets, while fine-tuning consistently yields positive correlations between predicted uncertainty and prediction error, even on the most challenging datasets. These results lay the groundwork for foundational UQ models for MLIPs, ultimately facilitating their deployment in practical settings by providing users with a priori information about model reliability and applicability.

</details>


### [344] [Backpropagation-free Deep Learning for Time-Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41805)
*Miha Kapš,Blaž Bertalanič,Carolina Fortuna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文首次在112个UCR单变量时间序列分类数据集上系统评测DFA、DRTP、NoProp三种免反向传播学习器（1D卷积架构），优化后平均F1为0.76–0.79，低于ROCKET(0.83)与InceptionTime(0.84)；并提出以DD分析框架刻画性能差距与数据集性质的关系及卷积特征学习质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反向传播带来update locking与weight-transport问题，DFA/DRTP/NoProp等免BP规则可缓解这些约束，但其评估几乎局限于2D图像基准，缺乏在时间序列分类上的系统证据。

Method: 将三种学习器适配到1D卷积的单变量TSC，每种方法给出一套超参数优化配置；在112个UCR数据集上评测；构建分析框架，将逐数据集相对InceptionTime的差距回归到数据集性质，并通过将flatten读出替换为全局平均池化、以与同架构BP对照的双重差分（DD）检验卷积特征学习。

Result: 优化后平均F1为0.76–0.79，低于ROCKET(0.83)与InceptionTime(0.84)。类内时间变异性是与差距最一致相关的数据集属性（偏Spearman ρ=0.28–0.40）。池化对DFA与DRTP的损害大于BP（DD分别为−0.025、−0.055），提示其卷积特征接近随机；NoProp对池化的容忍度与BP相当（DD=+0.013）。相对同架构BP，DRTP减少33%训练FLOPs且仅在序列长于约700步时训练更快，DFA无计算收益，NoProp峰值显存降低38%但训练时间增加48%。

Conclusion: 免BP学习器在单变量TSC上可训练但整体精度仍不及ROCKET/InceptionTime；除NoProp外，DFA/DRTP学习的卷积特征质量不足；计算收益具有条件性（DRTP受序列长度制约、NoProp以时间换显存），NoProp在特征可学习性与资源折衷上最接近BP。

Abstract: Deep neural networks for time series classification (TSC) are trained almost exclusively by error backpropagation, which imposes update locking and the weight-transport problem. Backpropagation-free learning rules such as Direct Feedback Alignment (DFA), Direct Random Target Projection (DRTP), and the diffusion-inspired NoProp relax these constraints but have been evaluated almost exclusively on 2D image benchmarks. We adapt these three learners to univariate TSC with 1D convolutional architectures and present the first systematic study of them across 112 datasets from the UCR time series archive, with one hyperparameter-optimized configuration per method. The optimized learners reach mean F1 scores of 0.76 to 0.79, below ROCKET (0.83) and InceptionTime (0.84). We propose an analysis framework that relates the per-dataset gap to InceptionTime to dataset properties and tests convolutional feature learning by replacing the flatten readout with global average pooling, measured as a difference-in-differences (DD) against a backpropagation control on the identical architecture. Intra-class temporal variability is the dataset property most consistently associated with the gap for all three learners (partial Spearman $ρ$ from $0.28$ to $0.40$). Pooling harms DFA and DRTP more than backpropagation (DD $-0.025$ and $-0.055$), consistent with near-random convolutional features, whereas NoProp tolerates pooling as backpropagation does (DD $+0.013$). Relative to backpropagation on the same architectures, DRTP reduces training floating-point operations (FLOPs) by 33% and trains faster only on series longer than about 700 time steps, DFA yields no computational reduction, and NoProp reduces peak memory by 38% at the cost of 48% longer training.

</details>


### [345] [Exploring Bad Minima in Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.41807)
*Mina Remeli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过三类对抗攻击构造反例，检验"源任务泛化好是否必然带来可迁移性"，实证结果未找到反例，反而显示源任务泛化与可迁移性呈强相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移学习成功但所需条件不明，作者希望厘清可迁移性的必要条件，特别检验"源任务的良好泛化是否必然蕴含向相关目标任务的可迁移性"这一假设（作者假设为否）。
